## Description

[Nest](https://github.com/nestjs/nest) framework TypeScript starter repository.

## Project setup

```bash
$ yarn install
```

## Compile and run the project

```bash
# development
$ yarn run start

# watch mode
$ yarn run start:dev

# production mode
$ yarn run start:prod
```

## Run tests

```bash
# unit tests
$ yarn run test

# e2e tests
$ yarn run test:e2e

# test coverage
$ yarn run test:cov
```

## Deployment

When you're ready to deploy your NestJS application to production, there are some key steps you can take to ensure it runs as efficiently as possible. Check out the [deployment documentation](https://docs.nestjs.com/deployment) for more information.

If you are looking for a cloud-based platform to deploy your NestJS application, check out [Mau](https://mau.nestjs.com), our official platform for deploying NestJS applications on AWS. Mau makes deployment straightforward and fast, requiring just a few simple steps:

```bash
$ yarn install -g @nestjs/mau
$ mau deploy
```

With Mau, you can deploy your application in just a few clicks, allowing you to focus on building features rather than managing infrastructure.

## Resources

Check out a few resources that may come in handy when working with NestJS:

- Visit the [NestJS Documentation](https://docs.nestjs.com) to learn more about the framework.
- For questions and support, please visit our [Discord channel](https://discord.gg/G7Qnnhy).
- To dive deeper and get more hands-on experience, check out our official video [courses](https://courses.nestjs.com/).
- Deploy your application to AWS with the help of [NestJS Mau](https://mau.nestjs.com) in just a few clicks.
- Visualize your application graph and interact with the NestJS application in real-time using [NestJS Devtools](https://devtools.nestjs.com).
- Need help with your project (part-time to full-time)? Check out our official [enterprise support](https://enterprise.nestjs.com).
- To stay in the loop and get updates, follow us on [X](https://x.com/nestframework) and [LinkedIn](https://linkedin.com/company/nestjs).
- Looking for a job, or have a job to offer? Check out our official [Jobs board](https://jobs.nestjs.com).

    "/auth/mfa/verify": {
      "post": {
        "description": "After receiving mfaRequired=true from login, use this endpoint to verify the TOTP code",
        "operationId": "AuthController_verifyMfa",
        "parameters": [
          {
            "name": "user-agent",
            "required": true,

              "type": "string"

                    "type": "string",

                  "code": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "6-digit TOTP code from authenticator app",
                    "pattern": "^\\d{6}$",
                    "example": "123456"
                  }
                },
                "required": [
                  "tempToken",
                  "code"
                ]
              }
            }
          }

                      "type": "string"
                    },
                    "refreshToken": {
                      "type": "string"
                    },
                    "message": {
                      "type": "string"
                    }
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          },
          "401": {
            "description": "Invalid MFA code or expired token"
          }
        },
        "summary": "Verify MFA code and complete login",
        "tags": [
          "Authentication"
        ]
      }

================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env node

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

/**
 * Code Consolidator Script
 * Recursively scans directories and consolidates all code files into a single text file
 */

class CodeConsolidator {
  constructor() {
    // Common code file extensions
    this.codeExtensions = new Set([
      '.js',
      '.jsx',
      '.ts',
      '.tsx',
      '.py',
      '.java',
      '.c',
      '.cpp',
      '.cc',
      '.cxx',
      '.cs',
      '.php',
      '.rb',
      '.go',
      '.rs',
      '.swift',
      '.kt',
      '.scala',
      '.r',
      '.m',
      '.mm',
      '.html',
      '.htm',
      '.css',
      '.scss',
      '.sass',
      '.less',
      '.xml',
      '.json',
      '.yaml',
      '.yml',
      '.toml',
      '.sh',
      '.bash',
      '.zsh',
      '.fish',
      '.ps1',
      '.bat',
      '.sql',
      '.graphql',
      '.proto',
      '.thrift',
      '.dockerfile',
      '.makefile',
      '.cmake',
      '.vue',
      '.svelte',
      '.dart',
      '.lua',
      '.pl',
      '.pm',
    ]);

    // Files to exclude
    this.excludeFiles = new Set([
      'package-lock.json',
      'yarn.lock',
      '.DS_Store',
      'Thumbs.db',
      '.env',
      '.env.local',
      '.env.production',
      '.env.development',
    ]);

    // Directories to exclude
    this.excludeDirs = new Set([
      'node_modules',
      '.git',
      '.svn',
      '.hg',
      'dist',
      'build',
      'target',
      'bin',
      'obj',
      '__pycache__',
      '.pytest_cache',
      'venv',
      'env',
      '.venv',
      '.env',
      'vendor',
      '.idea',
      '.vscode',
      '.vs',
      'coverage',
      '.nyc_output',
      '.cache',
    ]);

    this.stats = {
      filesProcessed: 0,
      totalLines: 0,
      skippedFiles: 0,
      errors: 0,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Check if file should be processed based on extension
   */
  isCodeFile(filePath) {
    const ext = path.extname(filePath).toLowerCase();
    const basename = path.basename(filePath).toLowerCase();

    // Special files without extensions
    const specialFiles = [
      'dockerfile',
      'makefile',
      'jenkinsfile',
      'readme',
      'license',
      'changelog',
    ];
    if (specialFiles.some((name) => basename.includes(name))) {
      return true;
    }

    return this.codeExtensions.has(ext);
  }

  /**
   * Check if file/directory should be excluded
   */
  shouldExclude(itemPath, isDirectory = false) {
    const basename = path.basename(itemPath);

    if (isDirectory) {
      return this.excludeDirs.has(basename) || basename.startsWith('.');
    }

    return (
      this.excludeFiles.has(basename) ||
      (basename.startsWith('.') && !this.isCodeFile(itemPath))
    );
  }

  /**
   * Get file size in a readable format
   */
  getFileSize(filePath) {
    try {
      const stats = fs.statSync(filePath);
      const bytes = stats.size;

      if (bytes === 0) return '0 B';
      const k = 1024;
      const sizes = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB'];
      const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
      return parseFloat((bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(2)) + ' ' + sizes[i];
    } catch (error) {
      return 'Unknown';
    }
  }

  /**
   * Process a single file and add its content to the output
   */
  processFile(filePath, outputStream) {
    try {
      const relativePath = path.relative(process.cwd(), filePath);
      const fileSize = this.getFileSize(filePath);
      const content = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf-8');
      const lineCount = content.split('\n').length;

      // Write file header
      outputStream.write('\n' + '='.repeat(80) + '\n');
      outputStream.write(`FILE: ${relativePath}\n`);
      outputStream.write(`SIZE: ${fileSize} | LINES: ${lineCount}\n`);
      outputStream.write('='.repeat(80) + '\n\n');

      // Write file content
      outputStream.write(content);
      outputStream.write('\n\n');

      this.stats.filesProcessed++;
      this.stats.totalLines += lineCount;

      console.log(`✓ Processed: ${relativePath} (${lineCount} lines)`);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`✗ Error processing ${filePath}: ${error.message}`);
      this.stats.errors++;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Recursively scan directory and process all code files
   */
  scanDirectory(dirPath, outputStream) {
    try {
      const items = fs.readdirSync(dirPath);

      for (const item of items) {
        const itemPath = path.join(dirPath, item);
        const stats = fs.statSync(itemPath);

        if (stats.isDirectory()) {
          if (!this.shouldExclude(itemPath, true)) {
            this.scanDirectory(itemPath, outputStream);
          } else {
            console.log(
              `⏭ Skipping directory: ${path.relative(process.cwd(), itemPath)}`,
            );
          }
        } else if (stats.isFile()) {
          if (
            this.isCodeFile(itemPath) &&
            !this.shouldExclude(itemPath, false)
          ) {
            this.processFile(itemPath, outputStream);
          } else {
            this.stats.skippedFiles++;
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`✗ Error scanning directory ${dirPath}: ${error.message}`);
      this.stats.errors++;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Main consolidation function
   */
  consolidate(inputPath = '.', outputPath = 'consolidated-code.txt') {
    console.log('🚀 Starting code consolidation...\n');
    console.log(`Input: ${path.resolve(inputPath)}`);
    console.log(`Output: ${path.resolve(outputPath)}\n`);

    const outputStream = fs.createWriteStream(outputPath);

    // Write header
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
    outputStream.write(`CODE CONSOLIDATION REPORT\n`);
    outputStream.write(`Generated: ${timestamp}\n`);
    outputStream.write(`Input Directory: ${path.resolve(inputPath)}\n`);
    outputStream.write(`${'='.repeat(80)}\n\n`);

    try {
      const inputStats = fs.statSync(inputPath);

      if (inputStats.isDirectory()) {
        this.scanDirectory(inputPath, outputStream);
      } else if (inputStats.isFile()) {
        this.processFile(inputPath, outputStream);
      } else {
        throw new Error('Input path is neither a file nor a directory');
      }

      // Write footer with statistics
      outputStream.write('\n' + '='.repeat(80) + '\n');
      outputStream.write('CONSOLIDATION SUMMARY\n');
      outputStream.write('='.repeat(80) + '\n');
      outputStream.write(`Files Processed: ${this.stats.filesProcessed}\n`);
      outputStream.write(
        `Total Lines: ${this.stats.totalLines.toLocaleString()}\n`,
      );
      outputStream.write(`Files Skipped: ${this.stats.skippedFiles}\n`);
      outputStream.write(`Errors: ${this.stats.errors}\n`);
      outputStream.write(`Generated: ${timestamp}\n`);

      outputStream.end();

      console.log('\n✅ Consolidation completed successfully!');
      console.log(`📊 Statistics:`);
      console.log(`   • Files processed: ${this.stats.filesProcessed}`);
      console.log(
        `   • Total lines: ${this.stats.totalLines.toLocaleString()}`,
      );
      console.log(`   • Files skipped: ${this.stats.skippedFiles}`);
      console.log(`   • Errors: ${this.stats.errors}`);
      console.log(`📁 Output saved to: ${path.resolve(outputPath)}`);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`💥 Fatal error: ${error.message}`);
      outputStream.destroy();
    }
  }

  /**
   * Add custom file extensions
   */
  addExtensions(extensions) {
    extensions.forEach((ext) => {
      if (!ext.startsWith('.')) ext = '.' + ext;
      this.codeExtensions.add(ext.toLowerCase());
    });
  }

  /**
   * Add custom exclude patterns
   */
  addExcludes(files = [], dirs = []) {
    files.forEach((file) => this.excludeFiles.add(file));
    dirs.forEach((dir) => this.excludeDirs.add(dir));
  }
}

// CLI Usage
if (require.main === module) {
  const args = process.argv.slice(2);
  const consolidator = new CodeConsolidator();

  // Parse command line arguments
  let inputPath = '.';
  let outputPath = 'consolidated-code.txt';
  let customExtensions = [];
  let excludeFiles = [];
  let excludeDirs = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
    switch (args[i]) {
      case '--input':
      case '-i':
        inputPath = args[++i];
        break;
      case '--output':
      case '-o':
        outputPath = args[++i];
        break;
      case '--extensions':
      case '-e':
        customExtensions = args[++i].split(',');
        break;
      case '--exclude-files':
        excludeFiles = args[++i].split(',');
        break;
      case '--exclude-dirs':
        excludeDirs = args[++i].split(',');
        break;
      case '--help':
      case '-h':
        console.log(`
Code Consolidator - Merge all code files into one text file

Usage: node consolidate.js [options]

Options:
  -i, --input <path>          Input directory or file (default: current directory)
  -o, --output <path>         Output file path (default: consolidated-code.txt)
  -e, --extensions <list>     Additional file extensions (comma-separated)
  --exclude-files <list>      Additional files to exclude (comma-separated)
  --exclude-dirs <list>       Additional directories to exclude (comma-separated)
  -h, --help                  Show this help

Examples:
  node consolidate.js
  node consolidate.js -i ./src -o output.txt
  node consolidate.js -e "md,txt" --exclude-dirs "temp,cache"
                `);
        process.exit(0);
    }
  }

  // Apply custom settings
  if (customExtensions.length) consolidator.addExtensions(customExtensions);
  if (excludeFiles.length || excludeDirs.length)
    consolidator.addExcludes(excludeFiles, excludeDirs);

  // Run consolidation
  consolidator.consolidate(inputPath, outputPath);
}

module.exports = CodeConsolidator;

export enum CacheTTL {
  tenMinutes = 60 * 10,

  twoHours = 60 * 60 * 2,
  oneDay = 60 * 60 * 24,

SIZE: 1.44 KB | LINES: 59

export function PaginationMetadata(): MethodDecorator {
  return function (
    target: any,
    propertyKey: string | symbol,
    descriptor: PropertyDescriptor,
  ) {
    const originalMethod = descriptor.value;

    descriptor.value = async function (...args: any[]) {
      const { page = '1', size: pageSize = '10' } = args[0];

      const result = await originalMethod.apply(this, args);

      const startIndex = (page - 1) * pageSize;
      const endIndex = startIndex + pageSize;

      const paginatedResults = result.data.slice(startIndex, endIndex);

      const metadata = {
        page,
        pageSize,
        totalPages: Math.ceil(result.data.length / pageSize),
        totalRecords: result.data.length,
      };

      return { metadata, results: paginatedResults };
    };

    return descriptor;
  };
}

export function PaginationMetadataUsers(): MethodDecorator {
  return function (
    target: any,
    propertyKey: string | symbol,
    descriptor: PropertyDescriptor,
  ) {
    const originalMethod = descriptor.value;

    descriptor.value = async function (...args: any[]) {
      const { page = '1', size: pageSize = '10' } = args[0];

      const result = await originalMethod.apply(this, args);

      const metadata = {
        page,
        pageSize,
        totalPages: Math.ceil(result.data.length / pageSize),
        totalRecords: result.data.length,
      };

      return { metadata, result };
    };

    return descriptor;
  };
}

import { IsOptional, IsInt, Min } from 'class-validator';
import { Type } from 'class-transformer';
import { ApiProperty } from '@nestjs/swagger';

export class PaginationQueryDto {
  @IsOptional()
  @IsInt()
  @Type(() => Number)
  @Min(1)
  @ApiProperty({ default: 1, description: 'Page number' })
  page?: number = 1;

  @IsOptional()
  @IsInt()
  @Type(() => Number)
  @Min(1)
  @ApiProperty({ default: 10, description: 'Number of items per page' })
  limit?: number = 10;

  @IsOptional()
  @ApiProperty({ description: 'Search query' })
  q?: string;
}

FILE: src/common/filters/http-exception.filter.ts
SIZE: 1.13 KB | LINES: 50
================================================================================

import {
  ExceptionFilter,
  Catch,
  ArgumentsHost,
  HttpException,
  HttpStatus,
} from '@nestjs/common';

@Catch(HttpException)
export class HttpExceptionFilter implements ExceptionFilter {
  catch(exception: HttpException, host: ArgumentsHost) {
    const ctx = host.switchToHttp();
    const response = ctx.getResponse();
    const status = exception.getStatus();
    const res = exception.getResponse() as
      | string
      | { message?: string | string[]; [key: string]: any };

    const message =
      typeof res === 'string'
        ? res
        : Array.isArray(res.message)
        ? res.message.join(', ')
        : res.message || 'Unexpected error';

    let code = 'INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR';
    switch (status) {
      case HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST:
        code = 'BAD_REQUEST';
        break;
      case HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED:
        code = 'UNAUTHORIZED';
        break;
      case HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN:
        code = 'FORBIDDEN';
        break;
      case HttpStatus.CONFLICT:
        code = 'CONFLICT';
        break;
    }

    response.status(status).json({
      error: {
        code,
        message,
      },
    });
  }
}

FILE: src/common/guards/fortknox-protected-route.guard.ts
SIZE: 2.31 KB | LINES: 78

// apps/auth-service/src/common/guards/fortknox-protected.guard.ts

import { Injectable, CanActivate, ExecutionContext } from "@nestjs/common";
import { Reflector } from "@nestjs/core";
import { TenantBindingGuard } from "./tenant-binding.guard";
import { JwtAuthGuard } from "@/common/guards/jwt-auth-guard";
import { PermissionsGuard } from "@/modules/rbac/guards/permissions.guard";
import { SessionGuard } from "@/common/guards/session.guard";

/**
 * FortKnoxProtectedGuard
 *
 * Composite guard that enforces complete auth boundary:
 * 1. JWT validation (token is valid)
 * 2. Tenant binding (token tenant == request tenant)
 * 3. Session validity (session exists, not revoked, not expired)
 * 4. Permissions (optional, only if @RequirePermissions decorator present)
 *
 * This is the "one guard to rule them all" approach.
 *
 * Usage:
 * @UseGuards(FortKnoxProtectedGuard)
 * @RequirePermissions('users:read') // Optional
 * async myProtectedRoute() { ... }
 *
 * Benefits:
 * - Single decorator
 * - Guaranteed correct order
 * - Easier to maintain
 * - Less error-prone
 */
@Injectable()
export class FortKnoxProtectedGuard implements CanActivate {
  constructor(
    private readonly reflector: Reflector,
    private readonly jwtAuthGuard: JwtAuthGuard,
    private readonly tenantBindingGuard: TenantBindingGuard,
    private readonly sessionGuard: SessionGuard,
    private readonly permissionsGuard: PermissionsGuard,
  ) {}

  async canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): Promise<boolean> {
    // Step 1: JWT Authentication
    const jwtResult = await this.jwtAuthGuard.canActivate(context);
    if (!jwtResult) {
      return false;
    }

    // Step 2: Tenant Binding
    const tenantResult = this.tenantBindingGuard.canActivate(context);
    if (!tenantResult) {
      return false;
    }

    // Step 3: Session Validation
    const sessionResult = await this.sessionGuard.canActivate(context);
    if (!sessionResult) {
      return false;
    }

    // Step 4: Permissions (only if decorator present)
    const permissions = this.reflector.get<string[]>(
      "permissions",
      context.getHandler(),
    );

    if (permissions && permissions.length > 0) {
      const permissionsResult =
        await this.permissionsGuard.canActivate(context);
      if (!permissionsResult) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    return true;
  }
}

FILE: src/common/guards/guards.module.ts

import { Module, Global } from "@nestjs/common";
import { TypeOrmModule } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import {
  Permission,
  Role,
  RolePermission,
  Session,
  User,
  UserRole,
} from "@repo/database";
import { JwtAuthGuard } from "./jwt-auth-guard";
import { TenantBindingGuard } from "./tenant-binding.guard";
import { SessionGuard } from "./session.guard";
import { PermissionsGuard } from "@/modules/rbac/guards/permissions.guard";
import { RolesGuard } from "@/modules/rbac/guards/roles.guard";
import { FortKnoxProtectedGuard } from "@/common/guards/fortknox-protected-route.guard";
import { RbacService } from "@/modules/rbac/services/rbac.service";
import { RoleService } from "@/modules/rbac/services/role.service";
import { PermissionService } from "@/modules/rbac/services/permission.service";

/**
 * GuardsModule
 *
 * Centralizes all authentication and authorization guards.
 * Marked as @Global so guards are available everywhere without importing.
 *
 * Guards included:
 * - JwtAuthGuard: JWT token validation
 * - TenantBindingGuard: Tenant isolation enforcement
 * - SessionGuard: Session validity checks
 * - PermissionsGuard: RBAC permission checks
 * - RolesGuard: RBAC role checks
 * - FortKnoxProtectedGuard: Composite guard (all of the above)
 */
@Global()
@Module({
  imports: [
    TypeOrmModule.forFeature([
      Session,
      User,
      UserRole,
      Role,
      RolePermission,
      Permission,
    ]),
  ],
  providers: [
    JwtAuthGuard,
    TenantBindingGuard,
    SessionGuard,
    PermissionsGuard,
    RolesGuard,
    FortKnoxProtectedGuard,
    RbacService,
    RoleService,
    PermissionService,
    RolePermission,
  ],
  exports: [
    JwtAuthGuard,
    TenantBindingGuard,
    SessionGuard,
    PermissionsGuard,
    RolesGuard,
    FortKnoxProtectedGuard,
    RbacService,
    RoleService,
    PermissionService,
    RolePermission,
  ],
})
export class GuardsModule {}

FILE: src/common/guards/jwt-auth-guard.ts
SIZE: 911 B | LINES: 38
================================================================================

import {
  Injectable,
  ExecutionContext,
  UnauthorizedException,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { AuthGuard } from "@nestjs/passport";
import { Observable } from "rxjs";

/**
 * JWT Authentication Guard
 *
 * Protects routes by validating JWT tokens
 */
@Injectable()
export class JwtAuthGuard extends AuthGuard("jwt") {
  handleRequest(err: any, user: any, info: any, context: ExecutionContext) {
    if (err || !user) {
      throw err || new UnauthorizedException("Invalid or expired token");
    }

    const req = context.switchToHttp().getRequest();

    // 1️⃣ Tenant binding
    const tokenTenant = user.tenantId;
    const requestTenant = req.tenant?.id;

    if (!tokenTenant || !requestTenant) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Tenant missing");
    }

    if (tokenTenant !== requestTenant) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Cross-tenant token");
    }

    return user;
  }
}

FILE: src/common/guards/jwt.strategy.ts
SIZE: 1.64 KB | LINES: 65
================================================================================

import { Injectable, UnauthorizedException } from "@nestjs/common";
import { PassportStrategy } from "@nestjs/passport";
import { ExtractJwt, Strategy } from "passport-jwt";
import { ConfigService } from "@nestjs/config";
import { SessionService } from "@/common/service/session.service";

/**
 * JWT Strategy
 *
 * Validates JWT tokens and extracts user payload
 */
@Injectable()
export class JwtStrategy extends PassportStrategy(Strategy) {
  constructor(
    private configService: ConfigService,
    private sessionService: SessionService,
  ) {
    super({
      jwtFromRequest: ExtractJwt.fromAuthHeaderAsBearerToken(),
      ignoreExpiration: false,
      secretOrKey: configService.getOrThrow("JWT_SECRET"),
    });
  }

  async validate(payload: any) {
    // if (!payload.sub) {
    //   throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid token payload");
    // }
    //
    // return {
    //   sub: payload.sub,
    //   email: payload.email,
    //   tenantId: payload.tenantId,
    // };

    const session = await this.sessionService.validateSession(
      payload.tenantId,
      payload.sessionId,
    );

    if (!session) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Session invalid or expired");
    }

    // Verify tenant matches
    if (session.tenantId !== payload.tenantId) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Tenant mismatch");
    }

    // Verify user matches
    if (session.userId !== payload.sub) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("User mismatch");
    }

    // Return user context
    return {
      sub: payload.sub,
      userId: session.userId,
      email: session.email,
      tenantId: session.tenantId,
      sessionId: session.id,
    };
  }
}



================================================================================
FILE: src/common/guards/session.guard.ts
SIZE: 4.29 KB | LINES: 160
================================================================================

import {
  Injectable,
  CanActivate,
  ExecutionContext,
  UnauthorizedException,
  Logger,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { InjectRepository } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { Repository } from "typeorm";
import { Session } from "@repo/database";

/**
 * SessionGuard
 *
 * Validates that the session referenced in the JWT:
 * 1. Exists in the database
 * 2. Belongs to the same user
 * 3. Belongs to the same tenant
 * 4. Is not revoked
 * 5. Is not expired
 *
 * MUST run AFTER:
 * - JwtAuthGuard (validates JWT)
 * - TenantBindingGuard (validates tenant match)
 *
 * This prevents:
 * - Using tokens after logout
 * - Using tokens after session revocation
 * - Using tokens after session expiry
 * - Token replay with old session IDs
 *
 * Usage:
 * @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, TenantBindingGuard, SessionGuard)
 */
@Injectable()
export class SessionGuard implements CanActivate {
  private readonly logger = new Logger(SessionGuard.name);

  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(Session)
    private readonly sessionRepository: Repository<Session>,
  ) {}

  async canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): Promise<boolean> {
    const request = context.switchToHttp().getRequest();

    // Step 1: Extract session ID from token payload
    const user = request.user;
    const sessionId = user?.sessionId;

    if (!sessionId) {
      this.logger.error("SessionGuard: No sessionId in token payload", {
        userId: user?.sub,
        email: user?.email,
      });
      throw new UnauthorizedException(
        "Invalid token: missing session reference",
      );
    }

    // Step 2: Fetch session from database
    const session = await this.sessionRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: sessionId },
      select: {
        id: true,
        userId: true,
        tenantId: true,
        revokedAt: true,
        expiresAt: true,
        ipAddress: true,
        userAgent: true,
        lastActivity: true,
      },
    });

    if (!session) {
      this.logger.warn("SessionGuard: Session not found", {
        sessionId,
        userId: user.sub,
        ip: request.ip,
      });
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Session not found. Please login again.");
    }

    // Step 3: Verify session belongs to same user
    if (session.userId !== user.sub) {
      this.logger.error("SessionGuard: Session user mismatch", {
        sessionId,
        sessionUserId: session.userId,
        tokenUserId: user.sub,
      });
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Session does not belong to this user");
    }

    // Step 4: Verify session belongs to same tenant
    if (session.tenantId !== request.tenantId) {
      this.logger.error("SessionGuard: Session tenant mismatch", {
        sessionId,
        sessionTenant: session.tenantId,
        requestTenant: request.tenantId,
      });
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Session does not belong to this tenant");
    }

    // Step 5: Check if session is revoked
    if (session.revokedAt) {
      this.logger.warn("SessionGuard: Session revoked", {
        sessionId,
        userId: user.sub,
        revokedAt: session.revokedAt,
      });
      throw new UnauthorizedException(
        "Session has been revoked. Please login again.",
      );
    }

    // Step 6: Check if session is expired
    if (session.expiresAt && session.expiresAt < new Date()) {
      this.logger.warn("SessionGuard: Session expired", {
        sessionId,
        userId: user.sub,
        expiresAt: session.expiresAt,
      });
      throw new UnauthorizedException(
        "Session has expired. Please login again.",
      );
    }

    // Step 7: Attach session to request for downstream use
    request.session = session;

    // Step 8: Update last activity (optional, can be async)
    // Don't await to avoid blocking the request
    this.updateLastActivity(sessionId).catch((err) => {
      this.logger.error("Failed to update last activity", {
        sessionId,
        error: err.message,
      });
    });

    this.logger.debug("Session validated successfully", {
      sessionId,
      userId: user.sub,
      tenantId: session.tenantId,
    });

    return true;
  }

  /**
   * Update session last activity timestamp
   * Non-blocking, fire-and-forget
   */
  private async updateLastActivity(sessionId: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.sessionRepository.update(sessionId, {
      lastActivity: new Date(),
    });
  }
}



================================================================================
FILE: src/common/guards/tenant-binding.guard.ts
SIZE: 2.46 KB | LINES: 86
================================================================================

import {
  Injectable,
  CanActivate,
  ExecutionContext,
  ForbiddenException,
  UnauthorizedException,
  Logger,
} from "@nestjs/common";

/**
 * TenantBindingGuard
 *
 * Enforces that the tenant in the JWT token matches the tenant resolved from the request.
 * This prevents token reuse across tenants (critical for multi-tenant security).
 *
 * MUST run AFTER:
 * - TenantResolverMiddleware (sets req.tenantId)
 * - JwtAuthGuard (validates JWT and sets req.user)
 *
 * Usage:
 * @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, TenantBindingGuard)
 */
@Injectable()
export class TenantBindingGuard implements CanActivate {
  private readonly logger = new Logger(TenantBindingGuard.name);

  canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): boolean {
    const request = context.switchToHttp().getRequest();

    // Step 1: Verify prerequisites
    const requestTenantId = request.tenantId;
    const user = request.user;

    if (!requestTenantId) {
      // This means TenantResolverMiddleware didn't run or failed
      this.logger.error("TenantBindingGuard: No tenantId on request");
      throw new ForbiddenException(
        "Tenant context not found. This route requires tenant resolution.",
      );
    }

    if (!user) {
      // This means JwtAuthGuard didn't run or token is invalid
      this.logger.error("TenantBindingGuard: No user on request");
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Authentication required");
    }

    // Step 2: Extract tenant from token payload
    const tokenTenantId = user.tenantId;

    if (!tokenTenantId) {
      // Token doesn't have tenantId (shouldn't happen with proper token generation)
      this.logger.error("TenantBindingGuard: Token missing tenantId", {
        userId: user.sub,
        email: user.email,
      });
      throw new ForbiddenException("Invalid token: missing tenant context");
    }

    // Step 3: Enforce tenant binding
    if (tokenTenantId !== requestTenantId) {
      this.logger.warn("Tenant binding violation detected", {
        userId: user.sub,
        email: user.email,
        tokenTenant: tokenTenantId,
        requestTenant: requestTenantId,
        ip: request.ip,
        path: request.path,
      });

      throw new ForbiddenException(
        "Token is not valid for this tenant. Please authenticate with the correct tenant.",
      );
    }

    // Step 4: Success - tenant binding is valid
    this.logger.debug("Tenant binding verified", {
      userId: user.sub,
      tenantId: tokenTenantId,
      path: request.path,
    });

    return true;
  }
}

import { CanActivate, ExecutionContext, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { JwtService } from '@nestjs/jwt';
import { logger } from '../logger';

@Injectable()
export class TokenVerificationGuard implements CanActivate {
  constructor(private readonly jwtService: JwtService) {}

  canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): boolean {
    const request = context.switchToHttp().getRequest();
    const token =
      request.cookies.accessToken ??
      request.headers['x-authorization'] ??
      request.headers['authorization'];

    if (!token) {
      return false;
    }

    try {
      const decoded = this.jwtService.verify(token, {
        secret: process.env.JWT_SECRET,
      });
      if (!decoded) {
        return false;
      }
      request.user = decoded;
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      return false;
    }
  }
}

@Injectable()
/**
 * Add Request User to the request object if present.
 * This guard is used to check if the user is authenticated.
 * If the user is authenticated, the user object is added to the request object.
 * If the user is not authenticated, the request object is not modified.
 * This will NOT block or PROTECT the route.
 *
 * If Protection is required, please use one of the above two Guards.
 */
export class RequestUserGuard implements CanActivate {
  constructor(private readonly jwtService: JwtService) {}

  canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): boolean {
    const request = context.switchToHttp().getRequest();
    const token =
      request.cookies.accessToken ??
      request.headers['x-authorization'] ??
      request.headers['authorization'];

    try {
      if (token) {
        const decoded = this.jwtService.verify(token, {
          secret: process.env.JWT_SECRET,
        });
        if (decoded) request.user = decoded;
      }
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      logger.error({
        message: 'Failed to verify token',
        error,
      });
      return true;
    }
  }
}

import {
  NestInterceptor,
  ExecutionContext,
  CallHandler,
  Injectable,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { map } from 'rxjs/operators';

@Injectable()
export class ResponseInterceptor implements NestInterceptor {
  intercept(context: ExecutionContext, next: CallHandler): Observable<any> {
    return next.handle().pipe(
      map((data) => {
        return {
          status: 200,
          data,
        };
      }),
    );
  }
}

// apps/auth-service/src/common/middleware/tenant-resolver.middleware.ts
import {
  Injectable,
  NestMiddleware,
  NotFoundException,
  Logger,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
import { TenantService } from "../../modules/tenant/tenant.service";
import { Tenant } from "@repo/database";

// Extend Express Request to include tenant
declare global {
  namespace Express {
    interface Request {
      tenant?: Tenant;
      tenantId?: string;
    }
  }
}

@Injectable()
export class TenantResolverMiddleware implements NestMiddleware {
  private readonly logger = new Logger(TenantResolverMiddleware.name);

  constructor(private tenantService: TenantService) {}

  async use(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) {
    try {
      const tenant = await this.resolveTenant(req);

      if (!tenant) {
        // Public endpoints (health, metrics) can proceed
        if (this.isPublicEndpoint(req.path)) {
          return next();
        }

        throw new NotFoundException(
          "Tenant not found. Please check your domain or tenant ID.",
        );
      }

      // Attach tenant to request
      req.tenant = tenant;
      req.tenantId = tenant.id;

      // Log for debugging (remove in production)
      this.logger.debug(
        `Resolved tenant: ${tenant.slug} (${tenant.id}) from ${req.hostname}`,
      );

      next();
    } catch (error) {
      next(error);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Resolve tenant from request
   * Priority:
   * 1. Custom domain (auth.acme.com)
   * 2. Subdomain (acme.fortknox.io)
   * 3. X-Tenant-ID header (fallback)
   */
  private async resolveTenant(req: Request): Promise<Tenant | null> {
    const hostname = req.hostname;

    // Strategy 1: Custom domain lookup
    // auth.acme.com → check if any tenant has this custom domain
    const customDomainTenant =
      await this.tenantService.findByCustomDomain(hostname);

    if (customDomainTenant) {
      return customDomainTenant;
    }

    // Strategy 2: Subdomain extraction
    // acme.fortknox.io → extract "acme"
    const subdomain = this.extractSubdomain(hostname);

    if (subdomain && subdomain !== "www" && subdomain !== "api") {
      const subdomainTenant = await this.tenantService.findBySlug(subdomain);

      if (subdomainTenant) {
        return subdomainTenant;
      }
    }

    // Strategy 3: X-Tenant-ID header (for development/testing)
    const tenantIdHeader = req.headers["x-tenant-id"] as string;

    if (tenantIdHeader) {
      // Try as ID first
      let tenant = await this.tenantService.findById(tenantIdHeader);

      // Try as slug if ID lookup fails
      if (!tenant) {
        tenant = await this.tenantService.findBySlug(tenantIdHeader);
      }

      if (tenant) {
        return tenant;
      }
    }

    return null;
  }

  /**
   * Extract subdomain from hostname
   * Examples:
   * - acme.fortknox.io → "acme"
   * - auth.acme.com → "auth"
   * - localhost:4000 → null
   */
  private extractSubdomain(hostname: string): string | null {
    // Remove port if present
    const host: string = hostname.split(":")[0] as string;

    // Localhost or IP address
    if (host === "localhost" || /^\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/.test(host)) {
      return null;
    }

    const parts = host.split(".");

    // Need at least 3 parts for subdomain (sub.domain.tld)
    if (parts.length < 3) {
      return null;
    }

    // Return first part as subdomain
    return parts[0] as string;
  }

  /**
   * Check if endpoint is public (doesn't require tenant)
   */
  private isPublicEndpoint(path: string): boolean {
    const publicPaths = ["/health", "/metrics", "/.well-known", "/favicon.ico"];

    return publicPaths.some((publicPath) => path.startsWith(publicPath));
  }
}

FILE: src/common/service/redis.service.ts
SIZE: 3.52 KB | LINES: 155

import { Injectable, OnModuleInit, OnModuleDestroy } from "@nestjs/common";
import { ConfigService } from "@nestjs/config";
import Redis from "ioredis";

@Injectable()
export class RedisService implements OnModuleInit, OnModuleDestroy {
  private client: Redis;
  private keyPrefix: string;

  constructor(private configService: ConfigService) {
    this.keyPrefix =
      this.configService.get("REDIS_KEY_PREFIX", "fortknox") + ":";
  }

  async onModuleInit() {
    this.client = new Redis({
      host: this.configService.get("REDIS_HOST", "localhost"),
      port: this.configService.get("REDIS_PORT", 6379),
      password: this.configService.get("REDIS_PASSWORD") || undefined,
      db: this.configService.get("REDIS_DB", 0),
      retryStrategy: (times) => {
        const delay = Math.min(times * 50, 2000);
        return delay;
      },
      maxRetriesPerRequest: 3,
    });

    this.client.on("connect", () => {
      console.log("✅ Redis connected");
    });

    this.client.on("error", (err) => {
      console.error("❌ Redis error:", err);
    });
  }

  async onModuleDestroy() {
    await this.client.quit();
  }

  /**
   * Build tenant-scoped key
   * Format: fortknox:t:{tenantId}:{resource}:{identifier}
   */
  private buildKey(
    tenantId: string,
    resource: string,
    identifier: string,
  ): string {
    return `${this.keyPrefix}t:${tenantId}:${resource}:${identifier}`;
  }

  /**
   * Set value with optional TTL
   */
  async set(
    tenantId: string,
    resource: string,
    identifier: string,
    value: any,
    ttl?: number,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const key = this.buildKey(tenantId, resource, identifier);
    const serialized = JSON.stringify(value);

    if (ttl) {
      await this.client.setex(key, ttl, serialized);
    } else {
      await this.client.set(key, serialized);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Get value
   */
  async get<T>(
    tenantId: string,
    resource: string,
    identifier: string,
  ): Promise<T | null> {
    const key = this.buildKey(tenantId, resource, identifier);
    const value = await this.client.get(key);

    if (!value) return null;

    try {
      return JSON.parse(value) as T;
    } catch {
      return value as any;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Delete value
   */
  async delete(
    tenantId: string,
    resource: string,
    identifier: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const key = this.buildKey(tenantId, resource, identifier);
    await this.client.del(key);
  }

  /**
   * Delete multiple keys by pattern
   */
  async deletePattern(tenantId: string, pattern: string): Promise<number> {
    const fullPattern = `${this.keyPrefix}t:${tenantId}:${pattern}`;
    const keys = await this.client.keys(fullPattern);

    if (keys.length === 0) return 0;

    return await this.client.del(...keys);
  }

  /**
   * Check if key exists
   */
  async exists(
    tenantId: string,
    resource: string,
    identifier: string,
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    const key = this.buildKey(tenantId, resource, identifier);
    const result = await this.client.exists(key);
    return result === 1;
  }

  /**
   * Get TTL (time to live) in seconds
   */
  async getTTL(
    tenantId: string,
    resource: string,
    identifier: string,
  ): Promise<number> {
    const key = this.buildKey(tenantId, resource, identifier);
    return await this.client.ttl(key);
  }

  /**
   * Extend TTL
   */
  async extendTTL(
    tenantId: string,
    resource: string,
    identifier: string,
    ttl: number,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const key = this.buildKey(tenantId, resource, identifier);
    await this.client.expire(key, ttl);
  }
}

FILE: src/common/service/session.service.ts
SIZE: 7.18 KB | LINES: 302
================================================================================

import { Injectable } from "@nestjs/common";
import { InjectRepository } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { Repository, IsNull } from "typeorm";
import { Session } from "@repo/database";
import { RedisService } from "./redis.service";
import { ConfigService } from "@nestjs/config";

export interface SessionData {
  id: string;
  userId: string;
  tenantId: string;
  email: string;
  refreshTokenFamilyId: string;
  ipAddress?: string;
  userAgent?: string;
  deviceId?: string;
  createdAt: Date;
  expiresAt: Date;
}

@Injectable()
export class SessionService {
  private sessionTTL: number;

  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(Session)
    private sessionRepository: Repository<Session>,
    private redisService: RedisService,
    private configService: ConfigService,
  ) {
    this.sessionTTL = this.configService.get("SESSION_TTL", 604800); // 7 days
  }

  /**
   * Create session (PostgreSQL + Redis)
   */
  async createSession(data: {
    userId: string;
    tenantId: string;
    email: string;
    refreshTokenFamilyId?: string;
    refreshTokenHash?: string;
    ipAddress?: string;
    userAgent?: string;
    deviceId?: string;
  }): Promise<Session> {
    // 1. Create in PostgreSQL (permanent record)
    const session = await this.sessionRepository.save({
      userId: data.userId,
      tenantId: data.tenantId,
      refreshTokenFamilyId: data.refreshTokenFamilyId,
      refreshTokenHash: data.refreshTokenHash,
      ipAddress: data.ipAddress,
      userAgent: data.userAgent,
      deviceId: data.deviceId,
      lastActivity: new Date(),
      expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + this.sessionTTL * 1000),
    });

    // 2. Store in Redis (fast lookup)
    const sessionData: SessionData = {
      id: session.id,
      userId: session.userId,
      tenantId: session.tenantId,
      email: data.email,
      refreshTokenFamilyId: session.refreshTokenFamilyId!,
      ipAddress: session.ipAddress,
      userAgent: session.userAgent,
      deviceId: session.deviceId,
      createdAt: session.createdAt,
      expiresAt: session.expiresAt!,
    };

    await this.redisService.set(
      data.tenantId,
      "session",
      session.id,
      sessionData,
      this.sessionTTL,
    );

    console.log(`✅ Session created: ${session.id} (PostgreSQL + Redis)`);

    return session;
  }

  /**
   * Validate session (Redis first, PostgreSQL fallback)
   */
  async validateSession(
    tenantId: string,
    sessionId: string,
  ): Promise<SessionData | null> {
    // 1. Try Redis first (fast path)
    const cached = await this.redisService.get<SessionData>(
      tenantId,
      "session",
      sessionId,
    );

    if (cached) {
      // Check expiry
      if (new Date(cached.expiresAt) < new Date()) {
        // Expired - remove from Redis
        await this.redisService.delete(tenantId, "session", sessionId);
        return null;
      }

      // Extend activity (update TTL)
      await this.redisService.extendTTL(
        tenantId,
        "session",
        sessionId,
        this.sessionTTL,
      );

      return cached;
    }

    // 2. Fallback to PostgreSQL (cache miss)
    const session = await this.sessionRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        id: sessionId,
        tenantId,
        revokedAt: IsNull(),
      },
      relations: ["user"],
    });

    if (!session) {
      return null;
    }

    // Check expiry
    if (session.expiresAt && session.expiresAt < new Date()) {
      return null;
    }

    // Rebuild cache
    const sessionData: SessionData = {
      id: session.id,
      userId: session.userId,
      tenantId: session.tenantId,
      email: session.user.email,
      refreshTokenFamilyId: session.refreshTokenFamilyId!,
      ipAddress: session.ipAddress,
      userAgent: session.userAgent,
      deviceId: session.deviceId,
      createdAt: session.createdAt,
      expiresAt: session.expiresAt!,
    };

    // Cache it
    await this.redisService.set(
      tenantId,
      "session",
      sessionId,
      sessionData,
      this.sessionTTL,
    );

    // Update last activity in DB (async, don't wait)
    this.sessionRepository
      .update(sessionId, { lastActivity: new Date() })
      .catch(console.error);

    return sessionData;
  }

  /**
   * Revoke session (PostgreSQL + Redis)
   */
  async revokeSession(
    tenantId: string,
    sessionId: string,
    reason: string = "user_logout",
  ): Promise<void> {
    // 1. Update PostgreSQL
    await this.sessionRepository.update(
      { id: sessionId, tenantId },
      {
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: reason,
      },
    );

    // 2. Remove from Redis
    await this.redisService.delete(tenantId, "session", sessionId);

    console.log(`🔒 Session revoked: ${sessionId} (${reason})`);
  }

  /**
   * Revoke all user sessions (PostgreSQL + Redis)
   */
  async revokeAllUserSessions(
    tenantId: string,
    userId: string,
    reason: string = "user_logout_all",
  ): Promise<void> {
    // 1. Get all active sessions
    const sessions = await this.sessionRepository.find({
      where: {
        userId,
        tenantId,
        revokedAt: IsNull(),
      },
    });

    // 2. Revoke in PostgreSQL
    await this.sessionRepository.update(
      { userId, tenantId, revokedAt: IsNull() },
      {
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: reason,
      },
    );

    // 3. Remove from Redis
    for (const session of sessions) {
      await this.redisService.delete(tenantId, "session", session.id);
    }

    console.log(`🔒 All sessions revoked for user: ${userId}`);
  }

  /**
   * Revoke session family (token reuse detected)
   */
  async revokeSessionFamily(tenantId: string, familyId: string): Promise<void> {
    // 1. Get all sessions in family
    const sessions = await this.sessionRepository.find({
      where: {
        refreshTokenFamilyId: familyId,
        tenantId,
        revokedAt: IsNull(),
      },
    });

    // 2. Revoke in PostgreSQL
    await this.sessionRepository.update(
      { refreshTokenFamilyId: familyId, tenantId },
      {
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: "token_reuse_detected",
      },
    );

    // 3. Remove from Redis
    for (const session of sessions) {
      await this.redisService.delete(tenantId, "session", session.id);
    }

    console.log(`🚨 Session family revoked: ${familyId}`);
  }

  /**
   * Update refresh token (rotation)
   */
  async updateRefreshToken(
    tenantId: string,
    sessionId: string,
    newRefreshTokenHash: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    // 1. Update PostgreSQL
    await this.sessionRepository.update(
      { id: sessionId, tenantId },
      {
        refreshTokenHash: newRefreshTokenHash,
        lastRefreshAt: new Date(),
        lastActivity: new Date(),
      },
    );

    // 2. Extend Redis TTL (session is being used)
    await this.redisService.extendTTL(
      tenantId,
      "session",
      sessionId,
      this.sessionTTL,
    );

    console.log(`🔄 Refresh token rotated: ${sessionId}`);
  }

  /**
   * Get active sessions for user
   */
  async getUserSessions(tenantId: string, userId: string): Promise<Session[]> {
    return this.sessionRepository.find({
      where: {
        userId,
        tenantId,
        revokedAt: IsNull(),
      },
      order: {
        lastActivity: "DESC",
      },
    });
  }
}

import * as Joi from 'joi';

export const validationSchema = Joi.object({
  NODE_ENV: Joi.string()
    .valid('development', 'test', 'production')
    .default('development'),
  PORT: Joi.number().port().default(4000),

  ISSUER_BASE_URL: Joi.string()
    .uri({ scheme: ['http', 'https'] })
    .required(),
  TENANT_RESOLUTION: Joi.string().valid('path', 'subdomain').default('path'),

  JWT_ALG: Joi.string().valid('RS256', 'EdDSA').default('RS256'),
  JWKS_CACHE_SECONDS: Joi.number().integer().min(0).default(300),

  KEYRING_MASTER_KEY: Joi.string().base64().length(44).required(), // 32 bytes (base64 => 44 chars)

  DB_URL: Joi.string()
    .uri({ scheme: ['postgres', 'postgresql'] })
    .required(),
  DB_SSL: Joi.string()
    .valid('true', 'false', '1', '0', 'yes', 'no', 'on', 'off')
    .default('false'),
  DB_LOGGING: Joi.string()
    .valid('true', 'false', '1', '0', 'yes', 'no', 'on', 'off')
    .default('false'),

  REDIS_HOST: Joi.string().default('localhost'),
  REDIS_PORT: Joi.number().integer().min(1).default(6379),
  REDIS_USERNAME: Joi.string().allow(''),
  REDIS_PASSWORD: Joi.string().allow(''),
});

import { Module, ValidationPipe } from "@nestjs/common";
import { NestFactory } from "@nestjs/core";
import { AppApiModule } from "./modules/app-module";
import { DocumentBuilder, SwaggerModule } from "@nestjs/swagger";
import { logger } from "src/common/logger";
import { ResponseInterceptor } from "src/common/interceptors/response.interceptors";
import { saveApiDoc } from "./services/utility/utility.service";
import { ALLOWED_URLS } from "../common/constants";
import cookieParser from "cookie-parser";
import session from "express-session";

@Module({
  imports: [],
})
export class AppModule {
  static async startApp(): Promise<void> {
    const app = await NestFactory.create(AppApiModule, { bufferLogs: true });
    app.enableCors({
      origin: ALLOWED_URLS,
      methods: "GET,HEAD,PUT,PATCH,POST,DELETE",
      optionsSuccessStatus: 200,
      credentials: true,
    });
    app.useGlobalPipes(
      new ValidationPipe({
        whitelist: true,
        transform: true,
      }),
    );

    const config = new DocumentBuilder()
      .setTitle("identity-server")
      .setDescription("API for Identity Server")
      .setVersion("1.0")
      .setExternalDoc("Sample", "https://id.sample.com")
      .addBearerAuth()
      .build();

    const documentForGeneralApis = SwaggerModule.createDocument(app, config);

    SwaggerModule.setup("api-docs", app, documentForGeneralApis);
    saveApiDoc("api-docs", documentForGeneralApis, "nest-be");

    if (app) {
      logger.info("Application started successfully");
      logger.info(`Application is running on: ${process.env.PORT}`);
      app.useGlobalInterceptors(new ResponseInterceptor());
      app.use(cookieParser());
      // app.setGlobalPrefix('api');
      app.use(
        session({
          secret: "nest-session-secret",
          resave: false,
          saveUninitialized: false,
        }),
      );
      await app.listen(process.env.PORT || 4000);
    }
  }
}

import { Module } from "@nestjs/common";
import { TypeOrmModule } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { ConfigModule, ConfigService } from "@nestjs/config";
import { dataSourceOptions } from "@repo/database";

@Module({
  imports: [
    TypeOrmModule.forRootAsync({
      imports: [ConfigModule],
      inject: [ConfigService],
      useFactory: (configService: ConfigService) =>
        ({
          ...dataSourceOptions,
          host: configService.get("DATABASE_HOST"),
          port: configService.get("DATABASE_PORT"),
          username: configService.get("DATABASE_USER"),
          password: configService.get("DATABASE_PASSWORD"),
          database: configService.get("DATABASE_NAME"),
          logging: configService.get("DATABASE_LOGGING") === "true",
          autoLoadEntities: true,
          synchronize: false, // NEVER use in production
        }) as any,
    }),
  ],
})
export class DatabaseModule {}

FILE: src/core/modules/cache/cache.service.ts
SIZE: 1.47 KB | LINES: 63
================================================================================

import { Injectable } from "@nestjs/common";
import Redis from "ioredis";
import { CacheTTL } from "@/common/constants/cache";

type RedisConfig = {
  host: string;
  port: number;
  username?: string;
  password?: string;
};

@Injectable()
export class RedisCacheService {
  private readonly redis: Redis;

  constructor() {
    this.redis = new Redis(RedisCacheService.getConfig());
  }

  static getConfig() {
    const config: RedisConfig = {
      host: process.env.REDIS_HOST || "localhost",
      port: parseInt(process.env.REDIS_PORT || "6379", 10),
    };
    if (process.env.REDIS_USERNAME) {
      config.username = process.env.REDIS_USERNAME;
    }

    if (process.env.REDIS_PASSWORD) {
      config.password = process.env.REDIS_PASSWORD;
    }

    return config;
  }

  async get<T>(key: string): Promise<T | null> {
    const value = await this.redis.get(key);
    return value ? JSON.parse(value) : null;
  }

  async set(key: string, value: unknown, ttlSeconds = CacheTTL.threeDays) {
    await this.redis.set(key, JSON.stringify(value), "EX", ttlSeconds);
  }

  async update(key: string, value: unknown) {
    await this.redis.set(key, JSON.stringify(value), "KEEPTTL");
  }

  async del(key: string) {
    await this.redis.del(key);
  }

  async flush() {
    await this.redis.flushall();
  }

  async deleteByPattern(pattern: string): Promise<string[]> {
    const keys = await this.redis.keys(pattern);
    if (keys.length) await this.redis.del(...keys);
    return keys;
  }
}

import { MiddlewareConsumer, Module, NestModule } from "@nestjs/common";
import { ConfigModule, ConfigService } from "@nestjs/config";
import { TypeOrmModule } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { HealthCheckModule } from "../health-check/health-check.module";
import { validationSchema } from "@/config/env.validation";
import { CacheModule } from "@nestjs/cache-manager";
import * as redisStore from "cache-manager-redis-store";
import { TenantResolverMiddleware } from "@/common/midllewares/tenant-resolver.middleware";
import { TenantModule } from "@/modules/tenant/tenant.module";

@Module({
  imports: [
    ConfigModule.forRoot({
      isGlobal: true,
      envFilePath: [".env"],
      validationSchema,
    }),

    HealthCheckModule,
    TenantModule,

    // Cache (Redis)
    CacheModule.registerAsync({
      isGlobal: true,
      imports: [ConfigModule],
      inject: [ConfigService],
      useFactory: (configService: ConfigService) => ({
        store: redisStore,
        host: configService.get("REDIS_HOST", "localhost"),
        port: configService.get("REDIS_PORT", 6379),
        password: configService.get("REDIS_PASSWORD"),
        ttl: 300, // 5 minutes default
      }),
    }),

    // Database
    TypeOrmModule.forRootAsync({
      imports: [ConfigModule],
      inject: [ConfigService],
      useFactory: (configService: ConfigService) => ({
        type: "postgres",
        host: configService.get("DATABASE_HOST"),
        port: configService.get("DATABASE_PORT"),
        username: configService.get("DATABASE_USER"),
        password: configService.get("DATABASE_PASSWORD"),
        database: configService.get("DATABASE_NAME"),
        autoLoadEntities: true,
        synchronize: false,
        logging: configService.get("DATABASE_LOGGING") === "true",
        ssl:
          configService.get("DB_SSL") === "true"
            ? { rejectUnauthorized: false }
            : false,
      }),
    }),
  ],
})
export class GlobalModule implements NestModule {
  configure(consumer: MiddlewareConsumer) {
    // Apply tenant resolver to ALL routes
    consumer.apply(TenantResolverMiddleware).forRoutes("*");
  }
}

import { Controller, Get, Req, Res } from "@nestjs/common";
import { HealthCheckService } from "./health-check.service";
import { type Response, type Request } from "express";
import { ApiOperation, ApiResponse, ApiTags } from "@nestjs/swagger";

@ApiTags("Health Check")
@Controller("/")
export class HealthCheckController {
  constructor(private readonly healthCheckService: HealthCheckService) {}

  @Get("")
  async welcome(): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
    return this.healthCheckService.welcome();
  }

  @Get("/api/health-check")
  async serviceCheck() {
    return {
      message: "Health Check - All Services are up and running!",
      status: 200,
    };
  }

  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Returns Api docs Yaml" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Returns API Docs Yaml." })
  @Get("api-docs.yaml")
  async getSwaggerYaml(@Req() req: Request, @Res() res: Response) {
    if (req.query["secret"] !== "DhaivatRocks") {
      return res.status(401).send("Unauthorized");
    }

    const data = await this.healthCheckService.getSwaggerYaml();
    res.setHeader("Content-Type", "application/x-yaml");
    res.send(data);
  }
}

FILE: src/core/modules/stats/stats.service.ts
SIZE: 986 B | LINES: 48
================================================================================

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import * as os from 'os';

@Injectable()
export class StatsService {
  private startTime: number;
  private requestCount: number;

  constructor() {
    this.startTime = Date.now();
    this.requestCount = 0;
  }

  getSystemStats() {
    const uptime = os.uptime();
    const loadAverage = os.loadavg();
    const memoryUsage = process.memoryUsage();
    const totalMemory = os.totalmem();
    const freeMemory = os.freemem();
    const cpuUsage = os.cpus();

    return {
      uptime,
      loadAverage,
      memoryUsage,
      totalMemory,
      freeMemory,
      cpuUsage,
    };
  }

  incrementRequestCount() {
    this.requestCount++;
  }

  getThroughput() {
    const currentTime = Date.now();
    const elapsedTime = (currentTime - this.startTime) / 1000; // in seconds
    const throughput = this.requestCount / elapsedTime;

    return {
      requestCount: this.requestCount,
      elapsedTime,
      throughput,
    };
  }
}

FILE: src/core/services/utility/utility.service.ts
SIZE: 442 B | LINES: 18
================================================================================

import { Injectable } from "@nestjs/common";
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from "fs";

@Injectable()
export class UtilityService {}

export function saveApiDoc(
  fileName: string,
  document: any,
  folderName: string,
) {
  const dir = `./api-management/${folderName}`;
  if (!existsSync(dir)) {
    mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  }
  writeFileSync(`${dir}/${fileName}.yaml`, JSON.stringify(document, null, 2));
}

import 'reflect-metadata';
import { DataSource } from 'typeorm';
import { join } from 'path';
import * as dotenv from 'dotenv';

// Always load root .env unless DOTENV_CONFIG_PATH is set
dotenv.config({
  path: process.env.DOTENV_CONFIG_PATH || join(process.cwd(), '.env'),
});

const yes = (v?: string, d = false) =>
  v === undefined
    ? d
    : ['1', 'true', 'yes', 'on'].includes(String(v).toLowerCase());

export const AppDataSource = new DataSource({
  type: 'postgres',
  url: process.env.DB_URL,
  ssl: yes(process.env.DB_SSL) ? { rejectUnauthorized: false } : false,
  logging: yes(process.env.DB_LOGGING),
  // find all entities anywhere under src/**
  entities: [join(__dirname, '../**/*.entity.{ts,js}')],
  // your repo keeps migrations under src/database/migrations
  migrations: [join(__dirname, './migrations/*.{ts,js}')],
});

// apps/auth-service/src/modules/oauth/scripts/seed-oauth-client.ts

import { NestFactory } from "@nestjs/core";
import { AppModule } from "@/core/app.module";
import { OAuthClientService } from "@/modules/oauth/services/oauth-client.service";

/**
 * Seed first-party OAuth client for Fort Knox Admin Portal
 */
async function seedOAuthClient() {
  const app = await NestFactory.createApplicationContext(AppModule);

  const clientService = app.get(OAuthClientService);

  // Get default tenant ID (you may need to query this)
  const tenantId = "your-tenant-id-here"; // TODO: Get from database

  try {
    // Create Fort Knox Admin Portal client
    const { client, clientSecret } = await clientService.createClient({
      tenantId,
      name: "Fort Knox Admin Portal",
      description: "First-party admin portal for Fort Knox",
      clientType: "public", // SPA doesn't have server
      grantTypes: ["authorization_code", "refresh_token"],
      redirectUris: [
        "http://localhost:3000/callback",
        "http://localhost:3000/auth/callback",
        "https://admin.fortknox.io/callback",
      ],
      postLogoutRedirectUris: [
        "http://localhost:3000",
        "https://admin.fortknox.io",
      ],
      allowedOrigins: ["http://localhost:3000", "https://admin.fortknox.io"],
      allowedScopes: ["openid", "profile", "email", "offline_access"],
      defaultScopes: ["openid", "profile", "email"],
      requirePkce: true, // MUST for public clients
      requireConsent: false, // First-party app
      isFirstParty: true,
      accessTokenTtl: 900, // 15 minutes
      refreshTokenTtl: 604800, // 7 days
      idTokenTtl: 3600, // 1 hour
    });

    console.log("✅ Created Fort Knox Admin Portal client:");
    console.log("   Client ID:", client.clientId);
    console.log("   Client Type:", client.clientType);
    console.log("   Grant Types:", client.grantTypes);
    console.log("   Redirect URIs:", client.redirectUris);
    console.log("   Require PKCE:", client.requirePkce);

    // Create a confidential client for testing
    const { client: backendClient, clientSecret: backendSecret } =
      await clientService.createClient({
        tenantId,
        name: "Fort Knox Backend Service",
        description: "Backend service for machine-to-machine auth",
        clientType: "confidential",
        grantTypes: ["client_credentials"],
        redirectUris: [], // Not needed for M2M
        allowedScopes: ["users:read", "users:write", "api:read", "api:write"],
        defaultScopes: ["users:read", "api:read"],
        requirePkce: false,
        requireConsent: false,
        isFirstParty: true,
        accessTokenTtl: 3600, // 1 hour for M2M
      });

    console.log("\n✅ Created Fort Knox Backend Service client:");
    console.log("   Client ID:", backendClient.clientId);
    console.log("   Client Secret:", backendSecret);
    console.log("   ⚠️  SAVE THIS SECRET - IT WILL NOT BE SHOWN AGAIN!");
    console.log("   Client Type:", backendClient.clientType);
    console.log("   Grant Types:", backendClient.grantTypes);

    // Create a third-party client example
    const { client: thirdPartyClient, clientSecret: thirdPartySecret } =
      await clientService.createClient({
        tenantId,
        name: "Example Third-Party App",
        description: "Example third-party application",
        logoUrl: "https://example.com/logo.png",
        websiteUrl: "https://example.com",
        privacyPolicyUrl: "https://example.com/privacy",
        termsOfServiceUrl: "https://example.com/terms",
        clientType: "confidential",
        grantTypes: ["authorization_code", "refresh_token"],
        redirectUris: ["https://example.com/callback"],
        allowedScopes: ["openid", "profile", "email"],
        requirePkce: false,
        requireConsent: true, // Third-party MUST show consent
        isFirstParty: false,
        accessTokenTtl: 900,
        refreshTokenTtl: 604800,
      });

    console.log("\n✅ Created Example Third-Party App client:");
    console.log("   Client ID:", thirdPartyClient.clientId);
    console.log("   Client Secret:", thirdPartySecret);
    console.log("   Client Type:", thirdPartyClient.clientType);
    console.log("   Require Consent:", thirdPartyClient.requireConsent);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("❌ Error seeding OAuth clients:", error);
  }

  await app.close();
}

// Run seed
seedOAuthClient()
  .then(() => {
    console.log("\n✅ OAuth client seeding complete");
    process.exit(0);
  })
  .catch((error) => {
    console.error("❌ Fatal error:", error);
    process.exit(1);
  });

             'partner_app_' || substr(md5(random()::text), 1, 16),
             '$argon2id$v=19$m=65536,t=3,p=4$placeholder_partner_secret', -- TODO: Hash actual secret
             'confidential',
             'QuantumSync Analytics',
             'Third-party analytics platform integrated with NexaTech',
             'https://quantumsync.io/logo.png',
             'https://quantumsync.io',
             'https://quantumsync.io/privacy',
             'https://quantumsync.io/terms',
             ARRAY['authorization_code', 'refresh_token']::text[],
             ARRAY[
                 'https://quantumsync.io/auth/callback',
             'https://app.quantumsync.io/callback'
                 ]::text[],
             ARRAY[
                 'https://quantumsync.io',
             'https://app.quantumsync.io'
                 ]::text[],
             ARRAY[
                 'https://quantumsync.io',
             'https://app.quantumsync.io'
                 ]::text[],
             'client_secret_post',
             false, -- Confidential client, PKCE optional
             ARRAY['openid', 'profile', 'email']::text[],
             ARRAY['openid', 'profile']::text[],
             900,

             3600,

             true,
             'web',
             'public',
             true, -- Third-party MUST show consent
             false,
             true,
             admin_user_id,
             NOW(),
             NOW()
         );

RAISE NOTICE '✅ Created OAuth client: QuantumSync Analytics (third-party, requires consent)';

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

    id,
    name,
    slug,
    active,
    created_at
FROM tenants
WHERE slug = 'nexatech';

-- Check admin user
SELECT
    id,
    email,
    first_name,
    last_name,
    email_verified,
    is_active
FROM users
WHERE email = 'admin@nexatech.io';

-- Check OAuth clients
SELECT
    client_id,
    name,
    client_type,
    grant_types,
    require_pkce,
    require_consent,
    is_first_party,
    is_active
FROM oauth_clients
WHERE tenant_id = (SELECT id FROM tenants WHERE slug = 'nexatech')
ORDER BY created_at;

import { DataSource } from "typeorm";
import { Tenant, Role, Permission } from "@repo/database";
import * as crypto from "crypto";

export async function seed() {
  // Connect to database
  const dataSource = new DataSource({
    type: "postgres",
    url: process.env.DATABASE_URL,
    entities: [Tenant, Role, Permission],
    ssl:
      process.env.DATABASE_SSL === "true"
        ? { rejectUnauthorized: false }
        : false,
  });

  await dataSource.initialize();
  console.log("🔌 Connected to database");

  try {
    // ==================================================
    // 1. Seed Tenants
    // ==================================================
    console.log("🏢 Seeding tenants...");

    const tenants = [
      {
        name: "Default Organization",
        slug: "default",
        subdomain: "default",
        status: "active",
        plan: "free",
        settings: {},
        featureFlags: {
          mfaRequired: false,
          passwordlessEnabled: true,
          socialProvidersEnabled: true,
        },
        isActive: true,
        environment: "production",
      },
      {
        name: "Acme Corporation",
        slug: "acme",
        subdomain: "acme",
        customDomain: "auth.acme.com",
        customDomainVerified: false,
        status: "active",
        plan: "professional",
        settings: {
          branding: {
            logo: "https://acme.com/logo.png",
            primaryColor: "#FF6B35",
          },
        },
        featureFlags: {
          mfaRequired: true,
          passwordlessEnabled: true,
          socialProvidersEnabled: true,
          samlEnabled: true,
        },
        isActive: true,
        environment: "production",
      },
      {
        name: "Startup Inc",
        slug: "startup",
        subdomain: "startup",
        status: "trial",
        plan: "starter",
        settings: {
          allowSignup: true,
          requireEmailVerification: true,
          mfaRequired: true,
        },
        featureFlags: {
          mfaRequired: false,
          passwordlessEnabled: false,
          socialProvidersEnabled: true,
        },
        isActive: true,
        environment: "production",
        trialEndsAt: new Date(Date.now() + 14 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000), // 14 days
      },
    ];

    const tenantRepo = dataSource.getRepository(Tenant);

    for (const tenantData of tenants) {
      const existing = await tenantRepo.findOne({
        where: { slug: tenantData.slug },
      });
      if (!existing) {
        const tenant = tenantRepo.create(tenantData);
        await tenantRepo.save(tenant);
        console.log(`  ✅ Created tenant: ${tenant.name} (${tenant.slug})`);
      } else {
        console.log(`  ⏭️  Tenant exists: ${existing.name}`);
      }
    }

    // ==================================================
    // 2. Seed Roles (per tenant)
    // ==================================================
    console.log("👥 Seeding roles...");

    const allTenants = await tenantRepo.find();
    const roleRepo = dataSource.getRepository(Role);

    const systemRoles = [
      {
        name: "Owner",
        description: "Full access to everything in the organization",
        isSystem: true,
      },
      {
        name: "Admin",
        description: "Administrative access with some limitations",
        isSystem: true,
      },
      {
        name: "Member",
        description: "Basic member access",
        isSystem: true,
      },
      {
        name: "Developer",
        description: "Access to API keys and developer tools",
        isSystem: false,
      },
      {
        name: "Viewer",
        description: "Read-only access",
        isSystem: false,
      },
    ];

    for (const tenant of allTenants) {
      for (const roleData of systemRoles) {
        const existing = await roleRepo.findOne({
          where: { name: roleData.name, tenantId: tenant.id },
        });

        if (!existing) {
          const role = roleRepo.create({
            ...roleData,
            tenantId: tenant.id,
          });
          await roleRepo.save(role);
          console.log(`  ✅ Created role: ${tenant.slug}/${role.name}`);
        }
      }
    }

    // ==================================================
    // 3. Seed Permissions (per tenant)
    // ==================================================
    console.log("🔐 Seeding permissions...");

    const permissionRepo = dataSource.getRepository(Permission);

    const basePermissions = [
      // User management
      { resource: "user", action: "create", description: "Create users" },
      { resource: "user", action: "read", description: "View users" },
      { resource: "user", action: "update", description: "Update users" },
      { resource: "user", action: "delete", description: "Delete users" },
      {
        resource: "user",
        action: "impersonate",
        description: "Impersonate users",
      },

      // Role management
      { resource: "role", action: "create", description: "Create roles" },
      { resource: "role", action: "read", description: "View roles" },
      { resource: "role", action: "update", description: "Update roles" },
      { resource: "role", action: "delete", description: "Delete roles" },
      {
        resource: "role",
        action: "assign",
        description: "Assign roles to users",
      },

      // Permission management
      {
        resource: "permission",
        action: "read",
        description: "View permissions",
      },
      {
        resource: "permission",
        action: "assign",
        description: "Assign permissions to roles",
      },

      // Tenant management
      {
        resource: "tenant",
        action: "read",
        description: "View tenant settings",
      },
      {
        resource: "tenant",
        action: "update",
        description: "Update tenant settings",
      },
      { resource: "tenant", action: "delete", description: "Delete tenant" },

      // OAuth client management
      {
        resource: "oauth_client",
        action: "create",
        description: "Create OAuth clients",
      },
      {
        resource: "oauth_client",
        action: "read",
        description: "View OAuth clients",
      },
      {
        resource: "oauth_client",
        action: "update",
        description: "Update OAuth clients",
      },
      {
        resource: "oauth_client",
        action: "delete",
        description: "Delete OAuth clients",
      },

      // Provider management
      {
        resource: "provider",
        action: "create",
        description: "Add identity providers",
      },
      {
        resource: "provider",
        action: "read",
        description: "View identity providers",
      },
      {
        resource: "provider",
        action: "update",
        description: "Update identity providers",
      },
      {
        resource: "provider",
        action: "delete",
        description: "Remove identity providers",
      },

      // Audit logs
      { resource: "audit", action: "read", description: "View audit logs" },
      { resource: "audit", action: "export", description: "Export audit logs" },

      // Settings
      { resource: "settings", action: "read", description: "View settings" },
      {
        resource: "settings",
        action: "update",
        description: "Update settings",
      },
    ];

    for (const tenant of allTenants) {
      for (const permData of basePermissions) {
        const existing = await permissionRepo.findOne({
          where: {
            tenantId: tenant.id,
            resource: permData.resource,
            action: permData.action,
          },
        });

        if (!existing) {
          const permission = permissionRepo.create({
            ...permData,
            tenantId: tenant.id,
          });
          await permissionRepo.save(permission);
          console.log(
            `  ✅ Created permission: ${tenant.slug}/${permission.resource}:${permission.action}`,
          );
        }
      }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("✅ Seeding complete!");
    console.log("");
    console.log("📊 Summary:");
    console.log(`   Tenants: ${allTenants.length}`);
    console.log(`   Roles per tenant: ${systemRoles.length}`);
    console.log(`   Permissions per tenant: ${basePermissions.length}`);
    console.log("");
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("❌ Seeding failed:", error);
    throw error;
  } finally {
    await dataSource.destroy();
  }
}

// Run seed
seed()
  .then(() => {
    console.log("✅ Done");
    process.exit(0);
  })
  .catch((error) => {
    console.error("❌ Error:", error);
    process.exit(1);
  });

import { logger } from "./common/logger";
import { AppModule } from "./core/app.module";

async function bootstrap() {
  try {
    await AppModule.startApp();
  } catch (err: any) {
    logger.error("Error starting the application: ", err);
    logger.error(err);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

bootstrap();

FILE: src/modules/auth/auth.controller.ts
