import {
  Controller,
  Post,
  Body,
  HttpCode,
  HttpStatus,
  Ip,
  Headers,
  Get,
  UseGuards,
  Req,
  UnauthorizedException,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import {
  ApiTags,
  ApiOperation,
  ApiResponse,
  ApiBearerAuth,
  ApiBody,
} from "@nestjs/swagger";
import { AuthService } from "./auth.service";
import {
  RegisterDto,
  LoginDto,
  ForgotPasswordDto,
  ResetPasswordDto,
  VerifyMfaDto,
} from "./auth.dto";
import { JwtAuthGuard } from "@/common/guards";
import { type Request } from "express";
import { FortKnoxProtectedGuard } from "@/common/guards/fortknox-protected-route.guard";

/**
 * Authentication Controller
 *
 * Handles all authentication-related endpoints:
 * - Registration
 * - Login
 * - Logout
 * - Password reset
 * - Email verification
 */
@ApiTags("Authentication")
@Controller("auth")
export class AuthController {
  constructor(private readonly authService: AuthService) {}

  /**
   * Register a new user
   */
  @Post("register")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.CREATED)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Register a new user" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 201, description: "User successfully registered" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 400, description: "Bad request - validation failed" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 409, description: "User already exists" })
  async register(
    @Req() req: Request,
    @Body() registerDto: RegisterDto,
    @Ip() ip: string,
    @Headers("user-agent") userAgent: string,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    return this.authService.register({
      ...registerDto,
      tenantId,
      ip,
      userAgent: userAgent || "unknown",
    });
  }

  /**
   * Login with email and password
   */
  @Post("login")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Login with email and password" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Login successful" })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 401,
    description: "Unauthorized - invalid credentials",
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 423, description: "Account locked" })
  async login(
    @Req() req: Request,
    @Body() loginDto: LoginDto,
    @Ip() ip: string,
    @Headers("user-agent") userAgent: string,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    return this.authService.login({
      ...loginDto,
      ip,
      tenantId,
      userAgent: userAgent || "unknown",
    });
  }

  /**
   * Logout - revoke session
   */
  @Post("logout")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)
  @ApiBearerAuth()
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Logout and revoke session" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Logout successful" })
  async logout(
    @Req() req: any,
    @Ip() ip: string,
    @Headers("user-agent") userAgent: string,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    const userId = req.user.sub;
    const sessionId = req.user.sessionId;
    await this.authService.logout(tenantId, userId, sessionId, userAgent, ip);
    return { message: "Logged out successfully" };
  }

  /**
   * Request password reset
   */
  @Post("forgot-password")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Request password reset email" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Password reset email sent" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: "User not found" })
  async forgotPassword(
    @Req() req: Request,
    @Body() forgotPasswordDto: ForgotPasswordDto,
    @Ip() ip: string,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    await this.authService.forgotPassword(
      tenantId,
      forgotPasswordDto.email,
      ip,
    );
    return {
      message:
        "If an account exists with this email, a password reset link has been sent",
    };
  }

  /**
   * Reset password with token
   */
  @Post("reset-password")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Reset password using token" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Password reset successful" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 400, description: "Invalid or expired token" })
  async resetPassword(
    @Req() req: Request,
    @Body() resetPasswordDto: ResetPasswordDto,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    await this.authService.resetPassword(
      tenantId,
      resetPasswordDto.token,
      resetPasswordDto.password,
    );
    return { message: "Password reset successfully" };
  }

  /**
   * Verify email address
   */
  @Post("verify-email")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Verify email address" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Email verified successfully" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 400, description: "Invalid or expired token" })
  async verifyEmail(@Req() req: Request, @Body("token") token: string) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    await this.authService.verifyEmail(tenantId, token);
    return { message: "Email verified successfully" };
  }

  /**
   * Resend verification email
   */
  @Post("resend-verification")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Resend email verification" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Verification email sent" })
  async resendVerification(@Req() req: Request, @Body("email") email: string) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    await this.authService.resendVerification(tenantId, email);
    return { message: "Verification email sent" };
  }

  /**
   * Refresh access token
   */
  @Post("refresh")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Refresh access token" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Token refreshed successfully" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: "Invalid refresh token" })
  async refresh(
    @Req() req: Request,
    @Body("refreshToken") refreshToken: string,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    return this.authService.refreshToken(tenantId, refreshToken);
  }

  /**
   * Get current authenticated user context
   *
   * This endpoint is critical for:
   * 1. Testing auth boundary works correctly
   * 2. Debugging token/session issues
   * 3. Frontend to get current user info
   *
   * Returns complete auth context:
   * - User info from JWT
   * - Tenant info
   * - Session info
   * - Permissions/roles (if loaded)
   */
  @Get("me")
  @UseGuards(FortKnoxProtectedGuard)
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Get current authenticated user",
    description:
      "Returns complete authentication context for debugging and user info display",
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: "Current user context",
    schema: {
      type: "object",
      properties: {
        user: {
          type: "object",
          properties: {
            id: { type: "string" },
            email: { type: "string" },
            firstName: { type: "string" },
            lastName: { type: "string" },
          },
        },
        tenant: {
          type: "object",
          properties: {
            id: { type: "string" },
            name: { type: "string" },
            slug: { type: "string" },
          },
        },
        session: {
          type: "object",
          properties: {
            id: { type: "string" },
            createdAt: { type: "string" },
            lastActivity: { type: "string" },
            expiresAt: { type: "string" },
            ipAddress: { type: "string" },
            userAgent: { type: "string" },
          },
        },
        auth: {
          type: "object",
          properties: {
            method: { type: "string" },
            issuedAt: { type: "string" },
            expiresAt: { type: "string" },
          },
        },
      },
    },
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 401,
    description: "Unauthorized - Invalid token or session",
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 403,
    description: "Forbidden - Tenant mismatch",
  })
  async getCurrentUser(@Req() req: Request) {
    const user = req.user; // From JWT payload
    const session: any = req.session; // From SessionGuard
    const tenant = req.tenant; // From TenantResolverMiddleware

    // Fetch full user details
    const fullUser = await this.authService.getCurrentUser(
      tenant?.id!,
      user.sub,
    );

    if (!fullUser) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("User not found");
    }

    return {
      user: {
        id: fullUser.id,
        email: fullUser.email,
        firstName: fullUser.firstName,
        lastName: fullUser.lastName,
        emailVerified: fullUser.emailVerified,
        lastLoginAt: fullUser.lastLoginAt,
        lastLoginIp: fullUser.lastLoginIp,
      },
      tenant: {
        id: tenant?.id,
        name: tenant?.name,
        slug: tenant?.slug,
        customDomain: tenant?.customDomain,
      },
      session: {
        id: session.id,
        createdAt: session.createdAt,
        lastActivity: session.lastActivity,
        expiresAt: session.expiresAt,
        ipAddress: session.ipAddress,
        userAgent: session.userAgent,
        deviceId: session.deviceId,
      },
      auth: {
        method: "jwt",
        issuedAt: new Date(user.iat * 1000).toISOString(),
        expiresAt: new Date(user.exp * 1000).toISOString(),
        tokenTenant: user.tenantId,
        requestTenant: req.tenantId,
        tenantMatch: user.tenantId === req.tenantId,
      },
      debug: {
        guards: {
          jwtAuth: true,
          tenantBinding: true,
          session: true,
        },
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    };
  }

  /**
   * Verify MFA code and complete login
   */
  @Post("mfa/verify")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Verify MFA code and complete login",
    description:
      "After receiving mfaRequired=true from login, use this endpoint to verify the TOTP code",
  })
  @ApiBody({
    schema: {
      type: "object",
      properties: {
        tempToken: {
          type: "string",
          description: "Temporary token from login response",
        },
        code: {
          type: "string",
          description: "6-digit TOTP code from authenticator app",
          pattern: "^\\d{6}$",
          example: "123456",
        },
      },
      required: ["tempToken", "code"],
    },
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: "MFA verified successfully, tokens issued",
    schema: {
      type: "object",
      properties: {
        accessToken: { type: "string" },
        refreshToken: { type: "string" },
        message: { type: "string" },
      },
    },
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 401,
    description: "Invalid MFA code or expired token",
  })
  async verifyMfa(
    @Req() req: Request,
    @Body() dto: VerifyMfaDto,
    @Ip() ip: string,
    @Headers("user-agent") userAgent: string,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    try {
      return await this.authService.verifyMfa(
        tenantId,
        dto.tempToken,
        dto.code,
        ip,
        userAgent,
      );
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error("❌ MFA verification failed:", error.message);

      if (error instanceof UnauthorizedException) {
        throw error;
      }

      throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid or expired token");
    }
  }

  /**
   * Verify MFA using backup code
   */
  @Post("mfa/verify-backup")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Verify MFA using backup code",
    description: "Use a backup code when authenticator app is unavailable",
  })
  @ApiBody({
    schema: {
      type: "object",
      properties: {
        tempToken: {
          type: "string",
          description: "Temporary token from login response",
        },
        code: {
          type: "string",
          description: "Backup code in format XXXX-XXXX",
          pattern: "^[A-Z0-9]{4}-[A-Z0-9]{4}$",
          example: "ABCD-1234",
        },
      },
      required: ["tempToken", "code"],
    },
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: "Backup code verified, tokens issued",
    schema: {
      type: "object",
      properties: {
        accessToken: { type: "string" },
        refreshToken: { type: "string" },
        message: { type: "string" },
        warning: {
          type: "string",
          description: "Warning about remaining backup codes",
        },
      },
    },
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 401,
    description: "Invalid backup code",
  })
  async verifyMfaBackup(
    @Req() req: Request,
    @Body() dto: VerifyMfaDto,
    @Ip() ip: string,
    @Headers("user-agent") userAgent: string,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    try {
      return await this.authService.verifyMfaBackup(
        tenantId,
        dto.tempToken,
        dto.code,
        ip,
        userAgent,
      );
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error("❌ Backup code verification failed:", error.message);

      if (error instanceof UnauthorizedException) {
        throw error;
      }

      throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid backup code");
    }
  }

  // apps/auth-service/src/modules/auth/auth.controller.ts
  // CLEAN CONTROLLER - ONLY ROUTING

  /**
   * Start WebAuthn authentication (passwordless login)
   */
  @Post("webauthn/login/start")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Start passwordless WebAuthn login",
    description: "Initiate WebAuthn authentication flow",
  })
  @ApiBody({
    schema: {
      type: "object",
      properties: {
        email: { type: "string", format: "email" },
      },
      required: ["email"],
    },
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: "WebAuthn authentication options",
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 401,
    description: "Invalid credentials",
  })
  async startWebAuthnLogin(
    @Req() req: Request,
    @Body() dto: { email: string },
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    return this.authService.startWebAuthnLogin(tenantId, dto.email);
  }

  /**
   * Finish WebAuthn authentication and get tokens
   */
  @Post("webauthn/login/finish")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Complete WebAuthn login",
    description: "Verify WebAuthn response and issue authentication tokens",
  })
  @ApiBody({
    schema: {
      type: "object",
      properties: {
        email: { type: "string", format: "email" },
        response: {
          type: "object",
          description: "WebAuthn authentication response from browser",
        },
      },
      required: ["email", "response"],
    },
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: "Authentication successful",
    schema: {
      type: "object",
      properties: {
        accessToken: { type: "string" },
        refreshToken: { type: "string" },
        user: {
          type: "object",
          properties: {
            email: { type: "string" },
            firstName: { type: "string" },
            lastName: { type: "string" },
            emailVerified: { type: "boolean" },
          },
        },
      },
    },
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 401,
    description: "Authentication failed",
  })
  async finishWebAuthnLogin(
    @Req() req: Request,
    @Body() dto: { email: string; response: any },
    @Ip() ip: string,
    @Headers("user-agent") userAgent: string,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    return this.authService.finishWebAuthnLogin({
      tenantId,
      email: dto.email,
      webauthnResponse: dto.response,
      ip,
      userAgent,
    });
  }
}

import {
  IsEmail,
  IsString,
  MinLength,
  MaxLength,
  Matches,
  IsOptional,
  IsNotEmpty,
} from "class-validator";
import { ApiProperty } from "@nestjs/swagger";

/**
 * Register DTO
 */
export class RegisterDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: "user@example.com" })
  @IsEmail()
  email!: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "SecureP@ssw0rd123!" })
  @IsString()
  @MinLength(8)
  @MaxLength(128)
  @Matches(/^(?=.*[a-z])(?=.*[A-Z])(?=.*\d)(?=.*[@$!%*?&])[A-Za-z\d@$!%*?&]/, {
    message:
      "Password must contain uppercase, lowercase, number and special character",
  })
  password!: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "tenant-uuid", required: false })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  tenantId?: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "John", required: false })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  firstName?: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "Doe", required: false })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  lastName?: string;
}

/**
 * Login DTO
 */
export class LoginDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: "user@example.com" })
  @IsEmail()
  email!: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "SecureP@ssw0rd123!" })
  @IsString()
  @IsNotEmpty()
  password!: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "tenant-uuid", required: false })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  tenantId?: string;
}

/**
 * Forgot Password DTO
 */
export class ForgotPasswordDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: "user@example.com" })
  @IsEmail()
  email!: string;
}

/**
 * Reset Password DTO
 */
export class ResetPasswordDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: "reset-token-here" })
  @IsString()
  token!: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: "NewSecureP@ssw0rd123!" })
  @IsString()
  @MinLength(8)
  @MaxLength(128)
  @Matches(/^(?=.*[a-z])(?=.*[A-Z])(?=.*\d)(?=.*[@$!%*?&])[A-Za-z\d@$!%*?&]/, {
    message:
      "Password must contain uppercase, lowercase, number and special character",
  })
  password!: string;
}

 * Verify Email DTO

  @IsString()

}

import { Module } from "@nestjs/common";
import { TypeOrmModule } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { JwtModule } from "@nestjs/jwt";
import { ConfigModule, ConfigService } from "@nestjs/config";
import {
  User,
  Session,
  SecurityEvent,
  Tenant,
  MfaSettings,
  OAuthRefreshToken,
  UserRole,
} from "@repo/database";
import { UserModule } from "../user/user.module";
import { EmailModule } from "../email/email.module";
import { AuthService } from "./auth.service";
import { AuthController } from "./auth.controller";
import { PasswordService } from "@repo/security";
import { PassportModule } from "@nestjs/passport";
import { JwtStrategy } from "@/common/guards/jwt.strategy";
import { JwtAuthGuard } from "@/common/guards";
import { SessionService } from "@/common/service/session.service";
import { RedisService } from "@/common/service/redis.service";
import { BruteForceService } from "@/modules/auth/brute-force.service";

import { WebAuthnService } from "@/modules/mfa/webauthn.service";

import {
  Injectable,
  UnauthorizedException,
  BadRequestException,
  NotFoundException,
  ConflictException,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { InjectRepository } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { Repository, MoreThan, IsNull } from "typeorm";
import { JwtService } from "@nestjs/jwt";
import { ConfigService } from "@nestjs/config";
import {
  User,
  Session,
  SecurityEvent,
  Tenant,
  OAuthRefreshToken,
} from "@repo/database";
import { PasswordService } from "@repo/security";
import { EmailService } from "../email/email.service";
import * as crypto from "crypto";
import { v4 as uuidv4 } from "uuid";
import { SessionService } from "@/common/service/session.service";
import { BruteForceService } from "@/modules/auth/brute-force.service";
import { MfaService } from "@/modules/mfa/mfa.service";
import { WebAuthnService } from "@/modules/mfa/webauthn.service";
/**
 * Authentication Service (Updated for Multi-Tenant)
 *
 * Handles all authentication business logic with tenant scoping:
 * - User registration (tenant-scoped)
 * - Login/logout (tenant-scoped)
 * - Password reset (tenant-scoped)
 * - Email verification (tenant-scoped)
 * - Session management (tenant-scoped)
 * - Security event logging (tenant-scoped)
 */
@Injectable()
export class AuthService {
  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(User)
    private readonly userRepository: Repository<User>,
    @InjectRepository(Session)
    private readonly sessionRepository: Repository<Session>,
    @InjectRepository(SecurityEvent)
    private readonly securityEventRepository: Repository<SecurityEvent>,
    @InjectRepository(Tenant)
    private readonly tenantRepository: Repository<Tenant>,
    @InjectRepository(OAuthRefreshToken)
    private readonly oauthRefreshTokenRepository: Repository<OAuthRefreshToken>,
    private readonly jwtService: JwtService,
    private readonly configService: ConfigService,
    private readonly passwordService: PasswordService,
    private readonly emailService: EmailService,
    private readonly sessionService: SessionService,
    private readonly bruteForceService: BruteForceService,
    private readonly mfaService: MfaService,
    private readonly webauthnService: WebAuthnService,
  ) {}

  /**
   * Register a new user (TENANT REQUIRED)
   */
  async register(data: {
    tenantId: string; // ← Now REQUIRED!
    email: string;
    password: string;
    firstName?: string;
    lastName?: string;
    ip: string;
    userAgent: string;
  }) {
    // Validate tenant exists and is active
    const tenant = await this.tenantRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: data.tenantId, active: true },
    });

    if (!tenant) {
      throw new BadRequestException("Invalid or inactive tenant");
    }

    // Validate password strength
    const validation = PasswordService.validateStrength(data.password);

    if (!validation.isValid) {
      throw new BadRequestException({
        message: "Password does not meet security requirements",
        errors: validation.errors,
        score: validation.score,
      });
    }

    // Check if user already exists IN THIS TENANT
    const existingUser = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        email: data.email,
        tenantId: data.tenantId, // ← Tenant-scoped lookup
      },
    });

    if (existingUser) {
      // Log failed registration attempt (tenant-scoped)
      await this.logSecurityEvent({
        tenantId: data.tenantId,
        eventType: "REGISTRATION_FAILED",
        severity: "warning",
        ipAddress: data.ip,
        userAgent: data.userAgent,
        metadata: { reason: "email_exists", email: data.email },
      });

      throw new ConflictException(
        "User with this email already exists in this tenant",
      );
    }

    // Hash password with Argon2id
    const passwordHash = await PasswordService.hash(data.password);

    // Generate email verification token
    const emailVerificationToken = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("hex");

    // Create user with tenantId
    const user = this.userRepository.create({
      tenantId: data.tenantId, // ← Always set tenant
      email: data.email,
      passwordHash,
      firstName: data.firstName,
      lastName: data.lastName,
      emailVerificationToken,
      emailVerificationExpiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000), // 24 hours
    });

    const savedUser = await this.userRepository.save(user);

    // Log successful registration (tenant-scoped)
    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      userId: savedUser.id,
      tenantId: savedUser.tenantId,
      eventType: "USER_CREATED",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: data.ip,
      userAgent: data.userAgent,
      metadata: { email: savedUser.email },
    });

    // Send welcome and verification email
    await this.emailService.sendWelcomeEmail(savedUser, emailVerificationToken);

    return {
      id: savedUser.id,
      email: savedUser.email,
      firstName: savedUser.firstName,
      lastName: savedUser.lastName,
      message:
        "Registration successful. Please check your email to verify your account.",
    };
  }

  /**
   * Login with email and password (TENANT REQUIRED)
   */
  async login(data: {
    tenantId: string;
    email: string;
    password: string;
    ip: string;
    userAgent: string;
    deviceId?: string;
  }): Promise<
    | { accessToken: string; refreshToken: string; user: any }
    | {
        mfaRequired: boolean;
        tempToken: string;
        message: string;
        availableMethods: string[];
        preferredMethod: string;
      }
  > {
    const { tenantId, email, password, ip, userAgent } = data;

    // Find user
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: { email, tenantId },
      relations: ["tenant"],
      select: {
        id: true,
        email: true,
        passwordHash: true, // ← Force select this hidden field
        firstName: true,
        lastName: true,
        emailVerified: true,
        tenantId: true,
        createdAt: true,
        updatedAt: true,
      },
    });

    console.log("🔍 Login attempt:", { email, tenantId, userFound: !!user }); // ← ADD

    if (!user) {
      await this.logSecurityEvent({
        tenantId,
        eventType: "login_failed",
        severity: "critical",
        ipAddress: ip,
        userAgent,
        metadata: { email, reason: "user_not_found" },
      });
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid email or password");
    }

    await this.bruteForceService.checkAccountLock(user);

    console.log("🔍 User found:", {
      userId: user.id,
      user,
      hasPassword: !!user.passwordHash,
    }); // ← ADD

    // Verify password
    const isValid = await PasswordService.verify(user.passwordHash, password);

    console.log("🔍 Password check:", { isValid }); // ← ADD

    if (!isValid) {
      await this.bruteForceService.recordFailedAttempt(user, tenantId, ip);
      await this.logSecurityEvent({
        tenantId,
        userId: user.id,
        eventType: "login_failed",
        severity: "critical",
        ipAddress: ip,
        userAgent,
        metadata: { email, reason: "invalid_password", password },
      });
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid email or password");
    }

    await this.bruteForceService.resetFailedAttempts(user);

    // Check if email verified (if required)
    if (
      !user.emailVerified &&
      user.tenant.featureFlags?.emailVerificationRequired
    ) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Please verify your email first");
    }

    // Check if account is active
    if (
      !user.emailVerified &&
      user.tenant.featureFlags?.emailVerificationRequired
    ) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Please verify your email first");
    }

    const mfaStatus = await this.mfaService.getMfaStatus(user.id, tenantId);

    if (mfaStatus.enabled) {
      // Generate temporary token for MFA verification
      const tempToken = this.jwtService.sign(
        {
          sub: user.id,
          email: user.email,
          tenantId,
          type: "mfa_pending",
        },
        {
          secret: this.configService.get("JWT_SECRET"),
          expiresIn: "5m", // Short-lived
        },
      );

      console.log(`🔐 MFA required for user ${user.email}`);

      // Log MFA challenge
      await this.logSecurityEvent({
        tenantId,
        userId: user.id,
        eventType: "mfa_challenge_sent",
        severity: "info",
        ipAddress: ip,
        userAgent,
      });

      return {
        mfaRequired: true,
        tempToken,
        availableMethods: mfaStatus.methods,
        preferredMethod: mfaStatus.preferredMethod || "totp",
        message: "MFA verification required",
      };
    }

    // === REFRESH TOKEN ROTATION LOGIC ===

    const session = await this.sessionService.createSession({
      userId: user.id,
      tenantId: data.tenantId,
      email: user.email,
      ipAddress: data.ip,
      userAgent: data.userAgent,
      deviceId: data.deviceId,
    });

    console.log("Session Created: ", session.id);

    // 1. Generate new family ID
    const familyId = uuidv4();

    // 2. Generate refresh token (opaque random string)
    const refreshToken = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("hex");

    // 3. Hash it before storing
    const refreshTokenHash = crypto
      .createHash("sha256")
      .update(refreshToken)
      .digest("hex");

    // Step 4: Store refresh token separately
    await this.oauthRefreshTokenRepository.save({
      tenantId: data.tenantId,
      userId: user.id,
      sessionId: session.id,
      tokenHash: refreshTokenHash,
      familyId,
      expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000), // 30 days
    });

    // Step 5: Update session with family ID
    await this.sessionRepository.update(session.id, {
      refreshTokenFamilyId: familyId,
      refreshTokenHash,
    });

    // 6. Generate access token
    const accessToken = this.jwtService.sign(
      {
        sub: user.id,
        email: user.email,
        tenantId,
        sessionId: session.id,
      },
      {
        secret: this.configService.get("JWT_SECRET"),
        expiresIn: "15m",
      },
    );

    // Log successful login
    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      tenantId,
      userId: user.id,
      eventType: "login_success",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: ip,
      userAgent,
      metadata: { sessionId: session.id, familyId },
    });

    return {
      accessToken,
      refreshToken, // ← Return plaintext token to user
      user: {
        // id: user.id,
        email: user.email,
        firstName: user.firstName,
        lastName: user.lastName,
        emailVerified: user.emailVerified,
        // tenant: {
        //   id: user.tenant.id,
        //   name: user.tenant.name,
        //   slug: user.tenant.slug,
        // },
      },
    };
  }

  /**
   * Logout - revoke current session (TENANT SCOPED)
   */
  async logout(
    tenantId: string,
    userId: string,
    sessionId?: string,
    userAgent?: string,
    ip?: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    if (sessionId) {
      await this.sessionService.revokeSession(tenantId, sessionId);
    } else {
      await this.sessionService.revokeAllUserSessions(tenantId, userId);
    }
    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      tenantId,
      userId,
      ip,
      userAgent,
      eventType: "logout",
      severity: "info",
      metadata: { sessionId },
    } as any);
  }

  /**
   * Request password reset (TENANT SCOPED)
   */
  async forgotPassword(tenantId: string, email: string, ip: string) {
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        email,
        tenantId, // ← Tenant filter
      },
    });

    // Don't reveal if user exists or not (security best practice)
    if (!user) {
      return;
    }

    // Generate reset token
    const resetToken = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
    const resetTokenHash = crypto
      .createHash("sha256")
      .update(resetToken)
      .digest("hex");

    await this.userRepository.update(
      { id: user.id, tenantId }, // ← Tenant-scoped update
      {
        passwordResetToken: resetTokenHash,
        passwordResetExpiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 60 * 60 * 1000), // 1 hour
      },
    );

    // Log password reset request
    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      userId: user.id,
      tenantId: user.tenantId,
      eventType: "PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: ip,
      userAgent: "system",
    });

    // Send password reset email
    await this.emailService.sendPasswordResetEmail(user, resetToken);

    console.log("Password reset token:", resetToken); // For development
  }

  /**
   * Reset password with token (TENANT SCOPED)
   */
  async resetPassword(tenantId: string, token: string, newPassword: string) {
    const tokenHash = crypto.createHash("sha256").update(token).digest("hex");

    const user = await this.userRepository
      .createQueryBuilder("user")
      .addSelect("user.passwordResetToken")
      .addSelect("user.passwordHash")
      .addSelect("user.passwordHistory")
      .where("user.passwordResetToken = :tokenHash", { tokenHash })
      .andWhere("user.tenantId = :tenantId", { tenantId }) // ← Tenant filter
      .andWhere("user.passwordResetExpiresAt > :now", { now: new Date() })
      .getOne();

    if (!user) {
      throw new BadRequestException("Invalid or expired password reset token");
    }

    // Validate new password
    const validation = PasswordService.validateStrength(newPassword);
    if (!validation.isValid) {
      throw new BadRequestException({
        message: "Password does not meet security requirements",
        errors: validation.errors,
      });
    }

    // Check password history
    const passwordHistory = user.passwordHistory || [];
    for (const oldHash of passwordHistory) {
      const isReused = await PasswordService.verify(oldHash, newPassword);
      if (isReused) {
        throw new BadRequestException(
          "Password has been used recently. Please choose a different password.",
        );
      }
    }

    // Hash new password
    const newPasswordHash = await PasswordService.hash(newPassword);

    // Update password and history
    const updatedHistory = [user.passwordHash, ...passwordHistory].slice(0, 5);

    await this.userRepository.update(
      { id: user.id, tenantId }, // ← Tenant-scoped update
      {
        passwordHash: newPasswordHash,
        passwordHistory: updatedHistory,
        passwordResetToken: undefined,
        passwordResetExpiresAt: undefined,
      },
    );

    // Log password reset
    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      userId: user.id,
      tenantId: user.tenantId,
      eventType: "PASSWORD_RESET_COMPLETED",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
      userAgent: "system",
    });

    // Send password changed confirmation email
    await this.emailService.sendPasswordChangedEmail(user);

    // Revoke all sessions IN THIS TENANT (force re-login)
    await this.sessionRepository.update(
      {
        userId: user.id,
        tenantId, // ← Tenant filter
        revokedAt: IsNull(),
      },
      {
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: "password_reset",
      },
    );
  }

  /**
   * Verify email address (TENANT SCOPED)
   */
  async verifyEmail(tenantId: string, token: string) {
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        tenantId, // ← Tenant filter
        emailVerificationToken: token,
        emailVerificationExpiresAt: MoreThan(new Date()),
      },
    });

    if (!user) {
      throw new BadRequestException("Invalid or expired verification token");
    }

    await this.userRepository.update(
      { id: user.id, tenantId }, // ← Tenant-scoped update
      {
        emailVerified: true,
        emailVerificationToken: undefined,
        emailVerificationExpiresAt: undefined,
      },
    );

    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      userId: user.id,
      tenantId: user.tenantId,
      eventType: "EMAIL_VERIFIED",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
      userAgent: "system",
    });
  }

  /**
   * Resend verification email (TENANT SCOPED)
   */
  async resendVerification(tenantId: string, email: string) {
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        email,
        tenantId, // ← Tenant filter
      },
    });

    if (!user) {
      // Don't reveal if user exists
      return;
    }

    if (user.emailVerified) {
      throw new BadRequestException("Email is already verified");
    }

    // Generate new token
    const emailVerificationToken = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("hex");

    await this.userRepository.update(
      { id: user.id, tenantId }, // ← Tenant-scoped update
      {
        emailVerificationToken,
        emailVerificationExpiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000),
      },
    );

    // Send verification email
    await this.emailService.sendVerificationEmail(user, emailVerificationToken);

    console.log("Verification token:", emailVerificationToken); // For development
  }

  /**
   * Refresh access token (TENANT SCOPED)
   */
  async refreshToken(
    tenantId: string,
    refreshToken: string,
  ): Promise<{ accessToken: string; refreshToken: string }> {
    // 1. Hash the incoming token
    const tokenHash = crypto
      .createHash("sha256")
      .update(refreshToken)
      .digest("hex");

    // 2. Find session by hash
    const session = await this.sessionRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        tenantId,
        refreshTokenHash: tokenHash,
        revokedAt: IsNull(),
      },
      relations: ["user"],
    });

    if (!session) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid refresh token");
    }

    // 3. Check if session expired
    if (session.expiresAt && session.expiresAt < new Date()) {
      await this.sessionRepository.update(session.id, {
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: "expired",
      });
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Session expired");
    }

    // === REUSE DETECTION ===
    // 4. Check if this token was already used
    if (session.lastRefreshAt) {
      // TOKEN REUSE DETECTED!
      // This token was already exchanged for a new one
      // Someone is replaying an old token → security breach

      console.error("🚨 REFRESH TOKEN REUSE DETECTED", {
        sessionId: session.id,
        familyId: session.refreshTokenFamilyId,
        userId: session.userId,
        tenantId: session.tenantId,
      });

      // Revoke entire family
      await this.revokeSessionFamily(session.refreshTokenFamilyId!);

      // Log critical security event
      await this.logSecurityEvent({
        tenantId,
        userId: session.userId,
        eventType: "refresh_token_reuse_detected",
        severity: "critical",
        ipAddress: session.ipAddress,
        userAgent: session.userAgent,
        metadata: {
          sessionId: session.id,
          familyId: session.refreshTokenFamilyId,
        },
      });

      throw new UnauthorizedException(
        "Token reuse detected - all sessions revoked",
      );
    }

    // === ROTATION ===
    // 5. Generate NEW refresh token
    const newRefreshToken = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
    const newRefreshTokenHash = crypto
      .createHash("sha256")
      .update(newRefreshToken)
      .digest("hex");

    await this.sessionService.updateRefreshToken(
      tenantId,
      session.id,
      newRefreshTokenHash,
    );

    // 7. Generate new access token
    const accessToken = this.jwtService.sign(
      {
        sub: session.userId,
        email: session.user.email,
        tenantId: session.tenantId,
        sessionId: session.id,
      },
      {
        secret: this.configService.get("JWT_SECRET"),
        expiresIn: "15m",
      },
    );

    // Log token refresh
    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      tenantId,
      userId: session.userId,
      eventType: "token_refreshed",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: session.ipAddress,
      userAgent: session.userAgent,
      metadata: { sessionId: session.id },
    });

    return {
      accessToken,
      refreshToken: newRefreshToken, // ← Return NEW token
    };
  }

  /**
   * Get current user (TENANT SCOPED)
   */
  async getCurrentUser(tenantId: string, userId: string) {
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        id: userId,
        tenantId, // ← Tenant filter
      },
      relations: ["tenant"],
    });

    if (!user) {
      throw new NotFoundException("User not found");
    }

    return {
      id: user.id,
      email: user.email,
      firstName: user.firstName,
      lastName: user.lastName,
      displayName: user.displayName,
      emailVerified: user.emailVerified,
      phoneVerified: user.phoneVerified,
      lastLoginAt: user.lastLoginAt,
      lastLoginIp: user.lastLoginIp,
      tenant: {
        id: user.tenant.id,
        name: user.tenant.name,
        slug: user.tenant.slug, // ← Use 'slug' not 'subdomain'
      },
    };
  }

  /**
   * Create session (TENANT SCOPED) - PRIVATE METHOD
   */
  private async createSession(data: {
    tenantId: string;
    userId: string;
    ipAddress?: string;
    userAgent?: string;
  }): Promise<Session> {
    const refreshTokenFamilyId = crypto.randomUUID();
    const deviceId = crypto.randomBytes(16).toString("hex");

    const session = this.sessionRepository.create({
      tenantId: data.tenantId, // ← Always set tenant
      userId: data.userId,
      refreshTokenFamilyId,
      deviceId,
      ipAddress: data.ipAddress,
      userAgent: data.userAgent,
      expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000), // 7 days
    });

    return this.sessionRepository.save(session);
  }

  /**
   * Increment failed login attempts (TENANT SCOPED) - PRIVATE METHOD
   */
  private async incrementFailedAttempts(tenantId: string, userId: string) {
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        id: userId,
        tenantId, // ← Tenant filter
      },
    });

    if (!user) return;

    const newAttempts = user.failedLoginAttempts + 1;

    // Lock account after 5 failed attempts
    const update: any = {
      failedLoginAttempts: newAttempts,
    };

    if (newAttempts >= 5) {
      update.lockedUntil = new Date(Date.now() + 15 * 60 * 1000); // 15 minutes
    }

    await this.userRepository.update(
      { id: userId, tenantId }, // ← Tenant-scoped update
      update,
    );
  }

  /**
   * Revoke all sessions in a token family
   * Used when token reuse is detected
   */
  private async revokeSessionFamily(familyId: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.sessionRepository.update(
      {
        refreshTokenFamilyId: familyId,
        revokedAt: IsNull(),
      },
      {
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: "token_reuse_detected",
      },
    );

    console.log(`🔒 Revoked all sessions in family: ${familyId}`);
  }

  /**
   * Verify MFA code and complete login
   */
  async verifyMfa(
    tenantId: string,
    tempToken: string,
    code: string,
    ip: string,
    userAgent: string,
  ) {
    // Step 1: Verify temporary token
    const payload = this.jwtService.verify(tempToken, {
      secret: this.configService.get("JWT_SECRET"),
    });

    if (payload.type !== "mfa_pending") {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid token type");
    }

    console.log(`🔐 Verifying MFA for user ${payload.email}`);

    // Step 2: Verify TOTP code
    const verified = await this.mfaService.verifyTotp(
      payload.sub,
      tenantId,
      code,
    );

    if (!verified) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid MFA code");
    }

    // Step 3: Generate real session and tokens
    const familyId = uuidv4();
    const refreshToken = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
    const refreshTokenHash = crypto
      .createHash("sha256")
      .update(refreshToken)
      .digest("hex");

    const session = await this.sessionService.createSession({
      userId: payload.sub,
      tenantId,
      email: payload.email,
      refreshTokenFamilyId: familyId,
      refreshTokenHash,
      ipAddress: ip,
      userAgent: userAgent || "unknown",
    });

    const accessToken = this.jwtService.sign(
      {
        sub: payload.sub,
        email: payload.email,
        tenantId,
        sessionId: session.id,
      },
      {
        secret: this.configService.get("JWT_SECRET"),
        expiresIn: "15m",
      },
    );

    console.log(`✅ MFA verified successfully for ${payload.email}`);

    return {
      accessToken,
      refreshToken,
      message: "MFA verified successfully",
    };
  }

  /**
   * Verify MFA using backup code
   */
  async verifyMfaBackup(
    tenantId: string,
    tempToken: string,
    code: string,
    ip: string,
    userAgent: string,
  ) {
    // Step 1: Verify temporary token
    const payload = this.jwtService.verify(tempToken, {
      secret: this.configService.get("JWT_SECRET"),
    });

    if (payload.type !== "mfa_pending") {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid token type");
    }

    console.log(`🔐 Verifying backup code for user ${payload.email}`);

    // Step 2: Verify backup code
    const verified = await this.mfaService.verifyBackupCode(
      payload.sub,
      tenantId,
      code,
    );

    if (!verified) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid backup code");
    }

    // Step 3: Generate real session and tokens
    const familyId = uuidv4();
    const refreshToken = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
    const refreshTokenHash = crypto
      .createHash("sha256")
      .update(refreshToken)
      .digest("hex");

    const session = await this.sessionService.createSession({
      userId: payload.sub,
      tenantId,
      email: payload.email,
      refreshTokenFamilyId: familyId,
      refreshTokenHash,
      ipAddress: ip,
      userAgent: userAgent || "unknown",
    });

    const accessToken = this.jwtService.sign(
      {
        sub: payload.sub,
        email: payload.email,
        tenantId,
        sessionId: session.id,
      },
      {
        secret: this.configService.get("JWT_SECRET"),
        expiresIn: "15m",
      },
    );

    console.log(`✅ Backup code verified for ${payload.email}`);

    // Check remaining backup codes
    const mfaStatus = await this.mfaService.getMfaStatus(payload.sub, tenantId);
    const remaining = mfaStatus.backupCodesRemaining as number;

    return {
      accessToken,
      refreshToken,
      message: "Backup code verified successfully",
      warning:
        remaining <= 2
          ? `⚠️ Only ${remaining} backup codes remaining. Please regenerate new codes.`
          : undefined,
    };
  }

  // apps/auth-service/src/modules/auth/auth.service.ts
  // ADD THESE METHODS TO AuthService

  /**
   * Start WebAuthn authentication (passwordless login)
   */
  async startWebAuthnLogin(tenantId: string, email: string): Promise<any> {
    // Find user
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: { email, tenantId },
    });

    if (!user) {
      // Don't reveal if user exists
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid credentials");
    }

    // Generate authentication options via WebAuthnService
    const options = await this.webauthnService.startAuthentication(
      user.id,
      tenantId,
    );

    console.log(`🔑 WebAuthn login started for ${email}`);

    return options;
  }

  /**
   * Finish WebAuthn authentication and create session
   */
  async finishWebAuthnLogin(data: {
    tenantId: string;
    email: string;
    webauthnResponse: any;
    ip: string;
    userAgent: string;
    deviceId?: string;
  }): Promise<{ accessToken: string; refreshToken: string; user: any }> {
    const { tenantId, email, webauthnResponse, ip, userAgent, deviceId } = data;

    // Find user
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: { email, tenantId },
      relations: ["tenant"],
    });

    if (!user) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid credentials");
    }

    // Verify WebAuthn response
    const verified = await this.webauthnService.finishAuthentication(
      user.id,
      tenantId,
      webauthnResponse,
    );

    if (!verified) {
      await this.logSecurityEvent({
        tenantId,
        userId: user.id,
        eventType: "login_failed",
        severity: "warning",
        ipAddress: ip,
        userAgent,
        metadata: { email, reason: "webauthn_verification_failed" },
      });

      throw new UnauthorizedException("WebAuthn verification failed");
    }

    // Check if account is active
    if (!user.isActive) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Account is disabled");
    }

    // Generate tokens (same as normal login)
    const familyId = uuidv4();
    const refreshToken = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
    const refreshTokenHash = crypto
      .createHash("sha256")
      .update(refreshToken)
      .digest("hex");

    const session = await this.sessionService.createSession({
      userId: user.id,
      tenantId,
      email: user.email,
      refreshTokenFamilyId: familyId,
      refreshTokenHash,
      ipAddress: ip,
      userAgent,
      deviceId,
    });

    const accessToken = this.jwtService.sign(
      {
        sub: user.id,
        email: user.email,
        tenantId,
        sessionId: session.id,
      },
      {
        secret: this.configService.get("JWT_SECRET"),
        expiresIn: "15m",
      },
    );

    // Log successful login
    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      tenantId,
      userId: user.id,
      eventType: "login_success",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: ip,
      userAgent,
      metadata: {
        sessionId: session.id,
        familyId,
        authMethod: "webauthn",
      },
    });

    console.log(`✅ WebAuthn login successful for ${user.email}`);

    return {
      accessToken,
      refreshToken,
      user: {
        email: user.email,
        firstName: user.firstName,
        lastName: user.lastName,
        emailVerified: user.emailVerified,
      },
    };
  }

  /**
   * Log security event (TENANT SCOPED) - PRIVATE METHOD
   */
  private async logSecurityEvent(data: {
    userId?: string;
    tenantId: string; // ← Now REQUIRED!
    eventType: string;
    severity: "info" | "warning" | "critical";
    ipAddress: string;
    ip?: string;
    userAgent: string;
    metadata?: Record<string, any>;
  }) {
    await this.securityEventRepository.save({
      tenantId: data.tenantId, // ← Always set tenant
      userId: data.userId,
      eventType: data.eventType,
      severity: data.severity,
      ipAddress: data.ipAddress || data.ip,
      userAgent: data.userAgent,
      metadata: data.metadata || {},
    });
  }
}

import { Injectable, UnauthorizedException } from "@nestjs/common";
import { InjectRepository } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { IsNull, Repository } from "typeorm";
import { User } from "@repo/database";

@Injectable()
export class BruteForceService {
  private readonly MAX_ATTEMPTS = 5;
  private readonly LOCKOUT_DURATION = 15 * 60 * 1000; // 15 minutes in ms
  private readonly PROGRESSIVE_DELAYS = [0, 1000, 2000, 5000, 10000]; // Delays in ms

  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(User)
    private userRepository: Repository<User>,
  ) {}

  /**
   * Check if account is currently locked
   * @throws UnauthorizedException if account is locked
   */
  async checkAccountLock(user: User): Promise<void> {
    if (user.lockedUntil && user.lockedUntil > new Date()) {
      const minutesLeft = Math.ceil(
        (user.lockedUntil.getTime() - Date.now()) / 60000,
      );

      console.log(
        `🔒 Account locked for user ${user.email} - ${minutesLeft} minutes remaining`,
      );

      throw new UnauthorizedException({
        message: `Account temporarily locked due to too many failed login attempts`,
        lockedUntil: user.lockedUntil,
        minutesRemaining: minutesLeft,
        statusCode: 423, // 423 Locked
      });
    }

    // Clear lock if expired
    if (user.lockedUntil && user.lockedUntil <= new Date()) {
      user.lockedUntil = undefined;
      user.failedLoginAttempts = 0;
      await this.userRepository.save(user);
      console.log(`🔓 Lock expired for user ${user.email}`);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Record a failed login attempt
   * Implements progressive delays and account lockout
   */
  async recordFailedAttempt(
    user: User,
    tenantId: string,
    ipAddress?: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    user.failedLoginAttempts = (user.failedLoginAttempts || 0) + 1;
    user.lastFailedLogin = new Date();

    console.log(
      `❌ Failed login attempt ${user.failedLoginAttempts}/${this.MAX_ATTEMPTS} for ${user.email}`,
    );

    // Lock account after max attempts
    if (user.failedLoginAttempts >= this.MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
      user.lockedUntil = new Date(Date.now() + this.LOCKOUT_DURATION);
      await this.userRepository.save(user);

      console.log(
        `🔒 Account locked for user ${user.email} until ${user.lockedUntil}`,
      );

      // TODO: Send email notification about account lock
      // await this.emailService.sendAccountLockedEmail(user);

      throw new UnauthorizedException({
        message: `Account locked due to too many failed login attempts. Try again in 15 minutes.`,
        lockedUntil: user.lockedUntil,
        statusCode: 423,
      });
    }

    // Save failed attempt count
    await this.userRepository.save(user);

    // Apply progressive delay (prevents brute force timing attacks)
    const delayIndex = Math.min(
      user.failedLoginAttempts - 1,
      this.PROGRESSIVE_DELAYS.length - 1,
    );
    const delay = this.PROGRESSIVE_DELAYS[delayIndex] as number;

    if (delay > 0) {
      console.log(`⏱️  Applying ${delay}ms delay for failed attempt`);
      await this.sleep(delay);
    }

    // Throw error after delay
    const attemptsLeft = this.MAX_ATTEMPTS - user.failedLoginAttempts;
    throw new UnauthorizedException({
      message: "Invalid email or password",
      attemptsRemaining: attemptsLeft,
    });
  }

  /**
   * Reset failed login attempts on successful login
   */
  async resetFailedAttempts(user: User): Promise<void> {
    if (user.failedLoginAttempts > 0 || user.lockedUntil) {
      console.log(`✅ Resetting failed attempts for ${user.email}`);

      user.failedLoginAttempts = 0;
      user.lockedUntil = null;
      user.lastFailedLogin = null;

      await this.userRepository.save(user);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Manually unlock account (admin function)
   */
  async unlockAccount(userId: string, tenantId: string): Promise<void> {
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: userId, tenantId },
    });

    if (!user) {
      throw new Error("User not found");
    }

    user.lockedUntil = null;
    user.failedLoginAttempts = 0;
    user.lastFailedLogin = null;

    await this.userRepository.save(user);

    console.log(`🔓 Account manually unlocked for ${user.email}`);
  }

  /**
   * Get account status
   */
  async getAccountStatus(userId: string, tenantId: string) {
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: userId, tenantId },
      select: [
        "id",
        "email",
        "failedLoginAttempts",
        "lockedUntil",
        "lastFailedLogin",
      ],
    });

    if (!user) {
      throw new Error("User not found");
    }

    const isLocked = user.lockedUntil && user.lockedUntil > new Date();
    const minutesUntilUnlock = isLocked
      ? Math.ceil((user.lockedUntil!.getTime() - Date.now()) / 60000)
      : 0;

    return {
      isLocked,
      failedAttempts: user.failedLoginAttempts || 0,
      maxAttempts: this.MAX_ATTEMPTS,
      attemptsRemaining: Math.max(
        0,
        this.MAX_ATTEMPTS - (user.failedLoginAttempts || 0),
      ),
      lockedUntil: user.lockedUntil,
      minutesUntilUnlock,
      lastFailedLogin: user.lastFailedLogin,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Helper: Sleep for specified milliseconds
   */
  private sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
  }
}

/**
 * Email Controller
 *
 * Test endpoint for development/testing email functionality.
 * Only works in development mode.
 */

import { Controller, Get, Query, BadRequestException } from "@nestjs/common";
import { ConfigService } from "@nestjs/config";
import { ApiTags, ApiOperation, ApiQuery } from "@nestjs/swagger";
import { EmailService } from "./email.service";

@ApiTags("Email (Development)")
@Controller("email")
export class EmailController {
  constructor(
    private readonly emailService: EmailService,
    private readonly configService: ConfigService,
  ) {}

  /**
   * Test email sending (development only)
   */
  @Get("test")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Test email sending (development only)" })
  @ApiQuery({ name: "type", required: true, description: "Email type to test" })
  @ApiQuery({ name: "to", required: true, description: "Recipient email" })
  async testEmail(
    @Query("type") type: string,
    @Query("to") to: string,
  ): Promise<any> {
    // Only allow in development
    const nodeEnv = this.configService.get<string>("NODE_ENV");
    if (nodeEnv === "production") {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        "Email testing is not available in production",
      );
    }

    // Mock user data for testing
    const mockUser = {
      id: "test-user-id",
      tenantId: "test-tenant-id",
      email: to,
      firstName: "Test",
      lastName: "User",
      emailVerified: false,
      passwordHash: "",
      createdAt: new Date(),
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    } as any;

    let result: boolean;

    switch (type) {
      case "welcome":
        result = await this.emailService.sendWelcomeEmail(
          mockUser,
          "test-verification-token-123",
        );
        break;

      case "verification":
        result = await this.emailService.sendVerificationEmail(
          mockUser,
          "test-verification-token-123",
        );
        break;

      case "password-reset":
        result = await this.emailService.sendPasswordResetEmail(
          mockUser,
          "test-reset-token-123",
        );
        break;

      case "password-changed":
        result = await this.emailService.sendPasswordChangedEmail(mockUser);
        break;

      case "magic-link":
        result = await this.emailService.sendMagicLinkEmail(
          mockUser,
          "test-magic-link-token-123",
        );
        break;

      case "otp":
        result = await this.emailService.sendOTPEmail(mockUser, "123456");
        break;

      case "account-deleted":
        result = await this.emailService.sendAccountDeletedEmail(mockUser);
        break;

      default:
        throw new BadRequestException(
          `Invalid email type. Valid types: welcome, verification, password-reset, password-changed, magic-link, otp, account-deleted`,
        );
    }

    return {
      success: result,
      type,
      recipient: to,
      message: result
        ? "Email sent successfully (check console if in development mode)"
        : "Email failed to send",
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Email Service
 *
 * Main email service that:
 * - Auto-detects which provider to use (SES > SMTP > Console)
 * - Handles email templating with Handlebars
 * - Logs all email attempts to security_events
 * - Supports multi-tenant branding
 */

import { Injectable, Logger } from "@nestjs/common";
import { ConfigService } from "@nestjs/config";
import { InjectRepository } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { Repository } from "typeorm";
import { User, Tenant, SecurityEvent } from "@repo/database";
import * as Handlebars from "handlebars";
import * as fs from "fs";
import * as path from "path";
import { EmailProvider } from "./providers/email-provider.interface";
import { NodeMailerProvider } from "./providers/nodemailer.provider";
import { SESProvider } from "./providers/ses.provider";

@Injectable()
export class EmailService {
  private readonly logger = new Logger(EmailService.name);
  private provider: EmailProvider | null = null;
  private templateCache: Map<string, HandlebarsTemplateDelegate> = new Map();

  constructor(
    private readonly configService: ConfigService,
    @InjectRepository(SecurityEvent)
    private readonly securityEventRepository: Repository<SecurityEvent>,
    @InjectRepository(Tenant)
    private readonly tenantRepository: Repository<Tenant>,
  ) {
    this.initializeProvider();
  }

  /**
   * Auto-detect and initialize email provider
   * Priority: SES > SMTP > Console (development)
   */
  private initializeProvider(): void {
    const awsSesAccessKey = this.configService.get<string>(
      "AWS_SES_ACCESS_KEY_ID",
    );
    const awsSesSecretKey = this.configService.get<string>(
      "AWS_SES_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY",
    );
    const smtpHost = this.configService.get<string>("SMTP_HOST");

    if (awsSesAccessKey && awsSesSecretKey) {
      // Use SES
      this.provider = new SESProvider(this.configService);
      this.logger.log("📧 Email provider: AWS SES");
    } else if (smtpHost) {
      // Use SMTP (NodeMailer)
      this.provider = new NodeMailerProvider(this.configService);
      this.logger.log("📧 Email provider: SMTP (NodeMailer)");
    } else {
      // Development mode - log to console
      this.provider = null;
      this.logger.warn(
        "📧 Email provider: CONSOLE (Development) - No email provider configured",
      );
    }
  }

  /**
   * Send email using the configured provider
   */
  async sendEmail(options: {
    to: string;
    subject: string;
    html: string;
    text?: string;
    tenantId: string;
    userId?: string;
    emailType: string;
  }): Promise<boolean> {
    const startTime = Date.now();
    let success = false;

    console.log(this.provider);

    try {
      if (this.provider) {
        // Use configured provider (SES or SMTP)
        await this.provider.sendEmail({
          to: options.to,
          subject: options.subject,
          html: options.html,
          text: options.text,
        });
        success = true;
        this.logger.log(
          `✅ Email sent to ${options.to} via ${this.provider.getProviderName()}`,
        );
      } else {
        // Development mode - log to console
        console.log("\n========== EMAIL (DEVELOPMENT MODE) ==========");
        console.log(`To: ${options.to}`);
        console.log(`Subject: ${options.subject}`);
        console.log(`Type: ${options.emailType}`);
        console.log(`HTML Length: ${options.html.length} chars`);
        console.log("==============================================\n");
        success = true;
      }
    } catch (error: any) {
      this.logger.error(
        `❌ Failed to send email to ${options.to}: ${error.message}`,
        error.stack,
      );
      success = false;
    }

    // Log to security events
    await this.logEmailEvent({
      tenantId: options.tenantId,
      userId: options.userId,
      emailType: options.emailType,
      recipient: options.to,
      provider: this.provider?.getProviderName() || "console",
      success,
      duration: Date.now() - startTime,
    });

    return success;
  }

  /**
   * Load and compile template
   */
  private async loadTemplate(
    templateName: string,
  ): Promise<HandlebarsTemplateDelegate> {
    // Check cache first
    if (this.templateCache.has(templateName)) {
      return this.templateCache.get(templateName)!;
    }

    const templatePath = path.join(
      __dirname,
      "../../../",
      "templates",
      `${templateName}.html`,
    );

    try {
      const templateContent = fs.readFileSync(templatePath, "utf-8");
      const compiledTemplate = Handlebars.compile(templateContent);
      this.templateCache.set(templateName, compiledTemplate);
      return compiledTemplate;
    } catch (error: any) {
      this.logger.error(
        `Failed to load template ${templateName}: ${error.message}`,
      );
      throw new Error(`Template ${templateName} not found`);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Render template with data
   */
  private async renderTemplate(
    templateName: string,
    data: Record<string, any>,
  ): Promise<string> {
    const template = await this.loadTemplate(templateName);
    return template({
      ...data,
      currentYear: new Date().getFullYear(),
    });
  }

  /**
   * Get tenant branding information
   */
  private async getTenantBranding(tenantId: string): Promise<{
    tenantName: string;
    tenantLogo: string;
  }> {
    try {
      const tenant = await this.tenantRepository.findOne({
        where: { id: tenantId },
      });

      return {
        tenantName: tenant?.name || "Fort Knox",
        tenantLogo:
          // TODO Tenant Logo URL
          // tenant?.metadata?.logoUrl ||
          "https://via.placeholder.com/150x50?text=Fort+Knox",
      };
    } catch (error) {
      this.logger.warn(
        `Failed to load tenant branding for ${tenantId}, using defaults`,
      );
      return {
        tenantName: "Fort Knox",
        tenantLogo: "https://via.placeholder.com/150x50?text=Fort+Knox",
      };
    }
  }

  /**
   * Send welcome email after registration
   */
  async sendWelcomeEmail(
    user: User,
    verificationToken: string,
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    const branding = await this.getTenantBranding(user.tenantId);
    const verificationUrl = `${this.configService.get<string>("FRONTEND_URL")}/verify-email?token=${verificationToken}`;

    const html = await this.renderTemplate("welcome", {
      userName: user.firstName || user.email.split("@")[0],
      userEmail: user.email,
      actionUrl: verificationUrl,
      expiresIn: "24 hours",
      ...branding,
    });

    return this.sendEmail({
      to: user.email,
      subject: `Welcome to ${branding.tenantName}!`,
      html,
      tenantId: user.tenantId,
      userId: user.id,
      emailType: "welcome",
    });
  }

  /**

  async sendVerificationEmail(

      emailType: "verification",
    });
  }

   */
  private async logEmailEvent(data: {
    tenantId: string;
    userId?: string;
    emailType: string;
    recipient: string;
    provider: string;
    success: boolean;
    duration: number;
  }): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await this.securityEventRepository.save({
        tenantId: data.tenantId,
        userId: data.userId,
        eventType: "email_sent",
        severity: data.success ? "info" : "warning",
        ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
        userAgent: "email-service",
        metadata: {
          emailType: data.emailType,
          recipient: data.recipient,
          provider: data.provider,
          success: data.success,
          duration: data.duration,
        },
      });
    } catch (error: any) {
      this.logger.error(
        `Failed to log email event: ${error.message}`,
        error.stack,
      );
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Email Provider Interface
 *
 * Abstract interface that all email providers must implement.
 * This allows easy switching between NodeMailer, SES, or other providers.
 */

export interface EmailOptions {
  to: string | string[];
  subject: string;
  html: string;
  text?: string;
  from?: string;
  replyTo?: string;
  cc?: string | string[];
  bcc?: string | string[];
  attachments?: Array<{
    filename: string;
    content?: string | Buffer;
    path?: string;
  }>;
}

export interface EmailProvider {
  /**
   * Send an email
   * @param options Email options
   * @returns Promise that resolves when email is sent
   */
  sendEmail(options: EmailOptions): Promise<void>;

  /**
   * Verify connection/credentials
   * @returns Promise that resolves to true if connection is valid
   */
  verify(): Promise<boolean>;

  /**
   * Get provider name
   * @returns Name of the provider (e.g., "smtp", "ses", "console")
   */
  getProviderName(): string;
}

/**
 * NodeMailer Email Provider
 *
 * SMTP-based email provider using NodeMailer.
 * Supports any SMTP server (Gmail, Outlook, custom servers, etc.)
 */

import { Injectable, Logger } from "@nestjs/common";
import { ConfigService } from "@nestjs/config";
import * as nodemailer from "nodemailer";
import { Transporter } from "nodemailer";
import { EmailProvider, EmailOptions } from "./email-provider.interface";

@Injectable()
export class NodeMailerProvider implements EmailProvider {
  private readonly logger = new Logger(NodeMailerProvider.name);
  private transporter: Transporter;

  constructor(private readonly configService: ConfigService) {
    this.initializeTransporter();
  }

  private initializeTransporter(): void {
    const smtpHost = this.configService.get<string>("SMTP_HOST");
    const smtpPort = this.configService.get<number>("SMTP_PORT", 587);
    const smtpSecure = this.configService.get<string>("SMTP_SECURE") === "true";
    const smtpUser = this.configService.get<string>("SMTP_USER");
    const smtpPass = this.configService.get<string>("SMTP_PASS");

    this.transporter = nodemailer.createTransport({
      host: smtpHost,
      port: smtpPort,
      secure: smtpSecure,
      auth: {
        user: smtpUser,
        pass: smtpPass,
      },
      pool: true, // Use connection pooling
      maxConnections: 5,
      maxMessages: 100,
    });

    this.logger.log(
      `NodeMailer initialized with SMTP server: ${smtpHost}:${smtpPort}`,
    );
  }

  async sendEmail(options: EmailOptions): Promise<void> {
    const from =
      options.from ||
      `"${this.configService.get<string>("EMAIL_FROM_NAME")}" <${this.configService.get<string>("EMAIL_FROM")}>`;

    const replyTo =
      options.replyTo || this.configService.get<string>("EMAIL_REPLY_TO");

    try {
      const info = await this.transporter.sendMail({
        from,
        to: Array.isArray(options.to) ? options.to.join(", ") : options.to,
        subject: options.subject,
        html: options.html,
        text: options.text,
        replyTo,
        cc: options.cc
          ? Array.isArray(options.cc)
            ? options.cc.join(", ")
            : options.cc
          : undefined,
        bcc: options.bcc
          ? Array.isArray(options.bcc)
            ? options.bcc.join(", ")
            : options.bcc
          : undefined,
        attachments: options.attachments,
      });

      this.logger.log(`Email sent successfully via SMTP: ${info.messageId}`);
    } catch (error: any) {
      this.logger.error(
        `Failed to send email via SMTP: ${error.message}`,
        error.stack,
      );
      throw error;
    }
  }

  async verify(): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      await this.transporter.verify();
      this.logger.log("SMTP connection verified successfully");
      return true;
    } catch (error: any) {
      this.logger.error(`SMTP verification failed: ${error.message}`);
      return false;
    }
  }

  getProviderName(): string {
    return "smtp";
  }
}

/**
 * AWS SES Email Provider
 *
 * Uses AWS Simple Email Service for sending emails.
 * More reliable and scalable than SMTP for production use.
 */

import { Injectable, Logger } from "@nestjs/common";
import { ConfigService } from "@nestjs/config";
import {
  SESClient,
  SendEmailCommand,
  VerifyEmailIdentityCommand,
} from "@aws-sdk/client-ses";
import { EmailProvider, EmailOptions } from "./email-provider.interface";

@Injectable()
export class SESProvider implements EmailProvider {
  private readonly logger = new Logger(SESProvider.name);
  private sesClient: SESClient;

  constructor(private readonly configService: ConfigService) {
    this.initializeSESClient();
  }

  private initializeSESClient(): void {
    const region = this.configService.get<string>(
      "AWS_SES_REGION",
      "us-east-1",
    );
    const accessKeyId = this.configService.get<string>("AWS_SES_ACCESS_KEY_ID");
    const secretAccessKey = this.configService.get<string>(
      "AWS_SES_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY",
    );

    this.sesClient = new SESClient({
      region,
      credentials: {
        accessKeyId: accessKeyId!,
        secretAccessKey: secretAccessKey!,
      },
    });

    this.logger.log(`AWS SES initialized with region: ${region}`);
  }

  async sendEmail(options: EmailOptions): Promise<void> {
    const from =
      options.from ||
      `"${this.configService.get<string>("EMAIL_FROM_NAME")}" <${this.configService.get<string>("EMAIL_FROM")}>`;

    const replyTo =
      options.replyTo || this.configService.get<string>("EMAIL_REPLY_TO");

    try {
      const command = new SendEmailCommand({
        Source: from,
        Destination: {
          ToAddresses: Array.isArray(options.to) ? options.to : [options.to],
          CcAddresses: options.cc
            ? Array.isArray(options.cc)
              ? options.cc
              : [options.cc]
            : undefined,
          BccAddresses: options.bcc
            ? Array.isArray(options.bcc)
              ? options.bcc
              : [options.bcc]
            : undefined,
        },
        Message: {
          Subject: {
            Data: options.subject,
            Charset: "UTF-8",
          },
          Body: {
            Html: {
              Data: options.html,
              Charset: "UTF-8",
            },
            Text: options.text
              ? {
                  Data: options.text,
                  Charset: "UTF-8",
                }
              : undefined,
          },
        },
        ReplyToAddresses: replyTo ? [replyTo] : undefined,
      });

      const response = await this.sesClient.send(command);
      this.logger.log(`Email sent successfully via SES: ${response.MessageId}`);
    } catch (error: any) {
      this.logger.error(
        `Failed to send email via SES: ${error.message}`,
        error.stack,
      );
      throw error;
    }
  }

  async verify(): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      // SES doesn't have a direct verify method like SMTP
      // We just check if we can initialize the client
      // In production, you'd verify email addresses/domains separately
      this.logger.log(
        "SES connection assumed valid (no verification endpoint)",
      );
      return true;
    } catch (error: any) {
      this.logger.error(`SES verification failed: ${error.message}`);
      return false;
    }
  }

  getProviderName(): string {
    return "ses";
  }

  /**
   * Verify an email address with SES (must be done before sending)
   * @param email Email address to verify
   */
  async verifyEmailIdentity(email: string): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const command = new VerifyEmailIdentityCommand({
        EmailAddress: email,
      });
      await this.sesClient.send(command);
      this.logger.log(
        `Verification email sent to ${email}. Check inbox to confirm.`,
      );
    } catch (error: any) {
      this.logger.error(
        `Failed to verify email identity: ${error.message}`,
        error.stack,
      );
      throw error;
    }
  }
}

import { ApiProperty, ApiPropertyOptional } from "@nestjs/swagger";
import { IsString, IsNotEmpty, Length, Matches } from "class-validator";

// ============================================================

// ============================================================

  @ApiProperty({ description: "Backup codes (save these!)", type: [String] })
  backupCodes!: string[];
}

// ============================================================

export class VerifyTotpDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: "123456", description: "6-digit TOTP code" })
  @IsString()
  @IsNotEmpty()
  @Length(6, 6)
  @Matches(/^\d{6}$/, { message: "Token must be 6 digits" })
  token!: string;
}

  @ApiProperty({ example: "ABCD-1234", description: "Backup code" })
  @IsString()
  @IsNotEmpty()

    message: "Invalid backup code format",
  })

}

FILE: src/modules/mfa/email-otp.service.ts

import { Injectable, NotFoundException } from "@nestjs/common";
import { InjectRepository } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { Repository } from "typeorm";
import * as crypto from "crypto";
import { EmailService } from "@/modules/email/email.service";
import { UserService } from "@/modules/user/user.service";

interface OtpEntry {
  code: string;
  expiresAt: Date;
  attempts: number;
}

@Injectable()
export class EmailOtpService {
  // In-memory store (replace with Redis in production)
  private otpStore = new Map<string, OtpEntry>();

  constructor(
    private readonly emailService: EmailService,
    private readonly userService: UserService,
  ) {
    // Cleanup expired OTPs every 5 minutes
    setInterval(() => this.cleanupExpiredOtps(), 5 * 60 * 1000);
  }

  /**
   * Generate and send OTP via email
   */
  async sendOtp(userId: string, tenantId: string): Promise<void> {
    // Generate 6-digit OTP
    const code = this.generateOtpCode();
    const user = await this.userService.findById(tenantId, userId);

    if (!user) {
      throw new NotFoundException("User not found");
    }

    // Store OTP (expires in 10 minutes)
    const key = `${userId}:${tenantId}`;
    this.otpStore.set(key, {
      code: await this.hashCode(code),
      expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 10 * 60 * 1000),
      attempts: 0,
    });

    await this.emailService.sendOTPEmail(user, code);

    console.log(`📧 OTP sent to ${user.email} (expires in 10 minutes)`);
  }

  /**
   * Verify OTP code
   */
  async verifyOtp(
    userId: string,
    tenantId: string,
    code: string,
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    const key = `${userId}:${tenantId}`;
    const entry = this.otpStore.get(key);

    if (!entry) {
      console.log("❌ No OTP found for user");
      return false;
    }

    // Check expiration
    if (entry.expiresAt < new Date()) {
      console.log("❌ OTP expired");
      this.otpStore.delete(key);
      return false;
    }

    // Check attempts (max 5)
    if (entry.attempts >= 5) {
      console.log("❌ Too many attempts");
      this.otpStore.delete(key);
      return false;
    }

    // Increment attempts
    entry.attempts++;

    // Verify code
    const hashedCode = await this.hashCode(code);
    if (hashedCode !== entry.code) {
      console.log("❌ Invalid OTP code");
      return false;
    }

    // Valid! Delete OTP (one-time use)
    this.otpStore.delete(key);
    console.log("✅ OTP verified");
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * Generate 6-digit OTP
   */
  private generateOtpCode(): string {
    return crypto.randomInt(100000, 999999).toString();
  }

  /**
   * Hash OTP code for storage
   */
  private async hashCode(code: string): Promise<string> {
    return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(code).digest("hex");
  }

  /**
   * Cleanup expired OTPs
   */
  private cleanupExpiredOtps(): void {
    const now = new Date();
    let cleaned = 0;

    for (const [key, entry] of this.otpStore.entries()) {
      if (entry.expiresAt < now) {
        this.otpStore.delete(key);
        cleaned++;
      }
    }

    if (cleaned > 0) {
      console.log(`🧹 Cleaned up ${cleaned} expired OTPs`);
    }
  }
}
