import {
  Controller,
  Get,
  Post,
  Delete,
  Body,
  Request,
  UseGuards,
  HttpCode,
  HttpStatus,
  UnauthorizedException,
  Param,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import {
  ApiTags,
  ApiOperation,
  ApiResponse,
  ApiBearerAuth,
} from "@nestjs/swagger";
import { MfaService } from "./mfa.service";
import {
  VerifyTotpDto,
  VerifyBackupCodeDto,
  SetupTotpResponseDto,
  MfaStatusDto,
  MfaEnabledResponseDto,
  BackupCodesResponseDto,
} from "./dto/mfa.dto";
import { JwtAuthGuard } from "@/common/guards";
import { EmailOtpService } from "@/modules/mfa/email-otp.service";
import { WebAuthnService } from "@/modules/mfa/webauthn.service";

@ApiTags("MFA")
@Controller("mfa")
@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)
@ApiBearerAuth()
export class MfaController {
  constructor(
    private readonly mfaService: MfaService,
    private readonly emailOtpService: EmailOtpService,
    private readonly webauthnService: WebAuthnService,
  ) {}

  // ============================================================
  // TOTP SETUP & MANAGEMENT
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Setup TOTP (Step 1: Generate QR code)
   */
  @Post("setup/totp")
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Setup TOTP MFA",
    description: "Generate QR code and backup codes for TOTP setup",
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: "TOTP setup data",
    type: SetupTotpResponseDto,
  })
  async setupTotp(@Request() req: any): Promise<SetupTotpResponseDto> {
    const userId = req.user.sub;
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    return this.mfaService.setupTotp(userId, tenantId);
  }

  /**
   * Verify and enable TOTP (Step 2: Verify code from authenticator app)
   */
  @Post("verify/totp")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Verify and enable TOTP MFA",
    description: "Verify TOTP code and enable MFA",
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: "TOTP enabled successfully",
    type: MfaEnabledResponseDto,
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 400,
    description: "TOTP not setup or already enabled",
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: "Invalid TOTP code" })
  async verifyAndEnableTotp(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Body() dto: VerifyTotpDto,
  ): Promise<MfaEnabledResponseDto> {
    const userId = req.user.sub;
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    const { backupCodes } = await this.mfaService.verifyAndEnableTotp(
      userId,
      tenantId,
      dto.token,
    );

    return {
      message: "TOTP MFA enabled successfully",
      backupCodes,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Disable TOTP MFA
   */
  @Delete("totp")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Disable TOTP MFA",
    description: "Turn off TOTP-based MFA",
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "TOTP disabled" })
  async disableTotp(@Request() req: any) {
    const userId = req.user.sub;
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    await this.mfaService.disableTotp(userId, tenantId);

    return { message: "TOTP MFA disabled successfully" };
  }

  // ============================================================
  // BACKUP CODES
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Regenerate backup codes
   */
  @Post("backup-codes/regenerate")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Regenerate backup codes",
    description: "Generate new backup codes (invalidates old ones)",
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: "New backup codes generated",
    type: BackupCodesResponseDto,
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 400, description: "MFA must be enabled first" })
  async regenerateBackupCodes(
    @Request() req: any,
  ): Promise<BackupCodesResponseDto> {
    const userId = req.user.sub;
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    const backupCodes = await this.mfaService.regenerateBackupCodes(
      userId,
      tenantId,
    );

    return {
      backupCodes,
      count: backupCodes.length,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Verify backup code (for testing)
   */
  @Post("verify/backup-code")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Verify backup code",
    description: "Test a backup code (consumes it if valid)",
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Backup code verified" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: "Invalid backup code" })
  async verifyBackupCode(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Body() dto: VerifyBackupCodeDto,
  ) {
    const userId = req.user.sub;
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    const verified = await this.mfaService.verifyBackupCode(
      userId,
      tenantId,
      dto.code,
    );

    if (!verified) {
      return { valid: false, message: "Invalid backup code" };
    }

    return { valid: true, message: "Backup code verified and consumed" };
  }

  // ============================================================
  // MFA STATUS
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Get MFA status
   */
  @Get("status")
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Get MFA status",
    description: "Check which MFA methods are enabled",
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: "MFA status",
    type: MfaStatusDto,
  })
  async getMfaStatus(@Request() req: any): Promise<MfaStatusDto> {
    const userId = req.user.sub;
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    return this.mfaService.getMfaStatus(
      userId,
      tenantId,
    ) as unknown as MfaStatusDto;
  }

  /**
   * Disable all MFA methods
   */
  @Delete("all")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Disable all MFA",
    description: "Turn off all MFA methods",
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "All MFA disabled" })
  async disableAllMfa(@Request() req: any) {
    const userId = req.user.sub;
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    await this.mfaService.disableAllMfa(userId, tenantId);

    return { message: "All MFA methods disabled successfully" };
  }

  /**
   * Request email OTP
   */
  @Post("request-email-otp")
  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)
  @ApiBearerAuth()
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Request OTP code via email",
    description: "Send a 6-digit code to user email for MFA",
  })
  async requestEmailOtp(@Request() req: any) {
    const userId = req.user.sub;
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    // Send OTP
    await this.emailOtpService.sendOtp(userId, tenantId);

    return {
      message: "OTP code sent to your email",
      expiresIn: "10 minutes",
    };
  }

  /**
   * Verify email OTP
   */
  @Post("verify-email-otp")
  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)
  @ApiBearerAuth()
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Verify email OTP code",
    description: "Verify the 6-digit code received via email",
  })
  async verifyEmailOtp(@Request() req: any, @Body() dto: { code: string }) {
    const userId = req.user.sub;
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    const verified = await this.emailOtpService.verifyOtp(
      userId,
      tenantId,
      dto.code,
    );

    if (!verified) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid or expired OTP code");
    }

    return { message: "Email OTP verified" };
  }

  /**
   * Start WebAuthn registration
   */
  @Post("webauthn/register/start")
  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)
  @ApiBearerAuth()
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Start WebAuthn credential registration" })
  async startWebAuthnRegistration(@Request() req: any) {
    const userId = req.user.sub;
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    const options = await this.webauthnService.startRegistration(
      userId,
      tenantId,
    );
    return options;
  }

  /**
   * Finish WebAuthn registration
   */
  @Post("webauthn/register/finish")
  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)
  @ApiBearerAuth()
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Finish WebAuthn credential registration" })
  async finishWebAuthnRegistration(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Body() body: { response: any; deviceName?: string },
  ) {
    const userId = req.user.sub;
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    const result = await this.webauthnService.finishRegistration(
      userId,
      tenantId,
      body.response,
      body.deviceName,
    );

    return {
      success: true,
      credentialId: result.credentialId,
      message: "Passkey registered successfully",
    };
  }

  /**
   * List WebAuthn credentials
   */
  @Get("webauthn/credentials")
  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)
  @ApiBearerAuth()
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "List user WebAuthn credentials" })
  async listWebAuthnCredentials(@Request() req: any) {
    const userId = req.user.sub;
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    const credentials = await this.webauthnService.listCredentials(
      userId,
      tenantId,
    );
    return { credentials };
  }

  /**
   * Delete WebAuthn credential
   */
  @Delete("webauthn/credentials/:credentialId")
  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)
  @ApiBearerAuth()
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Delete WebAuthn credential" })
  async deleteWebAuthnCredential(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Param("credentialId") credentialId: string,
  ) {
    const userId = req.user.sub;
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    await this.webauthnService.deleteCredential(userId, tenantId, credentialId);

    return {
      success: true,
      message: "Passkey deleted successfully",
    };
  }

import { Module } from "@nestjs/common";
import { TypeOrmModule } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import {
  MfaSettings,
  SecurityEvent,
  Session,
  Tenant,
  User,
} from "@repo/database";
import { MfaController } from "./mfa.controller";
import { MfaService } from "./mfa.service";
import { EmailOtpService } from "@/modules/mfa/email-otp.service";
import { EmailService } from "@/modules/email/email.service";
import { UserService } from "@/modules/user/user.service";
import { EmailModule } from "@/modules/email/email.module";
import { UserModule } from "@/modules/user/user.module";
import { WebAuthnService } from "@/modules/mfa/webauthn.service";

/**
 * MFA Module
 *
 * Handles Multi-Factor Authentication:
 * - TOTP (Google Authenticator, Authy, etc.)
 * - Backup codes
 * - MFA status management
 * - Future: SMS, Email, WebAuthn
 */
@Module({
  imports: [
    TypeOrmModule.forFeature([
      MfaSettings,
      User,
      SecurityEvent,
      Tenant,
      Session,
    ]),
    EmailModule,
    UserModule,
  ],
  controllers: [MfaController],
  providers: [
    MfaService,
    EmailOtpService,
    EmailService,
    UserService,
    WebAuthnService,
  ],
  exports: [MfaService], // Export for use in AuthService
})
export class MfaModule {}

// apps/auth-service/src/modules/mfa/mfa.service.ts

import {
  Injectable,
  NotFoundException,
  BadRequestException,
  UnauthorizedException,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { InjectRepository } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { Repository } from "typeorm";
import { MfaSettings, User } from "@repo/database";
import * as speakeasy from "speakeasy";
import * as QRCode from "qrcode";
import * as crypto from "crypto";

export interface SetupTotpResponse {
  secret: string;
  qrCode: string; // Data URL for QR code
  backupCodes: string[];
}

export interface VerifyTotpDto {
  token: string;
}

@Injectable()
export class MfaService {
  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(MfaSettings)
    private mfaRepository: Repository<MfaSettings>,
    @InjectRepository(User)
    private userRepository: Repository<User>,
  ) {}

  // ============================================================
  // TOTP (Google Authenticator, Authy, etc.)
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Generate TOTP secret and QR code for setup
   */
  async setupTotp(
    userId: string,
    tenantId: string,
  ): Promise<SetupTotpResponse> {
    // Get user for email/name
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: userId, tenantId },
    });

    if (!user) {
      throw new NotFoundException("User not found");
    }

    // Generate secret
    const secret = speakeasy.generateSecret({
      name: `FortKnox (${user.email})`,
      issuer: "Fort Knox Auth",
      length: 32,
    });

    // Get or create MFA settings
    let mfaSettings = await this.mfaRepository.findOne({
      where: { userId, tenantId },
    });

    if (!mfaSettings) {
      mfaSettings = this.mfaRepository.create({
        userId,
        tenantId,
      });
    }

    // Store secret (not yet verified)
    mfaSettings.totpSecret = secret.base32;
    await this.mfaRepository.save(mfaSettings);

    // Generate QR code
    const qrCode = await QRCode.toDataURL(secret.otpauth_url!);

    // Generate backup codes
    const backupCodes = this.generateBackupCodes();

    return {
      secret: secret.base32,
      qrCode,
      backupCodes,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Verify TOTP token and enable TOTP MFA
   */
  async verifyAndEnableTotp(
    userId: string,
    tenantId: string,
    token: string,
  ): Promise<{ backupCodes: string[] }> {
    const mfaSettings = await this.mfaRepository.findOne({
      where: { userId, tenantId },
      select: ["id", "totpSecret", "totpEnabled"], // Include secret
    });

    if (!mfaSettings || !mfaSettings.totpSecret) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        "TOTP not set up. Call /mfa/setup/totp first",
      );
    }

    if (mfaSettings.totpEnabled) {
      throw new BadRequestException("TOTP already enabled");
    }

    // Verify token
    const verified = speakeasy.totp.verify({
      secret: mfaSettings.totpSecret,
      encoding: "base32",
      token,
      window: 2, // Allow 2 time steps before/after (60s window)
    });

    if (!verified) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid TOTP token");
    }

    // Enable TOTP
    mfaSettings.totpEnabled = true;
    mfaSettings.totpVerifiedAt = new Date();

    // Generate and store backup codes
    const backupCodes = this.generateBackupCodes();
    mfaSettings.backupCodes = await Promise.all(
      backupCodes.map((code) => this.hashBackupCode(code)),
    );

    await this.mfaRepository.save(mfaSettings);

    console.log(`✅ TOTP MFA enabled for user ${userId}`);

    return { backupCodes };
  }

  /**
   * Verify TOTP token during login
   */
  async verifyTotp(
    userId: string,
    tenantId: string,
    token: string,
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    const mfaSettings = await this.mfaRepository.findOne({
      where: { userId, tenantId, totpEnabled: true },
      select: ["id", "totpSecret"],
    });

    if (!mfaSettings || !mfaSettings.totpSecret) {
      throw new BadRequestException("TOTP not enabled for this user");
    }

    const verified = speakeasy.totp.verify({
      secret: mfaSettings.totpSecret,
      encoding: "base32",
      token,
      window: 2,
    });

    if (!verified) {
      console.log(`❌ Invalid TOTP token for user ${userId}`);
      return false;
    }

    console.log(`✅ TOTP verified for user ${userId}`);
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * Disable TOTP MFA
   */
  async disableTotp(userId: string, tenantId: string): Promise<void> {
    const mfaSettings = await this.mfaRepository.findOne({
      where: { userId, tenantId },
    });

    if (!mfaSettings) {
      throw new NotFoundException("MFA settings not found");
    }

    mfaSettings.totpEnabled = false;
    mfaSettings.totpSecret = undefined;
    mfaSettings.totpVerifiedAt = undefined;

    await this.mfaRepository.save(mfaSettings);

    console.log(`✅ TOTP MFA disabled for user ${userId}`);
  }

  // ============================================================
  // BACKUP CODES
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Generate new backup codes
   */
  private generateBackupCodes(count = 10): string[] {
    const codes: string[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      // Generate 8-character code (XXXX-XXXX format)
      const code =
        this.generateRandomString(4) + "-" + this.generateRandomString(4);
      codes.push(code);
    }

    return codes;
  }

  /**
   * Hash backup code for storage
   */
  private async hashBackupCode(code: string): Promise<string> {
    return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(code).digest("hex");
  }

  /**
   * Verify backup code
   */
  async verifyBackupCode(
    userId: string,
    tenantId: string,
    code: string,
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    const mfaSettings = await this.mfaRepository.findOne({
      where: { userId, tenantId },
      select: ["id", "backupCodes"],
    });

    if (!mfaSettings || !mfaSettings.backupCodes) {
      throw new BadRequestException("No backup codes available");
    }

    const codeHash = await this.hashBackupCode(code);

    // Find and remove used code
    const codeIndex = mfaSettings.backupCodes.indexOf(codeHash);

    if (codeIndex === -1) {
      console.log(`❌ Invalid backup code for user ${userId}`);
      return false;
    }

    // Remove used code
    mfaSettings.backupCodes.splice(codeIndex, 1);
    await this.mfaRepository.save(mfaSettings);

    console.log(`✅ Backup code verified for user ${userId}`);
    return true;
  }

  /**
   * Regenerate backup codes
   */
  async regenerateBackupCodes(
    userId: string,
    tenantId: string,
  ): Promise<string[]> {
    const mfaSettings = await this.mfaRepository.findOne({
      where: { userId, tenantId },
    });

    if (!mfaSettings) {
      throw new NotFoundException("MFA settings not found");
    }

    if (!mfaSettings.totpEnabled) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        "MFA must be enabled to generate backup codes",
      );
    }

    // Generate new codes
    const backupCodes = this.generateBackupCodes();
    mfaSettings.backupCodes = await Promise.all(
      backupCodes.map((code) => this.hashBackupCode(code)),
    );

    await this.mfaRepository.save(mfaSettings);

    console.log(`✅ Backup codes regenerated for user ${userId}`);

    return backupCodes;
  }

  // ============================================================
  // MFA STATUS & MANAGEMENT
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Check if user has MFA enabled
   */
  async isMfaEnabled(userId: string, tenantId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const mfaSettings = await this.mfaRepository.findOne({
      where: { userId, tenantId },
    });

    if (!mfaSettings) {
      return false;
    }

    return (
      mfaSettings.totpEnabled ||
      mfaSettings.smsEnabled ||
      mfaSettings.emailEnabled ||
      mfaSettings.webauthnEnabled
    );
  }

  /**
   * Get MFA status for user
   */
  async getMfaStatus(userId: string, tenantId: string) {
    const mfaSettings = await this.mfaRepository.findOne({
      where: { userId, tenantId },
    });

    if (!mfaSettings) {
      return {
        enabled: false,
        methods: [],
      };
    }

    const methods: string[] = [];
    if (mfaSettings.totpEnabled) methods.push("totp");
    if (mfaSettings.smsEnabled) methods.push("sms");
    if (mfaSettings.emailEnabled) methods.push("email");
    if (mfaSettings.webauthnEnabled) methods.push("webauthn");

    return {
      enabled: methods.length > 0,
      methods,
      preferredMethod: mfaSettings.preferredMethod || methods[0],
      backupCodesRemaining: mfaSettings.backupCodes?.length || 0,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Disable all MFA methods
   */
  async disableAllMfa(userId: string, tenantId: string): Promise<void> {
    const mfaSettings = await this.mfaRepository.findOne({
      where: { userId, tenantId },
    });

    if (!mfaSettings) {
      return;
    }

    // Disable all methods
    mfaSettings.totpEnabled = false;
    mfaSettings.totpSecret = undefined;
    mfaSettings.smsEnabled = false;
    mfaSettings.emailEnabled = false;
    mfaSettings.webauthnEnabled = false;
    mfaSettings.backupCodes = [];

    await this.mfaRepository.save(mfaSettings);

    console.log(`✅ All MFA methods disabled for user ${userId}`);
  }

  // ============================================================
  // HELPER METHODS
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Generate random alphanumeric string
   */
  private generateRandomString(length: number): string {
    const chars = "ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789"; // Exclude similar chars
    let result = "";
    const bytes = crypto.randomBytes(length);

    for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
      result += chars[(bytes[i] as any) % chars.length];
    }

    return result;
  }
}

FILE: src/modules/mfa/webauthn.service.ts
SIZE: 11.12 KB | LINES: 408

import {
  Injectable,
  BadRequestException,
  NotFoundException,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { InjectRepository } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { Repository } from "typeorm";
import { MfaSettings, User } from "@repo/database";
import {
  generateRegistrationOptions,
  verifyRegistrationResponse,
  generateAuthenticationOptions,
  verifyAuthenticationResponse,
  VerifiedRegistrationResponse,
  VerifiedAuthenticationResponse,
} from "@simplewebauthn/server";
import type {
  RegistrationResponseJSON,
  AuthenticationResponseJSON,
} from "@simplewebauthn/server";
import { ConfigService } from "@nestjs/config";
import { type AuthenticatorTransportFuture } from "@simplewebauthn/server";

// Store challenges temporarily (use Redis in production!)
interface ChallengeEntry {
  challenge: string;
  expiresAt: Date;
}

const encoder = new TextEncoder();

@Injectable()
export class WebAuthnService {
  // In-memory store for challenges (replace with Redis!)
  private challenges = new Map<string, ChallengeEntry>();

  // WebAuthn configuration
  private readonly rpName = "Fort Knox";
  private readonly rpID: string;
  private readonly origin: string;

  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(MfaSettings)
    private mfaRepository: Repository<MfaSettings>,
    @InjectRepository(User)
    private userRepository: Repository<User>,
    private configService: ConfigService,
  ) {
    // Get from environment
    this.rpID = this.configService.get("WEBAUTHN_RP_ID") || "localhost";
    this.origin =
      this.configService.get("WEBAUTHN_ORIGIN") || "http://localhost:3000";

    // Cleanup expired challenges every 5 minutes
    setInterval(() => this.cleanupExpiredChallenges(), 5 * 60 * 1000);
  }

  /**
   * Start WebAuthn registration (generate options)
   */
  async startRegistration(userId: string, tenantId: string): Promise<any> {
    // Get user
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: userId, tenantId },
    });

    if (!user) {
      throw new NotFoundException("User not found");
    }

    // Get or create MFA settings
    let mfaSettings = await this.mfaRepository.findOne({
      where: { userId, tenantId },
    });

    if (!mfaSettings) {
      mfaSettings = this.mfaRepository.create({
        userId,
        tenantId,
        webauthnCredentials: [],
      });
      await this.mfaRepository.save(mfaSettings);
    }

    // Get existing credentials (to exclude them)
    const existingCredentials = mfaSettings.webauthnCredentials.map((cred) => ({
      id: cred.id,
      type: "public-key" as const,
    }));

    // Generate registration options
    const options = await generateRegistrationOptions({
      rpName: this.rpName,
      rpID: this.rpID,
      userID: encoder.encode(userId),
      userName: user.email,
      userDisplayName: `${user.firstName} ${user.lastName}`,

      // Don't prompt for password
      attestationType: "none",

      // Exclude existing credentials
      excludeCredentials: existingCredentials,

      // Authenticator selection
      authenticatorSelection: {
        residentKey: "preferred",
        userVerification: "preferred",
        authenticatorAttachment: "platform", // Prefer platform authenticators (Face ID, Touch ID)
      },
    });

    // Store challenge (expires in 5 minutes)
    const key = `${userId}:${tenantId}:register`;
    this.challenges.set(key, {
      challenge: options.challenge,
      expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 5 * 60 * 1000),
    });

    console.log("🔐 WebAuthn registration started for", user.email);

    return options;
  }

  /**
   * Finish WebAuthn registration (verify response)
   */
  async finishRegistration(
    userId: string,
    tenantId: string,
    response: RegistrationResponseJSON,
    deviceName?: string,
  ): Promise<{ credentialId: string }> {
    // Get stored challenge
    const key = `${userId}:${tenantId}:register`;
    const challengeEntry = this.challenges.get(key);

    if (!challengeEntry) {
      throw new BadRequestException("No registration in progress");
    }

    if (challengeEntry.expiresAt < new Date()) {
      this.challenges.delete(key);
      throw new BadRequestException("Registration challenge expired");
    }

    // Verify registration response
    let verification: VerifiedRegistrationResponse;

    try {
      verification = await verifyRegistrationResponse({
        response,
        expectedChallenge: challengeEntry.challenge,
        expectedOrigin: this.origin,
        expectedRPID: this.rpID,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("❌ WebAuthn registration verification failed:", error);
      throw new BadRequestException("Registration verification failed");
    }

    if (!verification.verified || !verification.registrationInfo) {
      throw new BadRequestException("Registration verification failed");
    }

    // Delete used challenge
    this.challenges.delete(key);

    // Get MFA settings
    const mfaSettings = await this.mfaRepository.findOne({
      where: { userId, tenantId },
    });

    if (!mfaSettings) {
      throw new NotFoundException("MFA settings not found");
    }

    // Store credential
    const { credential } = verification.registrationInfo;

    const {
      publicKey: credentialPublicKey,
      id: credentialID,
      counter,
    } = credential;

    const newCredential = {
      id: Buffer.from(credentialID).toString("base64url"),
      publicKey: Buffer.from(credentialPublicKey).toString("base64url"),
      counter,
      deviceName: deviceName || "Unnamed Device",
      createdAt: new Date(),
      lastUsedAt: new Date(),
      transports: response.response.transports || [],
    };

    mfaSettings.webauthnCredentials.push(newCredential);
    mfaSettings.webauthnEnabled = true;

    await this.mfaRepository.save(mfaSettings);

    console.log("✅ WebAuthn credential registered:", newCredential.id);

    return { credentialId: newCredential.id };
  }

  /**
   * Start WebAuthn authentication (generate options)
   */
  async startAuthentication(userId: string, tenantId: string): Promise<any> {
    // Get MFA settings
    const mfaSettings = await this.mfaRepository.findOne({
      where: { userId, tenantId, webauthnEnabled: true },
    });

    if (!mfaSettings || mfaSettings.webauthnCredentials.length === 0) {
      throw new BadRequestException("WebAuthn not enabled for this user");
    }

    // Get user's credentials
    const allowCredentials = mfaSettings.webauthnCredentials.map((cred) => ({
      id: cred.id,
      type: "public-key" as const,
      transports: cred.transports as AuthenticatorTransportFuture[] | undefined,
    }));

    // Generate authentication options
    const options = await generateAuthenticationOptions({
      rpID: this.rpID,
      allowCredentials,
      userVerification: "preferred",
    });

    // Store challenge (expires in 5 minutes)
    const key = `${userId}:${tenantId}:authenticate`;
    this.challenges.set(key, {
      challenge: options.challenge,
      expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 5 * 60 * 1000),
    });

    console.log("🔐 WebAuthn authentication started for user:", userId);

    return options;
  }

  /**
   * Finish WebAuthn authentication (verify response)
   */
  async finishAuthentication(
    userId: string,
    tenantId: string,
    response: AuthenticationResponseJSON,
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    // Get stored challenge
    const key = `${userId}:${tenantId}:authenticate`;
    const challengeEntry = this.challenges.get(key);

    if (!challengeEntry) {
      throw new BadRequestException("No authentication in progress");
    }

    if (challengeEntry.expiresAt < new Date()) {
      this.challenges.delete(key);
      throw new BadRequestException("Authentication challenge expired");
    }

    // Get MFA settings
    const mfaSettings = await this.mfaRepository.findOne({
      where: { userId, tenantId, webauthnEnabled: true },
    });

    if (!mfaSettings) {
      throw new BadRequestException("WebAuthn not enabled");
    }

    // Find the credential
    const credentialId = Buffer.from(response.rawId, "base64url").toString(
      "base64url",
    );
    const credential = mfaSettings.webauthnCredentials.find(
      (cred) => cred.id === credentialId,
    );

    if (!credential) {
      throw new BadRequestException("Credential not found");
    }

    // Verify authentication response
    let verification: VerifiedAuthenticationResponse;

    try {
      verification = await verifyAuthenticationResponse({
        response,
        expectedChallenge: challengeEntry.challenge,
        expectedOrigin: this.origin,
        expectedRPID: this.rpID,
        credential: {
          id: credential.id,
          publicKey: Buffer.from(credential.publicKey, "base64url"),
          counter: credential.counter,
        },
      });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("❌ WebAuthn authentication verification failed:", error);
      throw new BadRequestException("Authentication verification failed");
    }

    if (!verification.verified) {
      throw new BadRequestException("Authentication verification failed");
    }

    // Delete used challenge
    this.challenges.delete(key);

    // Update credential counter and last used
    credential.counter = verification.authenticationInfo.newCounter;
    credential.lastUsedAt = new Date();

    await this.mfaRepository.save(mfaSettings);

    console.log("✅ WebAuthn authentication verified:", credential.id);

    return true;
  }

  /**
   * List user's WebAuthn credentials
   */
  async listCredentials(
    userId: string,
    tenantId: string,
  ): Promise<
    Array<{
      id: string;
      deviceName: string;
      createdAt: Date;
      lastUsedAt?: Date;
      transports: string[];
    }>
  > {
    const mfaSettings = await this.mfaRepository.findOne({
      where: { userId, tenantId },
    });

    if (!mfaSettings) {
      return [];
    }

    return mfaSettings.webauthnCredentials.map((cred) => ({
      id: cred.id,
      deviceName: cred.deviceName || "Unnamed Device",
      createdAt: cred.createdAt,
      lastUsedAt: cred.lastUsedAt,
      transports: cred.transports || [],
    }));
  }

  /**
   * Delete a WebAuthn credential
   */
  async deleteCredential(
    userId: string,
    tenantId: string,
    credentialId: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const mfaSettings = await this.mfaRepository.findOne({
      where: { userId, tenantId },
    });

    if (!mfaSettings) {
      throw new NotFoundException("MFA settings not found");
    }

    // Remove credential
    mfaSettings.webauthnCredentials = mfaSettings.webauthnCredentials.filter(
      (cred) => cred.id !== credentialId,
    );

    // Disable WebAuthn if no credentials left
    if (mfaSettings.webauthnCredentials.length === 0) {
      mfaSettings.webauthnEnabled = false;
    }

    await this.mfaRepository.save(mfaSettings);

    console.log("🗑️ WebAuthn credential deleted:", credentialId);
  }

  /**
   * Cleanup expired challenges
   */
  private cleanupExpiredChallenges(): void {
    const now = new Date();
    let cleaned = 0;

    for (const [key, entry] of this.challenges.entries()) {
      if (entry.expiresAt < now) {
        this.challenges.delete(key);
        cleaned++;
      }
    }

    if (cleaned > 0) {
      console.log(`🧹 Cleaned up ${cleaned} expired WebAuthn challenges`);
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Authorization Request DTO
 * GET /oauth/authorize
 */

  @ApiProperty({
    description: "OAuth2 client ID",
    example: "client_abc123",
  })
  @IsString()
  @IsNotEmpty()
  client_id!: string;

  @ApiProperty({

    example: "https://app.example.com/callback",

    description: "OAuth2 response type",

    example: "code",

  @ApiProperty({

    example: "openid profile email",

  @IsString()
  @IsNotEmpty()

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    description: "OAuth2 state parameter for CSRF protection",
    example: "random_state_xyz",
  })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    description: "OIDC nonce parameter for replay protection",

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    description: "PKCE code challenge method",
    enum: ["S256", "plain"],
    example: "S256",
  })
  @IsString()
  @IsIn(["S256", "plain"])
  @IsOptional()

}

/**
 * Update OAuth Client Request DTO
 * PATCH /oauth/clients/:clientId
 */
export class UpdateOAuthClientDto {
  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    description: "Client name",
    example: "My Updated Application",
  })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  name?: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    description: "Client description",
    example: "Updated description",
  })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  description?: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    description: "Redirect URIs",
    example: ["https://app.example.com/callback"],
    isArray: true,
  })
  @IsArray()
  @IsUrl({}, { each: true })
  @IsOptional()
  redirectUris?: string[];

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    description: "Logo URL",
    example: "https://app.example.com/new-logo.png",
  })
  @IsUrl()

  logoUrl?: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    description: "Is active",
    example: true,
  })
  @IsBoolean()
  @IsOptional()
  isActive?: boolean;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    description: "Allowed scopes",
    example: ["openid", "profile", "email"],
    isArray: true,
  })
  @IsArray()

}

import { IsString, IsNotEmpty, IsOptional, IsIn } from "class-validator";
import { ApiProperty, ApiPropertyOptional } from "@nestjs/swagger";

/**
 * Token Request DTO (Authorization Code Grant)
 * POST /oauth/token
 */
export class TokenRequestDto {
  @ApiProperty({
    description: "OAuth2 grant type",
    enum: ["authorization_code", "refresh_token", "client_credentials"],
    example: "authorization_code",
  })
  @IsString()
  @IsIn(["authorization_code", "refresh_token", "client_credentials"])
  grant_type!: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    description: "Authorization code (for authorization_code grant)",
    example: "auth_code_abc123",
  })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  code?: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    description: "Redirect URI (must match authorization request)",
    example: "https://app.example.com/callback",
  })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  redirect_uri?: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    description: "Refresh token (for refresh_token grant)",
    example: "refresh_token_xyz789",
  })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  refresh_token?: string;

  @ApiProperty({
    description: "OAuth2 client ID",
    example: "client_abc123",
  })
  @IsString()
  @IsNotEmpty()
  client_id!: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    description: "OAuth2 client secret (for confidential clients)",
    example: "secret_xyz789",
  })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  client_secret?: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    description: "PKCE code verifier (for public clients)",
    example: "dBjftJeZ4CVP-mB92K27uhbUJU1p1r_wW1gFWFOEjXk",
  })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  code_verifier?: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    description: "Requested scopes (for client_credentials grant)",
    example: "api:read api:write",
  })
  @IsString()
  @IsOptional()
  scope?: string;
}

/**
 * Token Response DTO
 */
export class TokenResponseDto {
  @ApiProperty({
    description: "Access token (JWT)",
    example: "eyJhbGciOiJSUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9...",
  })
  access_token!: string;

  @ApiProperty({
    description: "Token type",
    example: "Bearer",
  })
  token_type!: string;

  @ApiProperty({
    description: "Expiration time in seconds",
    example: 900,
  })
  expires_in!: number;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    description: "Refresh token (opaque)",
    example: "refresh_token_xyz789",
  })
  refresh_token?: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    description: "ID token (JWT, OIDC only)",
    example: "eyJhbGciOiJSUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9...",
  })
  id_token?: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    description: "Granted scopes",
    example: "openid profile email",
  })
  scope?: string;
}

/**
 * Token Error Response DTO
 */
export class TokenErrorResponseDto {
  @ApiProperty({
    description: "Error code",
    enum: [
      "invalid_request",
      "invalid_client",
      "invalid_grant",
      "unauthorized_client",
      "unsupported_grant_type",
      "invalid_scope",
    ],
    example: "invalid_grant",
  })
  error!: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    description: "Human-readable error description",
    example: "The provided authorization code is invalid",
  })
  error_description?: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    description: "URI with error information",
    example: "https://docs.fortknox.io/errors/invalid_grant",
  })
  error_uri?: string;
}

import {
  Controller,
  Get,
  Post,
  Body,
  Query,
  Param,
  Req,
  Res,
  UseGuards,
  HttpCode,
  HttpStatus,
  UnauthorizedException,
  BadRequestException,
  Headers,
  Delete,
  Put,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import {
  ApiTags,
  ApiOperation,
  ApiResponse,
  ApiQuery,
  ApiBody,
  ApiBearerAuth,
  ApiHeader,
} from "@nestjs/swagger";
import { JwtAuthGuard } from "@/common/guards";
import { OAuthClientService } from "./services/oauth-client.service";
import { OAuthTokenService } from "./services/oauth-token.service";
import { OAuthConsentService } from "./services/oauth-consent.service";
import {
  AuthorizeRequestDto,
  TokenRequestDto,
  TokenResponseDto,
  TokenErrorResponseDto,
  UserInfoResponseDto,
  RevokeTokenRequestDto,
  IntrospectTokenRequestDto,
  IntrospectTokenResponseDto,
  CreateOAuthClientDto,
  UpdateOAuthClientDto,
  OAuthClientResponseDto,
  CreateClientResponseDto,
  OpenIDConfigurationDto,
  JWKSResponseDto,
} from "./dto";
import { ConfigService } from "@nestjs/config";
import { type Request, type Response } from "express";
import { PermissionsGuard } from "../rbac/guards/permissions.guard";
import { RequirePermissions } from "../rbac/decorators/rbac.decorator";

@ApiTags("OAuth2/OIDC")
@Controller("oauth")
export class OAuthController {
  constructor(
    private clientService: OAuthClientService,
    private tokenService: OAuthTokenService,
    private consentService: OAuthConsentService,
    private configService: ConfigService,
  ) {}

  // ============================================================
  // AUTHORIZATION ENDPOINT
  // ============================================================

  @Get("authorize")
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "OAuth2 Authorization Endpoint",
    description:
      "Initiate OAuth2 authorization code flow. User must be logged in.",
  })
  @ApiQuery({ name: "client_id", required: true })
  @ApiQuery({ name: "redirect_uri", required: true })
  @ApiQuery({ name: "response_type", required: true, enum: ["code"] })
  @ApiQuery({ name: "scope", required: true })
  @ApiQuery({ name: "state", required: false })
  @ApiQuery({ name: "nonce", required: false })
  @ApiQuery({ name: "code_challenge", required: false })
  @ApiQuery({
    name: "code_challenge_method",
    required: false,
    enum: ["S256", "plain"],
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 302,
    description: "Redirects to redirect_uri with code and state",
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 400,
    description: "Invalid request parameters",
  })
  async authorize(
    @Query() query: AuthorizeRequestDto,
    @Req() req: Request,
    @Res() res: Response,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    // TODO: Check if user is logged in
    // For now, assume user is authenticated and we have userId
    const userId = (req.user as any)?.userId;

    if (!userId) {
      // Redirect to login with return URL
      const loginUrl = `/auth/login?return_url=${encodeURIComponent(req.url)}`;
      return res.redirect(loginUrl);
    }

    // Validate client
    const client = await this.clientService.getClient(query.client_id);

    // Validate redirect URI
    await this.clientService.validateRedirectUri(client, query.redirect_uri);

    // Validate grant type
    await this.clientService.validateGrantType(client, "authorization_code");

    // Parse scopes
    const requestedScopes = query.scope.split(" ");

    // Validate scopes
    const grantedScopes = await this.clientService.validateScopes(
      client,
      requestedScopes,
    );

    // Check PKCE requirement
    if (client.requirePkce && !query.code_challenge) {
      throw new BadRequestException("code_challenge required for this client");
    }

    // Check if consent is required
    const existingConsent = await this.consentService.getConsent(
      tenantId,
      userId,
      client.id,
    );

    const requireConsent = await this.consentService.isConsentRequired(
      client,
      existingConsent ?? undefined,
    );

    if (requireConsent) {
      // TODO: Show consent screen
      // For now, auto-grant consent
      await this.consentService.grantConsent({
        tenantId,
        clientId: client.id,
        userId,
        grantedScopes,
        rememberConsent: false,
        ipAddress: req.ip,
        userAgent: req.get("user-agent"),
      });
    }

    // Generate authorization code
    const code = await this.tokenService.createAuthorizationCode({
      tenantId,
      clientId: client.id,
      userId,
      redirectUri: query.redirect_uri,
      scope: grantedScopes.join(" "),
      state: query.state,
      nonce: query.nonce,
      codeChallenge: query.code_challenge,
      codeChallengeMethod: query.code_challenge_method,
      ipAddress: req.ip,
      userAgent: req.get("user-agent"),
    });

    // Build redirect URL
    const redirectUrl = new URL(query.redirect_uri);
    redirectUrl.searchParams.set("code", code);
    if (query.state) {
      redirectUrl.searchParams.set("state", query.state);
    }

    // Redirect to client
    return res.redirect(redirectUrl.toString());
  }

  // ============================================================
  // TOKEN ENDPOINT
  // ============================================================

  @Post("token")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "OAuth2 Token Endpoint",
    description:
      "Exchange authorization code or refresh token for access token",
  })
  @ApiBody({ type: TokenRequestDto })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: "Token issued successfully",
    type: TokenResponseDto,
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 400,
    description: "Invalid request",
    type: TokenErrorResponseDto,
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 401,
    description: "Invalid credentials",
    type: TokenErrorResponseDto,
  })
  async token(
    @Body() body: TokenRequestDto,
    @Req() req: Request,
    @Headers("authorization") authHeader?: string,
  ): Promise<TokenResponseDto> {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    // Extract client credentials
    let clientId = body.client_id;
    let clientSecret = body.client_secret;

    // Check for Basic Auth
    if (authHeader && authHeader.startsWith("Basic ")) {
      const base64 = authHeader.slice(6);
      const decoded = Buffer.from(base64, "base64").toString("utf-8");
      const [id, secret] = decoded.split(":");
      clientId = id as string;
      clientSecret = secret;
    }

    if (!clientId) {
      throw new BadRequestException("client_id required");
    }

    // Validate client
    const client = await this.clientService.validateClient(
      clientId,
      clientSecret,
    );

    // Handle different grant types
    switch (body.grant_type) {
      case "authorization_code":
        return this.handleAuthorizationCodeGrant(
          tenantId,
          client,
          body,
          req.ip,
          req.get("user-agent"),
        );

      case "refresh_token":
        return this.handleRefreshTokenGrant(
          tenantId,
          client,
          body,
          req.ip,
          req.get("user-agent"),
        );

      case "client_credentials":
        return this.handleClientCredentialsGrant(
          tenantId,
          client,
          body,
          req.ip,
          req.get("user-agent"),
        );

      default:
        throw new BadRequestException("unsupported_grant_type");
    }
  }

  /**
   * Handle authorization code grant
   */
  private async handleAuthorizationCodeGrant(
    tenantId: string,
    client: any,
    body: TokenRequestDto,
    ipAddress?: string,
    userAgent?: string,
  ): Promise<TokenResponseDto> {
    if (!body.code) {
      throw new BadRequestException("code required");
    }

    if (!body.redirect_uri) {
      throw new BadRequestException("redirect_uri required");
    }

    // Validate and consume authorization code
    const authCode = await this.tokenService.validateAuthorizationCode(
      body.code,
      client.id,
      body.redirect_uri,
      body.code_verifier,
    );

    // Create access token
    const accessToken = await this.tokenService.createAccessToken(
      {
        tenantId,
        clientId: client.id,
        userId: authCode.userId,
        scope: authCode.scope,
        ipAddress,
        userAgent,
      },
      authCode.user,
    );

    // Create refresh token
    const refreshToken = await this.tokenService.createRefreshToken({
      tenantId,
      clientId: client.id,
      userId: authCode.userId,
      scope: authCode.scope,
      ipAddress,
      userAgent,
    });

    // Create ID token (if openid scope)
    let idToken: string | undefined;
    if (authCode.scope?.includes("openid")) {
      idToken = await this.tokenService.createIDToken(
        authCode.user,
        client,
        authCode.nonce,
      );
    }

    return {
      access_token: accessToken,
      token_type: "Bearer",
      expires_in: client.accessTokenTtl,
      refresh_token: refreshToken,
      id_token: idToken,
      scope: authCode.scope,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Handle refresh token grant
   */
  private async handleRefreshTokenGrant(
    tenantId: string,
    client: any,
    body: TokenRequestDto,
    ipAddress?: string,
    userAgent?: string,
  ): Promise<TokenResponseDto> {
    if (!body.refresh_token) {
      throw new BadRequestException("refresh_token required");
    }

    // Validate refresh token
    const refreshToken = await this.tokenService.validateRefreshToken(
      body.refresh_token,
    );

    // Verify client matches
    if (refreshToken.clientId !== client.id) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("client_mismatch");
    }

    // Rotate refresh token
    const newRefreshToken =
      await this.tokenService.rotateRefreshToken(refreshToken);

    // Create new access token
    const accessToken = await this.tokenService.createAccessToken(
      {
        tenantId,
        clientId: client.id,
        userId: refreshToken.userId,
        scope: refreshToken.scope,
        ipAddress,
        userAgent,
      },
      refreshToken.user,
    );

    return {
      access_token: accessToken,
      token_type: "Bearer",
      expires_in: client.accessTokenTtl,
      refresh_token: newRefreshToken,
      scope: refreshToken.scope,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Handle client credentials grant (M2M)
   */
  private async handleClientCredentialsGrant(
    tenantId: string,
    client: any,
    body: TokenRequestDto,
    ipAddress?: string,
    userAgent?: string,
  ): Promise<TokenResponseDto> {
    // Client must be confidential
    if (client.clientType !== "confidential") {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        "client_credentials requires confidential client",
      );
    }

    // Validate scopes
    const requestedScopes = body.scope ? body.scope.split(" ") : [];
    const grantedScopes = await this.clientService.validateScopes(
      client,
      requestedScopes,
    );

    // Create access token (no user)
    const accessToken = await this.tokenService.createAccessToken({
      tenantId,
      clientId: client.id,
      userId: undefined, // M2M token
      scope: grantedScopes.join(" "),
      ipAddress,
      userAgent,
    });

    return {
      access_token: accessToken,
      token_type: "Bearer",
      expires_in: client.accessTokenTtl,
      scope: grantedScopes.join(" "),
    };
  }

  // ============================================================
  // USERINFO ENDPOINT
  // ============================================================

  @Get("userinfo")
  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)
  @ApiBearerAuth()
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "OIDC UserInfo Endpoint",
    description: "Get authenticated user information",
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: "User information",
    type: UserInfoResponseDto,
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 401,
    description: "Unauthorized",
  })
  async userInfo(@Req() req: Request): Promise<UserInfoResponseDto> {
    const user = req.user as any;

    return {
      sub: user.userId,
      email: user.email,
      email_verified: true, // TODO: Get from user entity
      name: user.name,
      given_name: user.given_name,
      family_name: user.family_name,
      picture: user.picture,
      updated_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
    };
  }

  // ============================================================
  // REVOKE ENDPOINT
  // ============================================================

  @Post("revoke")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "OAuth2 Token Revocation",
    description: "Revoke an access or refresh token",
  })
  @ApiBody({ type: RevokeTokenRequestDto })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: "Token revoked (always returns 200 even if token invalid)",
  })
  async revoke(
    @Body() body: RevokeTokenRequestDto,
    @Headers("authorization") authHeader?: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    // TODO: Validate client credentials from Authorization header

    // Always return 200 per spec (don't leak token validity)
    try {
      if (body.token_type_hint === "refresh_token") {
        await this.tokenService.revokeRefreshToken(body.token);
      } else {
        await this.tokenService.revokeAccessToken(body.token);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      // Silently fail per spec
    }
  }

  // ============================================================
  // INTROSPECT ENDPOINT
  // ============================================================

  @Post("introspect")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "OAuth2 Token Introspection",
    description: "Get token metadata",
  })
  @ApiBody({ type: IntrospectTokenRequestDto })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: "Token metadata",
    type: IntrospectTokenResponseDto,
  })
  async introspect(
    @Body() body: IntrospectTokenRequestDto,
    @Headers("authorization") authHeader?: string,
  ): Promise<IntrospectTokenResponseDto> {
    // TODO: Validate client credentials from Authorization header

    return this.tokenService.introspectToken(body.token, body.token_type_hint);
  }

  // ============================================================
  // DISCOVERY ENDPOINTS
  // ============================================================

  @Get(".well-known/openid-configuration")
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "OpenID Connect Discovery",
    description: "Get OpenID Connect configuration",
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: "OpenID configuration",
    type: OpenIDConfigurationDto,
  })
  getOpenIDConfiguration(): OpenIDConfigurationDto {
    const baseUrl = this.configService.get(
      "BASE_URL",
      "https://auth.fortknox.io",
    );

    return {
      issuer: baseUrl,
      authorization_endpoint: `${baseUrl}/oauth/authorize`,
      token_endpoint: `${baseUrl}/oauth/token`,
      userinfo_endpoint: `${baseUrl}/oauth/userinfo`,
      jwks_uri: `${baseUrl}/oauth/jwks`,
      revocation_endpoint: `${baseUrl}/oauth/revoke`,
      introspection_endpoint: `${baseUrl}/oauth/introspect`,
      response_types_supported: ["code"],
      grant_types_supported: [
        "authorization_code",
        "refresh_token",
        "client_credentials",
      ],
      subject_types_supported: ["public"],
      id_token_signing_alg_values_supported: ["RS256"],
      scopes_supported: [
        "openid",
        "profile",
        "email",
        "phone",
        "address",
        "offline_access",
      ],
      token_endpoint_auth_methods_supported: [
        "client_secret_basic",
        "client_secret_post",
        "none",
      ],
      code_challenge_methods_supported: ["S256", "plain"],
      claims_supported: [
        "sub",
        "email",
        "email_verified",
        "name",
        "given_name",
        "family_name",
        "picture",
      ],
    };
  }

  @Get("jwks")
  @ApiOperation({

  })

  // ============================================================
  // CLIENT MANAGEMENT (Protected with RBAC)
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Create OAuth Client
   * Requires: clients:write permission
   * Only developers and admins can create OAuth clients
   */
  @Post("clients")
  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("clients:write")
  @ApiBearerAuth()
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Create OAuth Client (Requires: clients:write)",
    description: "Register a new OAuth2 client application",
  })
  @ApiBody({ type: CreateOAuthClientDto })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 201,
    description: "Client created",
    type: CreateClientResponseDto,
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: "Forbidden - missing permissions" })
  async createClient(
    @Body() dto: CreateOAuthClientDto,
    @Req() req: Request,
  ): Promise<CreateClientResponseDto> {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    const userId = (req.user as any).sub;

    const { client, clientSecret } = await this.clientService.createClient({
      tenantId,
      ...dto,
      createdBy: userId,
    });

    return {
      ...client,
      clientSecret, // Only shown once!
    } as any;
  }

  /**
   * List OAuth Clients
   * Requires: clients:read permission
   */
  @Get("clients")
  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("clients:read")
  @ApiBearerAuth()
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "List OAuth Clients (Requires: clients:read)",
    description: "Get all OAuth2 clients for current tenant",
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: "List of clients",
    type: [OAuthClientResponseDto],
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: "Forbidden - missing permissions" })
  async listClients(@Req() req: Request) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    const { clients } = await this.clientService.listClients(tenantId, {
      isActive: true,
    });

    return clients;
  }

  /**
   * Get OAuth Client
   * Requires: clients:read permission
   */
  @Get("clients/:clientId")
  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("clients:read")
  @ApiBearerAuth()
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Get OAuth Client (Requires: clients:read)",
    description: "Get OAuth2 client details",
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: "Client details",
    type: OAuthClientResponseDto,
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: "Forbidden - missing permissions" })
  async getClient(@Param("clientId") clientId: string) {
    return this.clientService.getClient(clientId);
  }

  /**
   * Update OAuth Client
   * Requires: clients:write permission
   */
  @Put("clients/:clientId")
  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("clients:write")
  @ApiBearerAuth()
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Update OAuth Client (Requires: clients:write)",
    description: "Update OAuth2 client configuration",
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Client updated" })

  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: "Client not found" })
  async updateClient(
    @Param("clientId") clientId: string,
    @Body() dto: UpdateOAuthClientDto,
    @Req() req: Request,
  ) {

    return { message: "Client updated successfully" };
  }

  /**
   * Delete OAuth Client
   * Requires: clients:delete permission
   */
  @Delete("clients/:clientId")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT)
  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("clients:delete")
  @ApiBearerAuth()
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Delete OAuth Client (Requires: clients:delete)",
    description: "Delete an OAuth2 client",
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 204, description: "Client deleted" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: "Forbidden - missing permissions" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: "Client not found" })
  async deleteClient(@Param("clientId") clientId: string) {
    // TODO: Implement deleteClient in service
    return;
  }

  /**
   * Rotate Client Secret
   * Requires: clients:write permission
   */
  @Post("clients/:clientId/rotate-secret")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("clients:write")
  @ApiBearerAuth()
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Rotate Client Secret (Requires: clients:write)",
    description: "Generate a new client secret",
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Secret rotated" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: "Forbidden - missing permissions" })
  async rotateClientSecret(@Param("clientId") clientId: string) {
    const newSecret = await this.clientService.rotateClientSecret(clientId);
    return {
      message: "Client secret rotated successfully",
      clientSecret: newSecret, // Only shown once!
    };
  }

  // ============================================================
  // NOTES ON PUBLIC ENDPOINTS (No RBAC protection needed)
  // ============================================================

  /*
  These endpoints remain public/unprotected:

  - GET /oauth/authorize - OAuth flow start (requires user login redirect)
  - POST /oauth/token - Token exchange (validated by client credentials)
  - GET /oauth/userinfo - Protected by Bearer token
  - POST /oauth/revoke - Token revocation
  - POST /oauth/introspect - Token introspection
  - GET /.well-known/openid-configuration - OIDC discovery
  - GET /oauth/jwks - Public keys

  These are part of the OAuth2/OIDC spec and must remain accessible
  for OAuth clients to function properly.
  */
}

import { Module } from "@nestjs/common";
import { TypeOrmModule } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { JwtModule } from "@nestjs/jwt";
import {
  OAuthClient,
  OAuthAuthorizationCode,
  OAuthAccessToken,
  OAuthRefreshToken,
  OAuthConsent,
  User,
  Role,
  Permission,
  UserRole,
} from "@repo/database";
import { PasswordService } from "@repo/security";
import { OAuthController } from "./oauth.controller";
import { OAuthClientService } from "./services/oauth-client.service";
import { OAuthTokenService } from "./services/oauth-token.service";
import { OAuthConsentService } from "./services/oauth-consent.service";
import { RbacService } from "@/modules/rbac/services/rbac.service";
import { RbacModule } from "@/modules/rbac/rbac.module";

@Module({
  imports: [
    TypeOrmModule.forFeature([
      OAuthClient,
      OAuthAuthorizationCode,
      OAuthAccessToken,
      OAuthRefreshToken,
      OAuthConsent,
      User,
      Role,
      Permission,
      UserRole,
    ]),
    JwtModule.register({}), // Config provided in service
    RbacModule,
  ],
  controllers: [OAuthController],
  providers: [
    OAuthClientService,
    OAuthTokenService,
    OAuthConsentService,
    PasswordService,
    RbacService,
  ],
  exports: [OAuthClientService, OAuthTokenService, OAuthConsentService],
})
export class OAuthModule {}

import {
  Injectable,
  NotFoundException,
  BadRequestException,
  ConflictException,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { InjectRepository } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { Repository } from "typeorm";
import { OAuthClient } from "@repo/database";
import { PasswordService } from "@repo/security";
import * as crypto from "crypto";

export interface CreateClientDto {
  tenantId: string;
  name: string;
  description?: string;
  logoUrl?: string;
  websiteUrl?: string;
  privacyPolicyUrl?: string;
  termsOfServiceUrl?: string;
  clientType: "confidential" | "public";
  grantTypes: string[];
  redirectUris: string[];
  postLogoutRedirectUris?: string[];
  allowedOrigins?: string[];
  allowedScopes?: string[];
  defaultScopes?: string[];
  requirePkce?: boolean;
  requireConsent?: boolean;
  isFirstParty?: boolean;
  accessTokenTtl?: number;
  refreshTokenTtl?: number;
  idTokenTtl?: number;
  metadata?: Record<string, any>;
  createdBy?: string;
}

export interface UpdateClientDto {
  name?: string;
  description?: string;
  logoUrl?: string;
  websiteUrl?: string;
  privacyPolicyUrl?: string;
  termsOfServiceUrl?: string;
  grantTypes?: string[];
  redirectUris?: string[];
  postLogoutRedirectUris?: string[];
  allowedOrigins?: string[];
  allowedScopes?: string[];
  defaultScopes?: string[];
  requirePkce?: boolean;
  requireConsent?: boolean;
  isActive?: boolean;
  accessTokenTtl?: number;
  refreshTokenTtl?: number;
  idTokenTtl?: number;
  metadata?: Record<string, any>;
}

@Injectable()
export class OAuthClientService {
  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(OAuthClient)
    private clientRepository: Repository<OAuthClient>,
    private passwordService: PasswordService,
  ) {}

  /**
   * Create new OAuth client
   * Returns client with plaintext secret (only time it's visible)
   */
  async createClient(data: CreateClientDto): Promise<{
    client: OAuthClient;
    clientSecret?: string;
  }> {
    // Validate grant types
    const validGrantTypes = [
      "authorization_code",
      "refresh_token",
      "client_credentials",
      "password", // Optional, legacy
    ];

    const invalidGrants = data.grantTypes.filter(
      (g) => !validGrantTypes.includes(g),
    );
    if (invalidGrants.length > 0) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        `Invalid grant types: ${invalidGrants.join(", ")}`,
      );
    }

    // Validate redirect URIs for authorization_code grant
    if (data.grantTypes.includes("authorization_code")) {
      if (!data.redirectUris || data.redirectUris.length === 0) {
        throw new BadRequestException(
          "redirect_uris required for authorization_code grant",
        );
      }

      // Validate URIs
      for (const uri of data.redirectUris) {
        try {
          const url = new URL(uri);
          // Allow localhost for development
          const isLocalhost =
            url.hostname === "localhost" || url.hostname === "127.0.0.1";
          const isHttps = url.protocol === "https:";

          if (!isHttps && !isLocalhost) {
            throw new BadRequestException(
              `Redirect URI must use HTTPS: ${uri}`,
            );
          }
        } catch (error) {
          throw new BadRequestException(`Invalid redirect URI: ${uri}`);
        }
      }
    }

    // Generate client ID
    const clientId = `${data.isFirstParty ? "fortknox" : "client"}_${crypto.randomBytes(16).toString("hex")}`;

    // Generate client secret (for confidential clients)
    let clientSecret: string | undefined;
    let clientSecretHash: string | undefined;

    if (data.clientType === "confidential") {
      clientSecret = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("base64url");
      clientSecretHash = await PasswordService.hash(clientSecret);
    }

    // Public clients MUST use PKCE
    const requirePkce =
      data.clientType === "public" ? true : (data.requirePkce ?? false);

    // Third-party clients MUST show consent
    const requireConsent = data.isFirstParty
      ? false
      : (data.requireConsent ?? true);

    // Create client
    const client = await this.clientRepository.save({
      tenantId: data.tenantId,
      clientId,
      clientSecretHash,
      clientType: data.clientType,
      name: data.name,
      description: data.description,
      logoUrl: data.logoUrl,
      websiteUrl: data.websiteUrl,
      privacyPolicyUrl: data.privacyPolicyUrl,
      termsOfServiceUrl: data.termsOfServiceUrl,
      grantTypes: data.grantTypes,
      redirectUris: data.redirectUris,
      postLogoutRedirectUris: data.postLogoutRedirectUris,
      allowedOrigins: data.allowedOrigins,
      allowedScopes: data.allowedScopes,
      defaultScopes: data.defaultScopes,
      requirePkce,
      requireConsent,
      isFirstParty: data.isFirstParty ?? false,
      accessTokenTtl: data.accessTokenTtl ?? 900, // 15 min
      refreshTokenTtl: data.refreshTokenTtl ?? 604800, // 7 days
      idTokenTtl: data.idTokenTtl ?? 3600, // 1 hour
      authorizationCodeTtl: 600, // 10 minutes
      metadata: data.metadata,
      createdBy: data.createdBy,
    });

    console.log(`✅ Created OAuth client: ${client.clientId} (${client.name})`);

    return {
      client,
      clientSecret, // Return plaintext secret only on creation
    };
  }

  /**
   * Get client by client_id
   */
  async getClient(clientId: string): Promise<OAuthClient> {
    const client = await this.clientRepository.findOne({
      where: { clientId, isActive: true },
    });

    if (!client) {
      throw new NotFoundException("Client not found");
    }

    return client;
  }

  /**
   * Get client with secret hash (for validation)
   */
  async getClientWithSecret(clientId: string): Promise<OAuthClient> {
    const client = await this.clientRepository
      .createQueryBuilder("client")
      .where("client.clientId = :clientId", { clientId })
      .andWhere("client.isActive = :isActive", { isActive: true })
      .addSelect("client.clientSecretHash")
      .getOne();

    if (!client) {
      throw new NotFoundException("Client not found");
    }

    return client;
  }

  /**
   * Validate client credentials
   */
  async validateClient(
    clientId: string,
    clientSecret?: string,
  ): Promise<OAuthClient> {
    const client = await this.getClientWithSecret(clientId);

    // Public clients don't have secrets
    if (client.clientType === "public") {
      if (clientSecret) {
        throw new BadRequestException(
          "Public clients should not send client_secret",
        );
      }
      return client;
    }

    // Confidential clients must provide secret
    if (client.clientType === "confidential") {
      if (!clientSecret) {
        throw new BadRequestException(
          "client_secret required for confidential clients",
        );
      }

      if (!client.clientSecretHash) {
        throw new Error("Client secret hash missing");
      }

      const isValid = await PasswordService.verify(
        client.clientSecretHash,
        clientSecret,
      );

      if (!isValid) {
        throw new BadRequestException("Invalid client credentials");
      }

      return client;
    }

    throw new Error("Invalid client type");
  }

  /**
   * Validate redirect URI
   */
  async validateRedirectUri(
    client: OAuthClient,
    redirectUri: string,
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!client.redirectUris.includes(redirectUri)) {
      throw new BadRequestException("Invalid redirect_uri");
    }

    return true;
  }

  /**
   * Validate grant type
   */
  async validateGrantType(
    client: OAuthClient,
    grantType: string,
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!client.grantTypes.includes(grantType)) {
      throw new BadRequestException(`Grant type not allowed: ${grantType}`);
    }

    return true;
  }

  /**
   * Validate scopes
   */
  async validateScopes(
    client: OAuthClient,
    requestedScopes: string[],
  ): Promise<string[]> {
    // If no scopes allowed, use defaults or return empty
    if (!client.allowedScopes || client.allowedScopes.length === 0) {
      return client.defaultScopes || [];
    }

    // Filter requested scopes to only allowed ones
    const allowedScopes = requestedScopes.filter((scope) =>
      client.allowedScopes!.includes(scope),
    );

    // If no valid scopes, use defaults
    if (allowedScopes.length === 0 && client.defaultScopes) {
      return client.defaultScopes;
    }

    return allowedScopes;
  }

  /**
   * Update client
   */
  async updateClient(
    clientId: string,
    data: UpdateClientDto,
  ): Promise<OAuthClient> {
    const client = await this.getClient(clientId);

    // Validate redirect URIs if updated
    if (data.redirectUris) {
      for (const uri of data.redirectUris) {
        try {
          const url = new URL(uri);
          const isLocalhost =
            url.hostname === "localhost" || url.hostname === "127.0.0.1";
          const isHttps = url.protocol === "https:";

          if (!isHttps && !isLocalhost) {
            throw new BadRequestException(
              `Redirect URI must use HTTPS: ${uri}`,
            );
          }
        } catch (error) {
          throw new BadRequestException(`Invalid redirect URI: ${uri}`);
        }
      }
    }

    // Update fields
    Object.assign(client, data);

    await this.clientRepository.save(client);

    console.log(`✅ Updated OAuth client: ${client.clientId}`);

    return client;
  }

  /**
   * Rotate client secret
   */
  async rotateClientSecret(clientId: string): Promise<{
    client: OAuthClient;
    clientSecret: string;
  }> {
    const client = await this.getClient(clientId);

    if (client.clientType !== "confidential") {
      throw new BadRequestException("Only confidential clients have secrets");
    }

    // Generate new secret
    const clientSecret = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("base64url");
    const clientSecretHash = await PasswordService.hash(clientSecret);

    // Update client
    client.clientSecretHash = clientSecretHash;
    await this.clientRepository.save(client);

    console.log(`🔄 Rotated client secret: ${client.clientId}`);

    return {
      client,
      clientSecret, // Return plaintext secret only once
    };
  }

  /**
   * Delete client
   */
  async deleteClient(clientId: string): Promise<void> {
    const client = await this.getClient(clientId);

    // Soft delete by marking inactive
    client.isActive = false;
    await this.clientRepository.save(client);

    console.log(`🗑️  Deleted OAuth client: ${client.clientId}`);
  }

  /**
   * List clients for tenant
   */
  async listClients(
    tenantId: string,
    options?: {
      isActive?: boolean;
      isFirstParty?: boolean;
      limit?: number;
      offset?: number;
    },
  ): Promise<{ clients: OAuthClient[]; total: number }> {
    const query = this.clientRepository
      .createQueryBuilder("client")
      .where("client.tenantId = :tenantId", { tenantId });

    if (options?.isActive !== undefined) {
      query.andWhere("client.isActive = :isActive", {
        isActive: options.isActive,
      });
    }

    if (options?.isFirstParty !== undefined) {
      query.andWhere("client.isFirstParty = :isFirstParty", {
        isFirstParty: options.isFirstParty,
      });
    }

    query.orderBy("client.createdAt", "DESC");

    if (options?.limit) {
      query.take(options.limit);
    }

    if (options?.offset) {
      query.skip(options.offset);
    }

    const [clients, total] = await query.getManyAndCount();

    return { clients, total };
  }
}

// apps/auth-service/src/modules/oauth/services/oauth-consent.service.ts

import { Injectable, NotFoundException } from "@nestjs/common";
import { InjectRepository } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { Repository, IsNull } from "typeorm";
import { OAuthConsent, OAuthClient, User } from "@repo/database";

export interface GrantConsentDto {
  tenantId: string;
  clientId: string;
  userId: string;
  grantedScopes: string[];
  rememberConsent?: boolean;
  expiresInDays?: number;
  ipAddress?: string;
  userAgent?: string;
}

@Injectable()
export class OAuthConsentService {
  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(OAuthConsent)
    private consentRepository: Repository<OAuthConsent>,
    @InjectRepository(OAuthClient)
    private clientRepository: Repository<OAuthClient>,
    @InjectRepository(User)
    private userRepository: Repository<User>,
  ) {}

  /**
   * Check if user has granted consent to client for requested scopes
   */
  async hasConsent(
    tenantId: string,
    userId: string,
    clientId: string,
    requestedScopes: string[],
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    const consent = await this.getConsent(tenantId, userId, clientId);

    if (!consent) {
      return false;
    }

    // Check if consent has all requested scopes
    return consent.hasAllScopes(requestedScopes);
  }

  /**
   * Get active consent for user-client pair
   */
  async getConsent(
    tenantId: string,
    userId: string,
    clientId: string,
  ): Promise<OAuthConsent | null> {
    const consent = await this.consentRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        tenantId,
        userId,
        clientId,
        revokedAt: IsNull(),
      },
      relations: ["client", "user"],
    });

    if (!consent) {
      return null;
    }

    // Check if expired
    if (consent.isExpired) {
      return null;
    }

    return consent;
  }

  /**
   * Grant consent
   */
  async grantConsent(data: GrantConsentDto): Promise<OAuthConsent> {
    // Validate client exists
    const client = await this.clientRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: data.clientId },
    });

    if (!client) {
      throw new NotFoundException("Client not found");
    }

    // Validate user exists
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: data.userId },
    });

    if (!user) {
      throw new NotFoundException("User not found");
    }

    // Calculate expiry
    let expiresAt: Date | undefined;
    if (data.expiresInDays && !data.rememberConsent) {
      expiresAt = new Date(
        Date.now() + data.expiresInDays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
      );
    }
    // If rememberConsent = true, expiresAt = null (permanent)

    // Check if consent already exists
    const existingConsent = await this.consentRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        tenantId: data.tenantId,
        userId: data.userId,
        clientId: data.clientId,
      },
    });

    if (existingConsent) {
      // Update existing consent
      existingConsent.grantedScopes = data.grantedScopes;
      existingConsent.rememberConsent = data.rememberConsent || false;
      existingConsent.expiresAt = expiresAt;
      existingConsent.revokedAt = undefined; // Un-revoke if previously revoked
      existingConsent.revokedReason = undefined;
      existingConsent.ipAddress = data.ipAddress;
      existingConsent.userAgent = data.userAgent;

      await this.consentRepository.save(existingConsent);

      console.log(
        `✅ Updated consent: user ${data.userId} → client ${client.clientId}`,
      );

      return existingConsent;
    }

    // Create new consent
    const consent = await this.consentRepository.save({
      tenantId: data.tenantId,
      clientId: data.clientId,
      userId: data.userId,
      grantedScopes: data.grantedScopes,
      rememberConsent: data.rememberConsent || false,
      expiresAt,
      ipAddress: data.ipAddress,
      userAgent: data.userAgent,
    });

    console.log(
      `✅ Granted consent: user ${data.userId} → client ${client.clientId}`,
    );

    return consent;
  }

  /**
   * Revoke consent
   */
  async revokeConsent(
    tenantId: string,
    userId: string,
    clientId: string,
    reason: string = "user_revoked",
  ): Promise<void> {
    const consent = await this.consentRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        tenantId,
        userId,
        clientId,
      },
    });

    if (!consent) {
      return; // Already doesn't exist
    }

    consent.revokedAt = new Date();
    consent.revokedReason = reason;

    await this.consentRepository.save(consent);

    console.log(`🔒 Revoked consent: user ${userId} → client ${clientId}`);
  }

  /**
   * Revoke all consents for user
   */
  async revokeAllUserConsents(
    tenantId: string,
    userId: string,
    reason: string = "user_revoked_all",
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.consentRepository.update(
      {
        tenantId,
        userId,
        revokedAt: IsNull(),
      },
      {
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: reason,
      },
    );

    console.log(`🔒 Revoked all consents for user ${userId}`);
  }

  /**
   * Revoke all consents for client
   */
  async revokeAllClientConsents(
    tenantId: string,
    clientId: string,
    reason: string = "client_deactivated",
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.consentRepository.update(
      {
        tenantId,
        clientId,
        revokedAt: IsNull(),
      },
      {
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: reason,
      },
    );

    console.log(`🔒 Revoked all consents for client ${clientId}`);
  }

  /**
   * List user consents
   */
  async listUserConsents(
    tenantId: string,
    userId: string,
    includeRevoked: boolean = false,
  ): Promise<OAuthConsent[]> {
    const query = this.consentRepository
      .createQueryBuilder("consent")
      .leftJoinAndSelect("consent.client", "client")
      .where("consent.tenantId = :tenantId", { tenantId })
      .andWhere("consent.userId = :userId", { userId });

    if (!includeRevoked) {
      query.andWhere("consent.revokedAt IS NULL");
    }

    query.orderBy("consent.createdAt", "DESC");

    return query.getMany();
  }

  /**
   * List client consents
   */
  async listClientConsents(
    tenantId: string,
    clientId: string,
    includeRevoked: boolean = false,
  ): Promise<OAuthConsent[]> {
    const query = this.consentRepository
      .createQueryBuilder("consent")
      .leftJoinAndSelect("consent.user", "user")
      .where("consent.tenantId = :tenantId", { tenantId })
      .andWhere("consent.clientId = :clientId", { clientId });

    if (!includeRevoked) {
      query.andWhere("consent.revokedAt IS NULL");
    }

    query.orderBy("consent.createdAt", "DESC");

    return query.getMany();
  }

  /**
   * Get consent statistics for client
   */
  async getClientConsentStats(
    tenantId: string,
    clientId: string,
  ): Promise<{
    total: number;
    active: number;
    revoked: number;
    expired: number;
  }> {
    const allConsents = await this.consentRepository.find({
      where: {
        tenantId,
        clientId,
      },
    });

    const stats = {
      total: allConsents.length,
      active: 0,
      revoked: 0,
      expired: 0,
    };

    for (const consent of allConsents) {
      if (consent.revokedAt) {
        stats.revoked++;
      } else if (consent.isExpired) {
        stats.expired++;
      } else {
        stats.active++;
      }
    }

    return stats;
  }

  /**
   * Clean up expired consents
   */
  async cleanupExpiredConsents(): Promise<number> {
    const now = new Date();

    // Soft-delete expired consents (mark as revoked)
    const result = await this.consentRepository
      .createQueryBuilder()
      .update(OAuthConsent)
      .set({
        revokedAt: now,
        revokedReason: "expired",
      })
      .where("expiresAt IS NOT NULL")
      .andWhere("expiresAt < :now", { now })
      .andWhere("revokedAt IS NULL")
      .execute();

    const count = result.affected || 0;

    if (count > 0) {
      console.log(`🧹 Cleaned up ${count} expired consents`);
    }

    return count;
  }

  /**
   * Check if consent is required for client
   */
  async isConsentRequired(
    client: OAuthClient,
    existingConsent?: OAuthConsent,
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    // First-party clients don't need consent
    if (client.isFirstParty) {
      return false;
    }

    // If client doesn't require consent
    if (!client.requireConsent) {
      return false;
    }

    // If user already has valid consent
    if (existingConsent && existingConsent.isValid) {
      return false;
    }

    // Otherwise, consent is required
    return true;
  }
}
