import {
  Injectable,
  UnauthorizedException,
  BadRequestException,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { InjectRepository } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { Repository, IsNull, LessThan } from "typeorm";
import { ConfigService } from "@nestjs/config";
import { JwtService } from "@nestjs/jwt";
import {
  OAuthAuthorizationCode,
  OAuthAccessToken,
  OAuthRefreshToken,
  OAuthClient,
  User,
} from "@repo/database";
import * as crypto from "crypto";
import { v4 as uuidv4 } from "uuid";

export interface CreateAuthorizationCodeDto {
  tenantId: string;
  clientId: string;
  userId: string;
  redirectUri: string;
  scope?: string;
  state?: string;
  nonce?: string;
  codeChallenge?: string;
  codeChallengeMethod?: "S256" | "plain";
  ipAddress?: string;
  userAgent?: string;
}

export interface CreateAccessTokenDto {
  tenantId: string;
  clientId: string;
  userId?: string; // NULL for client_credentials
  sessionId?: string;
  scope?: string;
  audience?: string;
  ipAddress?: string;
  userAgent?: string;
}

export interface CreateRefreshTokenDto {
  tenantId: string;
  clientId: string;
  userId: string;
  accessTokenId?: string;
  familyId?: string; // For rotation
  parentTokenId?: string; // For rotation
  scope?: string;
  deviceId?: string;
  ipAddress?: string;
  userAgent?: string;
}

@Injectable()
export class OAuthTokenService {
  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(OAuthAuthorizationCode)
    private authCodeRepository: Repository<OAuthAuthorizationCode>,
    @InjectRepository(OAuthAccessToken)
    private accessTokenRepository: Repository<OAuthAccessToken>,
    @InjectRepository(OAuthRefreshToken)
    private refreshTokenRepository: Repository<OAuthRefreshToken>,
    @InjectRepository(OAuthClient)
    private clientRepository: Repository<OAuthClient>,
    @InjectRepository(User)
    private userRepository: Repository<User>,
    private jwtService: JwtService,
    private configService: ConfigService,
  ) {}

  // ============================================================
  // AUTHORIZATION CODES
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Create authorization code
   * Returns plaintext code (opaque, random string)
   */
  async createAuthorizationCode(
    data: CreateAuthorizationCodeDto,
  ): Promise<string> {
    const client = await this.clientRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: data.clientId },
    });

    if (!client) {
      throw new BadRequestException("Invalid client");
    }

    // Generate random code
    const code = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("base64url");
    const codeHash = crypto.createHash("sha256").update(code).digest("hex");

    // Calculate expiry
    const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + client.authorizationCodeTtl * 1000);

    // Save code
    await this.authCodeRepository.save({
      tenantId: data.tenantId,
      clientId: data.clientId,
      userId: data.userId,
      codeHash,
      redirectUri: data.redirectUri,
      scope: data.scope,
      state: data.state,
      nonce: data.nonce,
      codeChallenge: data.codeChallenge,
      codeChallengeMethod: data.codeChallengeMethod,
      expiresAt,
      ipAddress: data.ipAddress,
      userAgent: data.userAgent,
    });

    console.log(`✅ Created authorization code for user ${data.userId}`);

    return code; // Return plaintext code
  }

  /**
   * Validate and consume authorization code
   */
  async validateAuthorizationCode(
    code: string,
    clientId: string,
    redirectUri: string,
    codeVerifier?: string,
  ): Promise<OAuthAuthorizationCode> {
    const codeHash = crypto.createHash("sha256").update(code).digest("hex");

    const authCode = await this.authCodeRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        codeHash,
        clientId,
        usedAt: IsNull(),
      },
      relations: ["client", "user"],
    });

    if (!authCode) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid authorization code");
    }

    // Check expiry
    if (authCode.expiresAt < new Date()) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Authorization code expired");
    }

    // Check redirect URI matches
    if (authCode.redirectUri !== redirectUri) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Redirect URI mismatch");
    }

    // Validate PKCE if present
    if (authCode.codeChallenge) {
      if (!codeVerifier) {
        throw new UnauthorizedException("code_verifier required for PKCE");
      }

      const isValid = await this.validatePKCE(
        codeVerifier,
        authCode.codeChallenge,
        authCode.codeChallengeMethod || "S256",
      );

      if (!isValid) {
        throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid code_verifier");
      }
    }

    // Mark as used
    authCode.usedAt = new Date();
    await this.authCodeRepository.save(authCode);

    console.log(`✅ Consumed authorization code for user ${authCode.userId}`);

    return authCode;
  }

  /**
   * Validate PKCE code verifier
   */
  async validatePKCE(
    codeVerifier: string,
    codeChallenge: string,
    method: "S256" | "plain",
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    let computedChallenge: string;

    if (method === "S256") {
      computedChallenge = crypto
        .createHash("sha256")
        .update(codeVerifier)
        .digest("base64url");
    } else if (method === "plain") {
      computedChallenge = codeVerifier;
    } else {
      throw new BadRequestException("Invalid code_challenge_method");
    }

    return computedChallenge === codeChallenge;
  }

  // ============================================================
  // ACCESS TOKENS
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Create access token (JWT)
   */
  async createAccessToken(
    data: CreateAccessTokenDto,
    user?: User,
  ): Promise<string> {
    const client = await this.clientRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: data.clientId },
    });

    if (!client) {
      throw new BadRequestException("Invalid client");
    }

    // Generate JTI (JWT ID)
    const jti = uuidv4();
    const tokenHash = crypto.createHash("sha256").update(jti).digest("hex");

    // Calculate expiry
    const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + client.accessTokenTtl * 1000);
    const exp = Math.floor(expiresAt.getTime() / 1000);
    const iat = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

    // Build JWT payload
    const payload: any = {
      iss: this.configService.get("JWT_ISSUER", "https://auth.fortknox.io"),
      sub: data.userId || data.clientId, // User ID or client ID for M2M
      aud: data.audience || data.clientId,
      exp,
      iat,
      jti,
      client_id: client.clientId,
      tenant_id: data.tenantId,
      token_type: "access_token",
    };

    // Add scope if present
    if (data.scope) {
      payload.scope = data.scope;
    }

    // Add session ID if present
    if (data.sessionId) {
      payload.session_id = data.sessionId;
    }

    // Add user claims for user tokens
    if (user) {
      payload.email = user.email;
      payload.email_verified = user.emailVerified;
    }

    // Sign JWT
    const accessToken = this.jwtService.sign(payload, {
      secret: this.configService.get("JWT_SECRET"),
      expiresIn: client.accessTokenTtl,
    });

    // Store token record (for revocation tracking)
    await this.accessTokenRepository.save({
      tenantId: data.tenantId,
      clientId: data.clientId,
      userId: data.userId,
      sessionId: data.sessionId,
      tokenHash,
      scope: data.scope,
      audience: data.audience,
      expiresAt,
      ipAddress: data.ipAddress,
      userAgent: data.userAgent,
    });

    console.log(`✅ Created access token for ${data.userId || "M2M"}`);

    return accessToken;
  }

  /**
   * Validate access token
   */
  async validateAccessToken(token: string): Promise<OAuthAccessToken> {
    // Verify JWT signature
    let payload: any;
    try {
      payload = this.jwtService.verify(token, {
        secret: this.configService.get("JWT_SECRET"),
      });
    } catch (error) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid access token");
    }

    // Look up token in database (for revocation check)
    const jti = payload.jti;
    const tokenHash = crypto.createHash("sha256").update(jti).digest("hex");

    const accessToken = await this.accessTokenRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        tokenHash,
        revokedAt: IsNull(),
      },
    });

    if (!accessToken) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Access token revoked or invalid");
    }

    // Check expiry
    if (accessToken.expiresAt < new Date()) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Access token expired");
    }

    // Update last used
    accessToken.lastUsedAt = new Date();
    accessToken.useCount += 1;
    await this.accessTokenRepository.save(accessToken);

    return accessToken;
  }

  /**
   * Revoke access token
   */
  async revokeAccessToken(
    token: string,
    reason: string = "user_revoked",
  ): Promise<void> {
    // Decode without verifying (to get JTI)
    const payload = this.jwtService.decode(token) as any;

    if (!payload || !payload.jti) {
      return; // Invalid token, nothing to revoke
    }

    const jti = payload.jti;
    const tokenHash = crypto.createHash("sha256").update(jti).digest("hex");

    await this.accessTokenRepository.update(
      { tokenHash },
      {
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: reason,
      },
    );

    console.log(`🔒 Revoked access token: ${reason}`);
  }

  // ============================================================
  // REFRESH TOKENS
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Create refresh token (opaque)
   */
  async createRefreshToken(data: CreateRefreshTokenDto): Promise<string> {
    const client = await this.clientRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: data.clientId },
    });

    if (!client) {
      throw new BadRequestException("Invalid client");
    }

    // Generate random token
    const token = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString("base64url");
    const tokenHash = crypto.createHash("sha256").update(token).digest("hex");

    // Generate family ID if not provided (new family)
    const familyId = data.familyId || uuidv4();

    // Calculate expiry
    const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + client.refreshTokenTtl * 1000);

    // Save token
    await this.refreshTokenRepository.save({
      tenantId: data.tenantId,
      clientId: data.clientId,
      userId: data.userId,
      accessTokenId: data.accessTokenId,
      tokenHash,
      familyId,
      parentTokenId: data.parentTokenId,
      scope: data.scope,
      expiresAt,
      deviceId: data.deviceId,
      ipAddress: data.ipAddress,
      userAgent: data.userAgent,
    });

    console.log(`✅ Created refresh token for user ${data.userId}`);

    return token; // Return plaintext token
  }

  /**
   * Validate refresh token
   */
  async validateRefreshToken(token: string): Promise<OAuthRefreshToken> {
    const tokenHash = crypto.createHash("sha256").update(token).digest("hex");

    const refreshToken = await this.refreshTokenRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        tokenHash,
        revokedAt: IsNull(),
      },
      relations: ["client", "user"],
    });

    if (!refreshToken) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Invalid refresh token");
    }

    // Check expiry
    if (refreshToken.expiresAt < new Date()) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException("Refresh token expired");
    }

    // REUSE DETECTION
    if (refreshToken.usedAt) {
      // Token already used! Security breach!
      console.error("🚨 REFRESH TOKEN REUSE DETECTED", {
        tokenId: refreshToken.id,
        familyId: refreshToken.familyId,
        userId: refreshToken.userId,
      });

      // Revoke entire family
      await this.revokeRefreshTokenFamily(
        refreshToken.familyId,
        "token_reuse_detected",
      );

      throw new UnauthorizedException(
        "Token reuse detected - all sessions revoked",
      );
    }

    return refreshToken;
  }

  /**
   * Rotate refresh token (exchange old for new)
   */
  async rotateRefreshToken(oldToken: OAuthRefreshToken): Promise<string> {
    // Mark old token as used
    oldToken.usedAt = new Date();
    oldToken.revokedAt = new Date();
    oldToken.revokedReason = "rotated";
    await this.refreshTokenRepository.save(oldToken);

    // Create new token in same family
    const newToken = await this.createRefreshToken({
      tenantId: oldToken.tenantId,
      clientId: oldToken.clientId,
      userId: oldToken.userId,
      familyId: oldToken.familyId, // Same family
      parentTokenId: oldToken.id, // Track rotation chain
      scope: oldToken.scope,
      deviceId: oldToken.deviceId,
      ipAddress: oldToken.ipAddress,
      userAgent: oldToken.userAgent,
    });

    console.log(`🔄 Rotated refresh token for user ${oldToken.userId}`);

    return newToken;
  }

  /**
   * Revoke refresh token
   */
  async revokeRefreshToken(
    token: string,
    reason: string = "user_revoked",
  ): Promise<void> {
    const tokenHash = crypto.createHash("sha256").update(token).digest("hex");

    await this.refreshTokenRepository.update(
      { tokenHash },
      {
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: reason,
      },
    );

    console.log(`🔒 Revoked refresh token: ${reason}`);
  }

  /**
   * Revoke entire refresh token family (reuse detection)
   */
  async revokeRefreshTokenFamily(
    familyId: string,
    reason: string = "token_reuse_detected",
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.refreshTokenRepository.update(
      {
        familyId,
        revokedAt: IsNull(),
      },
      {
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: reason,
      },
    );

    console.log(`🚨 Revoked token family: ${familyId} (${reason})`);
  }

  /**
   * Revoke all refresh tokens for user
   */
  async revokeAllUserRefreshTokens(
    userId: string,
    tenantId: string,
    reason: string = "logout_all",
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.refreshTokenRepository.update(
      {
        userId,
        tenantId,
        revokedAt: IsNull(),
      },
      {
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: reason,
      },
    );

    console.log(`🔒 Revoked all refresh tokens for user ${userId}`);
  }

  /**
   * Revoke all tokens for client
   */
  async revokeAllClientTokens(
    clientId: string,
    tenantId: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    // Revoke access tokens
    await this.accessTokenRepository.update(
      {
        clientId,
        tenantId,
        revokedAt: IsNull(),
      },
      {
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: "client_revoked",
      },
    );

    // Revoke refresh tokens
    await this.refreshTokenRepository.update(
      {
        clientId,
        tenantId,
        revokedAt: IsNull(),
      },
      {
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: "client_revoked",
      },
    );

    console.log(`🔒 Revoked all tokens for client ${clientId}`);
  }

  // ============================================================
  // ID TOKENS (OIDC)
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Create ID token (JWT with user claims)
   */
  async createIDToken(
    user: User,
    client: OAuthClient,
    nonce?: string,
  ): Promise<string> {
    const exp = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + client.idTokenTtl;
    const iat = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

    // Build ID token payload (OIDC standard claims)
    const payload: any = {
      iss: this.configService.get("JWT_ISSUER", "https://auth.fortknox.io"),
      sub: user.id,
      aud: client.clientId,
      exp,
      iat,

      // Standard OIDC claims
      email: user.email,
      email_verified: user.emailVerified,
      name: `${user.firstName} ${user.lastName}`.trim(),
      given_name: user.firstName,
      family_name: user.lastName,

      // Include nonce for replay protection
      nonce,
    };

    // Add optional claims
    if (user.avatarUrl) {
      payload.picture = user.avatarUrl;
    }

    // Sign ID token
    const idToken = this.jwtService.sign(payload, {
      secret: this.configService.get("JWT_SECRET"),
      expiresIn: client.idTokenTtl,
    });

    console.log(`✅ Created ID token for user ${user.id}`);

    return idToken;
  }

  // ============================================================
  // CLEANUP
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Clean up expired tokens
   */
  async cleanupExpiredTokens(): Promise<void> {
    const now = new Date();

    // Delete expired authorization codes (older than 1 day)
    const oldCodeDate = new Date(now.getTime() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    const deletedCodes = await this.authCodeRepository.delete({
      expiresAt: LessThan(oldCodeDate),
    });

    // Delete expired access tokens (older than 1 day)
    const deletedAccessTokens = await this.accessTokenRepository.delete({
      expiresAt: LessThan(oldCodeDate),
    });

    // Delete expired refresh tokens (older than 30 days)
    const oldRefreshDate = new Date(now.getTime() - 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    const deletedRefreshTokens = await this.refreshTokenRepository.delete({
      expiresAt: LessThan(oldRefreshDate),
    });

    console.log(`🧹 Cleaned up expired tokens:`, {
      codes: deletedCodes.affected || 0,
      accessTokens: deletedAccessTokens.affected || 0,
      refreshTokens: deletedRefreshTokens.affected || 0,
    });
  }

  // ============================================================
  // INTROSPECTION
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Introspect token (get metadata)
   */
  async introspectToken(
    token: string,
    tokenTypeHint?: "access_token" | "refresh_token",
  ): Promise<any> {
    // Try as JWT first (access token)
    try {
      const payload = this.jwtService.verify(token, {
        secret: this.configService.get("JWT_SECRET"),
      });

      const jti = payload.jti;
      const tokenHash = crypto.createHash("sha256").update(jti).digest("hex");

      const accessToken = await this.accessTokenRepository.findOne({
        where: { tokenHash },
      });

      if (!accessToken || accessToken.revokedAt) {
        return { active: false };
      }

      return {
        active: true,
        scope: accessToken.scope,
        client_id: payload.client_id,
        username: payload.email,
        token_type: "Bearer",
        exp: payload.exp,
        iat: payload.iat,
        sub: payload.sub,
        aud: payload.aud,
      };
    } catch {
      // Not a JWT, try as refresh token
      const tokenHash = crypto.createHash("sha256").update(token).digest("hex");

      const refreshToken = await this.refreshTokenRepository.findOne({
        where: { tokenHash },
        relations: ["client", "user"],
      });

      if (
        !refreshToken ||
        refreshToken.revokedAt ||
        refreshToken.expiresAt < new Date()
      ) {
        return { active: false };
      }

      return {
        active: true,
        scope: refreshToken.scope,
        client_id: refreshToken.client.clientId,
        username: refreshToken.user.email,
        token_type: "refresh_token",
        exp: Math.floor(refreshToken.expiresAt.getTime() / 1000),
        sub: refreshToken.userId,
      };
    }
  }
}

// apps/auth-service/src/modules/rbac/decorators/rbac.decorators.ts

import { SetMetadata } from "@nestjs/common";
import { PERMISSIONS_KEY } from "../guards/permissions.guard";
import { ROLES_KEY } from "../guards/roles.guard";

/**
 * Decorator to require specific permissions
 *
 * @example
 * @RequirePermissions('users:write', 'users:delete')
 * @UseGuards(JwtGuard, PermissionsGuard)
 * async deleteUser() { ... }
 */
export const RequirePermissions = (...permissions: string[]) =>
  SetMetadata(PERMISSIONS_KEY, permissions);

/**
 * Decorator to require specific roles
 *
 * @example
 * @RequireRoles('admin', 'developer')
 * @UseGuards(JwtGuard, RolesGuard)
 * async manageSettings() { ... }
 */
export const RequireRoles = (...roles: string[]) =>
  SetMetadata(ROLES_KEY, roles);

/**
 * Decorator to require admin role
 *
 * @example
 * @RequireAdmin()
 * @UseGuards(JwtGuard, RolesGuard)
 * async deleteAll() { ... }
 */
export const RequireAdmin = () =>
  SetMetadata(ROLES_KEY, ["admin", "super_admin"]);

// apps/auth-service/src/modules/rbac/guards/permissions.guard.ts

import {
  Injectable,
  CanActivate,
  ExecutionContext,
  ForbiddenException,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { Reflector } from "@nestjs/core";
import { RbacService } from "../services/rbac.service";

export const PERMISSIONS_KEY = "permissions";

/**
 * Guard to check if user has required permissions
 *
 * Usage:
 * @RequirePermissions('users:write', 'users:delete')
 * @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
 * async deleteUser() { ... }
 */
@Injectable()
export class PermissionsGuard implements CanActivate {
  constructor(
    private reflector: Reflector,
    private rbacService: RbacService,
  ) {}

  async canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): Promise<boolean> {
    // Get required permissions from decorator
    const requiredPermissions = this.reflector.getAllAndOverride<string[]>(
      PERMISSIONS_KEY,
      [context.getHandler(), context.getClass()],
    );

    // If no permissions required, allow access
    if (!requiredPermissions || requiredPermissions.length === 0) {
      return true;
    }

    // Get request and user
    const request = context.switchToHttp().getRequest();
    const user = request.user; // From JwtGuard
    const tenantId = request.tenantId; // From TenantMiddleware

    if (!user || !user.sub) {
      throw new ForbiddenException("User not authenticated");
    }

    if (!tenantId) {
      throw new ForbiddenException("Tenant context not found");
    }

    // Check if user has ALL required permissions
    const hasPermissions = await this.rbacService.userHasAllPermissions(
      user.sub,
      tenantId,
      requiredPermissions,
    );

    if (!hasPermissions) {
      throw new ForbiddenException(
        `Missing required permissions: ${requiredPermissions.join(", ")}`,
      );
    }

    return true;
  }
}

// apps/auth-service/src/modules/rbac/guards/roles.guard.ts

import {
  Injectable,
  CanActivate,
  ExecutionContext,
  ForbiddenException,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { Reflector } from "@nestjs/core";
import { RbacService } from "../services/rbac.service";

export const ROLES_KEY = "roles";

/**
 * Guard to check if user has required roles
 *
 * Usage:
 * @RequireRoles('admin', 'developer')
 * @UseGuards(RolesGuard)
 * async manageSettings() { ... }
 */
@Injectable()
export class RolesGuard implements CanActivate {
  constructor(
    private reflector: Reflector,
    private rbacService: RbacService,
  ) {}

  async canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): Promise<boolean> {
    // Get required roles from decorator
    const requiredRoles = this.reflector.getAllAndOverride<string[]>(
      ROLES_KEY,
      [context.getHandler(), context.getClass()],
    );

    // If no roles required, allow access
    if (!requiredRoles || requiredRoles.length === 0) {
      return true;
    }

    // Get request and user
    const request = context.switchToHttp().getRequest();
    const user = request.user; // From JwtGuard
    const tenantId = request.tenantId; // From TenantMiddleware

    if (!user || !user.sub) {
      throw new ForbiddenException("User not authenticated");
    }

    if (!tenantId) {
      throw new ForbiddenException("Tenant context not found");
    }

    // Check if user has ANY of the required roles
    const hasRole = await this.rbacService.userHasAnyRole(
      user.sub,
      tenantId,
      requiredRoles,
    );

    if (!hasRole) {
      throw new ForbiddenException(
        `Missing required role. Required one of: ${requiredRoles.join(", ")}`,
      );
    }

    return true;
  }
}

import {
  Controller,
  Get,
  Post,
  Put,
  Delete,
  Body,
  Param,
  Query,
  UseGuards,
  Request,
  HttpCode,
  HttpStatus,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import {
  ApiTags,
  ApiOperation,
  ApiResponse,
  ApiBearerAuth,
} from "@nestjs/swagger";
import { PermissionsGuard } from "./guards/permissions.guard";
import { RolesGuard } from "./guards/roles.guard";
import {
  RequirePermissions,
  RequireRoles,
  RequireAdmin,
} from "./decorators/rbac.decorator";
import { RbacService } from "./services/rbac.service";
import { RoleService } from "./services/role.service";
import { PermissionService } from "./services/permission.service";
import {
  CreatePermissionDto,
  UpdatePermissionDto,
  CreateRoleDto,
  UpdateRoleDto,
  AssignPermissionsDto,
  RemovePermissionsDto,
  AssignRoleDto,
  AssignRolesByNameDto,
  SetUserRolesDto,
  CheckPermissionDto,
  CheckRoleDto,
} from "./dto/rbac.dto";
import { JwtAuthGuard } from "@/common/guards";

@ApiTags("RBAC")
@Controller("rbac")
@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)
@ApiBearerAuth()
export class RbacController {
  constructor(
    private rbacService: RbacService,
    private roleService: RoleService,
    private permissionService: PermissionService,
  ) {}

  // ============================================================
  // PERMISSION ENDPOINTS
  // ============================================================

  @Get("permissions")
  @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("permissions:read")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "List all permissions" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Permissions retrieved" })
  async listPermissions(@Request() req: any) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    return this.permissionService.listPermissions(tenantId);
  }

  @Post("permissions")
  @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("permissions:write")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Create permission" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 201, description: "Permission created" })
  async createPermission(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Body() dto: CreatePermissionDto,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    return this.permissionService.createPermission({
      tenantId,
      ...dto,
    });
  }

  @Get("permissions/:id")
  @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("permissions:read")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Get permission by ID" })
  async getPermission(@Request() req: any, @Param("id") id: string) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    return this.permissionService.getPermission(id, tenantId);
  }

  @Put("permissions/:id")
  @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("permissions:write")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Update permission" })
  async updatePermission(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Param("id") id: string,
    @Body() dto: UpdatePermissionDto,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    return this.permissionService.updatePermission(id, tenantId, dto);
  }

  @Delete("permissions/:id")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT)
  @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("permissions:write")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Delete permission" })
  async deletePermission(@Request() req: any, @Param("id") id: string) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    await this.permissionService.deletePermission(id, tenantId);
  }

  // ============================================================
  // ROLE ENDPOINTS
  // ============================================================

  @Get("roles")
  @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("roles:read")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "List all roles" })
  async listRoles(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Query("loadPermissions") loadPermissions?: string,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    return this.roleService.listRoles(tenantId, {
      loadPermissions: loadPermissions === "true",
    });
  }

  @Post("roles")
  @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("roles:write")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Create role" })
  async createRole(@Request() req: any, @Body() dto: CreateRoleDto) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    const userId = req.user.sub;
    return this.roleService.createRole({
      tenantId,
      ...dto,
      createdBy: userId,
    });
  }

  @Get("roles/:id")
  @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("roles:read")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Get role by ID" })
  async getRole(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Param("id") id: string,
    @Query("loadPermissions") loadPermissions?: string,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    return this.roleService.getRole(id, tenantId, loadPermissions === "true");
  }

  @Put("roles/:id")
  @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("roles:write")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Update role" })
  async updateRole(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Param("id") id: string,
    @Body() dto: UpdateRoleDto,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    return this.roleService.updateRole(id, tenantId, dto);
  }

  @Delete("roles/:id")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT)
  @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("roles:write")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Delete role" })
  async deleteRole(@Request() req: any, @Param("id") id: string) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    await this.roleService.deleteRole(id, tenantId);
  }

  // ============================================================

  @Post("roles/:id/permissions")
  @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("roles:write")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Assign permissions to role" })
  async assignPermissionsToRole(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Param("id") id: string,
    @Body() dto: AssignPermissionsDto,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    const userId = req.user.sub;
    await this.roleService.assignPermissionsToRole(
      id,
      tenantId,
      dto.permissionNames,
      userId,
    );
    return { message: "Permissions assigned successfully" };
  }

  @Delete("roles/:id/permissions")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("roles:write")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Remove permissions from role" })

    @Request() req: any,

  ) {

    );
    return { message: "Permissions removed successfully" };
  }

  @Get("roles/:id/permissions")
  @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("roles:read")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Get role permissions" })
  async getRolePermissions(@Request() req: any, @Param("id") id: string) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    return this.roleService.getRolePermissions(id, tenantId);
  }

  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    const assignedBy = req.user.sub;

    await this.rbacService.assignRolesByNames({
      userId,
      tenantId,
      roleNames: dto.roleNames,
      assignedBy,
      expiresAt: dto.expiresAt ? new Date(dto.expiresAt) : undefined,
      reason: dto.reason,
    });

    return { message: "Roles assigned successfully" };
  }

  @Put("users/:userId/roles")

  @RequirePermissions("users:write")

  async getUserPermissions(

  }

  @Post("users/:userId/check-permission")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Check if user has permission" })
  async checkPermission(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Param("userId") userId: string,
    @Body() dto: CheckPermissionDto,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    const hasPermission = await this.rbacService.userHasPermission(
      userId,
      tenantId,
      dto.permission,
    );
    return { hasPermission };

  @Post("users/:userId/check-role")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Check if user has role" })
  async checkRole(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Param("userId") userId: string,
    @Body() dto: CheckRoleDto,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    const hasRole = await this.rbacService.userHasRole(
      userId,
      tenantId,
      dto.role,
    );
    return { hasRole };
  }

  // ============================================================

  // ============================================================

  @Get("me/roles")

  }

  @Post("me/check-permission")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Check if I have permission" })
  async checkMyPermission(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Body() dto: CheckPermissionDto,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    const userId = req.user.sub;
    const hasPermission = await this.rbacService.userHasPermission(
      userId,
      tenantId,
      dto.permission,
    );
    return { hasPermission };
  }

  // ============================================================
  // ADMIN ENDPOINTS
  // ============================================================

  @Post("initialize")
  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)
  @RequireAdmin()
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Initialize RBAC for tenant (create default permissions & roles)",
  })
  async initializeTenant(@Request() req: any) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    const userId = req.user.sub;
    await this.rbacService.initializeTenant(tenantId, userId);
    return { message: "RBAC initialized successfully" };
  }

  @Post("cleanup-expired")
  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)
  @RequireAdmin()
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Remove expired role assignments" })
  async cleanupExpiredRoles(@Request() req: any) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    const count = await this.rbacService.cleanupExpiredRoles(tenantId);
    return { message: `Removed ${count} expired role assignments` };
  }
}

import {
  Permission,
  Role,
  RolePermission,
  UserRole,
  User,
} from "@repo/database";

import { RolesGuard } from "./guards/roles.guard";

  imports: [
    TypeOrmModule.forFeature([
      Permission,
      Role,
      RolePermission,
      UserRole,
      User,
    ]),

  providers: [
    RbacService,
    RoleService,
    PermissionService,
    PermissionsGuard,
    RolesGuard,
  ],

    RbacService,

    PermissionService,

// apps/auth-service/src/modules/rbac/services/permission.service.ts

import {
  Injectable,
  NotFoundException,
  ConflictException,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { InjectRepository } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { Repository } from "typeorm";
import { Permission } from "@repo/database";

export interface CreatePermissionDto {
  tenantId: string;
  name: string;
  resource: string;
  action: string;
  description?: string;
  scope?: string;
  isSystem?: boolean;
  metadata?: Record<string, any>;
}

export interface UpdatePermissionDto {
  description?: string;
  metadata?: Record<string, any>;
}

@Injectable()
export class PermissionService {
  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(Permission)
    private permissionRepository: Repository<Permission>,
  ) {}

  /**
   * Create a new permission
   */
  async createPermission(data: CreatePermissionDto): Promise<Permission> {
    // Build permission name if not provided
    const name = data.name || `${data.resource}:${data.action}`;

    // Check if permission already exists
    const existing = await this.permissionRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        tenantId: data.tenantId,
        name,
      },
    });

    if (existing) {
      throw new ConflictException(`Permission ${name} already exists`);
    }

    const permission = await this.permissionRepository.save({
      tenantId: data.tenantId,
      name,
      resource: data.resource,
      action: data.action,
      description: data.description,
      scope: data.scope || "tenant",
      isSystem: data.isSystem || false,
      metadata: data.metadata,
    });

    console.log(`✅ Created permission: ${permission.name}`);

    return permission;
  }

  /**
   * Bulk create permissions
   */
  async bulkCreatePermissions(
    tenantId: string,
    permissions: Array<{
      resource: string;
      action: string;
      description?: string;
    }>,
  ): Promise<Permission[]> {
    const created: Permission[] = [];

    for (const perm of permissions) {
      try {
        const permission = await this.createPermission({
          tenantId,
          name: `${perm.resource}:${perm.action}`,
          resource: perm.resource,
          action: perm.action,
          description: perm.description,
        });
        created.push(permission);
      } catch (error) {
        // Skip if already exists
        if (error instanceof ConflictException) {
          console.log(
            `⏭️  Skipping existing permission: ${perm.resource}:${perm.action}`,
          );
        } else {
          throw error;
        }
      }
    }

    return created;
  }

  /**
   * Get permission by ID
   */
  async getPermission(id: string, tenantId: string): Promise<Permission> {
    const permission = await this.permissionRepository.findOne({
      where: { id, tenantId },
    });

    if (!permission) {
      throw new NotFoundException("Permission not found");
    }

    return permission;
  }

  /**
   * Get permission by name
   */
  async getPermissionByName(
    name: string,
    tenantId: string,
  ): Promise<Permission | null> {
    return this.permissionRepository.findOne({
      where: { name, tenantId },
    });
  }

  /**
   * List all permissions for tenant
   */
  async listPermissions(
    tenantId: string,
    options?: {
      resource?: string;
      action?: string;
      scope?: string;
      isSystem?: boolean;
    },
  ): Promise<Permission[]> {
    const query = this.permissionRepository
      .createQueryBuilder("permission")
      .where("permission.tenantId = :tenantId", { tenantId });

    if (options?.resource) {
      query.andWhere("permission.resource = :resource", {
        resource: options.resource,
      });
    }

    if (options?.action) {
      query.andWhere("permission.action = :action", { action: options.action });
    }

    if (options?.scope) {
      query.andWhere("permission.scope = :scope", { scope: options.scope });
    }

    if (options?.isSystem !== undefined) {
      query.andWhere("permission.isSystem = :isSystem", {
        isSystem: options.isSystem,
      });
    }

    query
      .orderBy("permission.resource", "ASC")
      .addOrderBy("permission.action", "ASC");

    return query.getMany();
  }

  /**
   * Get permissions grouped by resource
   */
  async getPermissionsByResource(
    tenantId: string,
  ): Promise<Record<string, Permission[]>> {
    const permissions = await this.listPermissions(tenantId);

    const grouped: Record<string, Permission[]> = {};

    for (const permission of permissions) {
      if (!grouped[permission.resource]) {
        grouped[permission.resource] = [];
      }
      grouped[permission.resource]?.push(permission);
    }

    return grouped;
  }

  /**
   * Update permission
   */
  async updatePermission(
    id: string,
    tenantId: string,
    data: UpdatePermissionDto,
  ): Promise<Permission> {
    const permission = await this.getPermission(id, tenantId);

    if (permission.isSystem) {
      throw new ConflictException("Cannot update system permission");
    }

    Object.assign(permission, data);

    await this.permissionRepository.save(permission);

    console.log(`✅ Updated permission: ${permission.name}`);

    return permission;
  }

  /**
   * Delete permission
   */
  async deletePermission(id: string, tenantId: string): Promise<void> {
    const permission = await this.getPermission(id, tenantId);

    if (permission.isSystem) {
      throw new ConflictException("Cannot delete system permission");
    }

    await this.permissionRepository.remove(permission);

    console.log(`🗑️  Deleted permission: ${permission.name}`);
  }

  /**
   * Check if permissions exist
   */
  async permissionsExist(
    permissionNames: string[],
    tenantId: string,
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    const count = await this.permissionRepository.count({
      where: permissionNames.map((name) => ({ name, tenantId })),
    });

    return count === permissionNames.length;
  }

  /**
   * Get permissions by names
   */
  async getPermissionsByNames(
    permissionNames: string[],
    tenantId: string,
  ): Promise<Permission[]> {
    if (permissionNames.length === 0) {
      return [];
    }

    return this.permissionRepository
      .createQueryBuilder("permission")
      .where("permission.tenantId = :tenantId", { tenantId })
      .andWhere("permission.name IN (:...names)", { names: permissionNames })
      .getMany();
  }

  /**
   * Seed default permissions for a tenant
   */
  async seedDefaultPermissions(tenantId: string): Promise<Permission[]> {
    const defaultPermissions = [
      // User permissions
      { resource: "users", action: "read", description: "View users" },
      {
        resource: "users",
        action: "write",
        description: "Create/update users",
      },
      { resource: "users", action: "delete", description: "Delete users" },
      { resource: "users", action: "*", description: "All user permissions" },

      // Role permissions
      { resource: "roles", action: "read", description: "View roles" },
      {
        resource: "roles",
        action: "write",
        description: "Create/update roles",
      },
      { resource: "roles", action: "delete", description: "Delete roles" },
      { resource: "roles", action: "*", description: "All role permissions" },

      // Permission permissions
      {
        resource: "permissions",
        action: "read",
        description: "View permissions",
      },
      {
        resource: "permissions",
        action: "write",
        description: "Manage permissions",
      },

      // OAuth client permissions
      {
        resource: "clients",
        action: "read",
        description: "View OAuth clients",
      },
      {
        resource: "clients",
        action: "write",
        description: "Create/update clients",
      },
      { resource: "clients", action: "delete", description: "Delete clients" },
      {
        resource: "clients",
        action: "*",
        description: "All client permissions",
      },

      // API permissions
      { resource: "api", action: "read", description: "Read API resources" },
      { resource: "api", action: "write", description: "Write API resources" },

      // Profile permissions
      { resource: "profile", action: "read", description: "View own profile" },
      {
        resource: "profile",
        action: "write",
        description: "Update own profile",
      },

      // Super admin wildcard
      {
        resource: "*",
        action: "*",
        description: "All permissions (super admin)",
      },
    ];

    return this.bulkCreatePermissions(tenantId, defaultPermissions);
  }
}

import {
  Injectable,
  NotFoundException,
  ConflictException,
  BadRequestException,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { InjectRepository } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { Repository, In } from "typeorm";
import { User, Role, Permission, UserRole } from "@repo/database";
import { RoleService } from "./role.service";
import { PermissionService } from "./permission.service";

export interface AssignRoleDto {
  userId: string;
  roleId: string;
  tenantId: string;
  assignedBy?: string;
  expiresAt?: Date;
  reason?: string;
  metadata?: Record<string, any>;
}

export interface AssignRolesByNameDto {
  userId: string;
  roleNames: string[];
  tenantId: string;
  assignedBy?: string;
  expiresAt?: Date;
  reason?: string;
}

@Injectable()
export class RbacService {
  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(User)
    private userRepository: Repository<User>,
    @InjectRepository(UserRole)
    private userRoleRepository: Repository<UserRole>,
    private roleService: RoleService,
    private permissionService: PermissionService,
  ) {}

  // ============================================================
  // USER-ROLE ASSIGNMENTS
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Assign role to user
   */
  async assignRole(data: AssignRoleDto): Promise<UserRole> {
    // Verify user exists
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: data.userId, tenantId: data.tenantId },
    });

    if (!user) {
      throw new NotFoundException("User not found");
    }

    // Verify role exists
    const role = await this.roleService.getRole(data.roleId, data.tenantId);

    // Check if already assigned
    const existing = await this.userRoleRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        userId: data.userId,
        roleId: data.roleId,
        tenantId: data.tenantId,
      },
    });

    if (existing) {
      // Update expiry if provided
      if (data.expiresAt) {
        existing.expiresAt = data.expiresAt;
        existing.reason = data.reason;
        existing.metadata = data.metadata;
        await this.userRoleRepository.save(existing);
        console.log(
          `✅ Updated role assignment: ${role.name} for user ${user.email}`,
        );
        return existing;
      }

      throw new ConflictException("Role already assigned to user");
    }

    // Create assignment
    const userRole = await this.userRoleRepository.save({
      tenantId: data.tenantId,
      userId: data.userId,
      roleId: data.roleId,
      assignedBy: data.assignedBy,
      assignedAt: new Date(),
      expiresAt: data.expiresAt,
      reason: data.reason,
      metadata: data.metadata,
    });

    console.log(`✅ Assigned role ${role.name} to user ${user.email}`);

    return userRole;
  }

  /**
   * Assign multiple roles to user by role names
   */
  async assignRolesByNames(data: AssignRolesByNameDto): Promise<UserRole[]> {
    // Get roles by names
    const roles = await this.roleService.listRoles(data.tenantId);
    const roleMap = new Map(roles.map((r) => [r.name, r]));

    const assignments: UserRole[] = [];

    for (const roleName of data.roleNames) {
      const role = roleMap.get(roleName);
      if (!role) {
        throw new NotFoundException(`Role not found: ${roleName}`);
      }

      try {
        const userRole = await this.assignRole({
          userId: data.userId,
          roleId: role.id,
          tenantId: data.tenantId,
          assignedBy: data.assignedBy,
          expiresAt: data.expiresAt,
          reason: data.reason,
        });
        assignments.push(userRole);
      } catch (error) {
        // Skip if already assigned
        if (!(error instanceof ConflictException)) {
          throw error;
        }
      }
    }

    return assignments;
  }

  /**
   * Remove role from user
   */
  async removeRole(
    userId: string,
    roleId: string,
    tenantId: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const userRole = await this.userRoleRepository.findOne({
      where: { userId, roleId, tenantId },
    });

    if (!userRole) {
      throw new NotFoundException("Role assignment not found");
    }

    await this.userRoleRepository.remove(userRole);

    console.log(`🗑️  Removed role assignment for user ${userId}`);
  }

  /**
   * Remove role from user by role name
   */
  async removeRoleByName(
    userId: string,
    roleName: string,
    tenantId: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const role = await this.roleService.getRoleByName(roleName, tenantId);

    if (!role) {
      throw new NotFoundException(`Role not found: ${roleName}`);
    }

    await this.removeRole(userId, role.id, tenantId);
  }

  /**
   * Set exact roles for user (replace all)
   */
  async setUserRoles(
    userId: string,
    roleNames: string[],
    tenantId: string,
    assignedBy?: string,
  ): Promise<UserRole[]> {
    // Remove all existing roles
    await this.userRoleRepository.delete({
      userId,
      tenantId,
    });

    // Assign new roles
    if (roleNames.length > 0) {
      return this.assignRolesByNames({
        userId,
        roleNames,
        tenantId,
        assignedBy,
      });
    }

    return [];
  }

  /**
   * Get user roles
   */
  async getUserRoles(userId: string, tenantId: string): Promise<Role[]> {
    const userRoles = await this.userRoleRepository.find({
      where: { userId, tenantId },
      relations: [
        "role",
        "role.rolePermissions",
        "role.rolePermissions.permission",
      ],
    });

    // Filter out expired roles
    return userRoles.filter((ur) => !ur.isExpired).map((ur) => ur.role);
  }

  /**
   * Get user with roles and permissions loaded
   */
  async getUserWithRoles(userId: string, tenantId: string): Promise<User> {
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: userId, tenantId },
      relations: [
        "userRoles",
        "userRoles.role",
        "userRoles.role.rolePermissions",
        "userRoles.role.rolePermissions.permission",
      ],
    });

    if (!user) {
      throw new NotFoundException("User not found");
    }

    // Filter out expired role assignments
    user.userRoles = user.userRoles.filter((ur) => !ur.isExpired);

    return user;
  }

  /**
   * Get all permissions for user (aggregated from all roles)
   */
  async getUserPermissions(
    userId: string,
    tenantId: string,
  ): Promise<Permission[]> {
    const roles = await this.getUserRoles(userId, tenantId);

    const permissionMap = new Map<string, Permission>();

    for (const role of roles) {
      if (role.rolePermissions) {
        for (const rp of role.rolePermissions) {
          if (rp.permission) {
            permissionMap.set(rp.permission.id, rp.permission);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    return Array.from(permissionMap.values());
  }

  // ============================================================
  // PERMISSION CHECKS
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Check if user has specific permission
   */
  async userHasPermission(
    userId: string,
    tenantId: string,
    permissionName: string,
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    const user = await this.getUserWithRoles(userId, tenantId);
    return user.hasPermission(permissionName);
  }

  /**
   * Check if user has any of the permissions
   */
  async userHasAnyPermission(
    userId: string,
    tenantId: string,
    permissionNames: string[],
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    const user = await this.getUserWithRoles(userId, tenantId);
    return user.hasAnyPermission(permissionNames);
  }

  /**
   * Check if user has all permissions
   */
  async userHasAllPermissions(
    userId: string,
    tenantId: string,
    permissionNames: string[],
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    const user = await this.getUserWithRoles(userId, tenantId);
    return user.hasAllPermissions(permissionNames);
  }

  /**
   * Check if user has specific role
   */
  async userHasRole(
    userId: string,
    tenantId: string,
    roleName: string,
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    const user = await this.getUserWithRoles(userId, tenantId);
    return user.hasRole(roleName);
  }

  /**
   * Check if user has any of the roles
   */
  async userHasAnyRole(
    userId: string,
    tenantId: string,
    roleNames: string[],
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    const user = await this.getUserWithRoles(userId, tenantId);
    return user.hasAnyRole(roleNames);
  }

  /**
   * Check if user is admin
   */
  async userIsAdmin(userId: string, tenantId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const user = await this.getUserWithRoles(userId, tenantId);
    return user.isAdmin;
  }

  // ============================================================
  // BULK OPERATIONS
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Assign default role to new user
   */
  async assignDefaultRole(userId: string, tenantId: string): Promise<void> {
    // Get default roles
    const defaultRoles = await this.roleService.listRoles(tenantId, {
      isDefault: true,
      isActive: true,
    });

    if (defaultRoles.length === 0) {
      console.log("⚠️  No default roles found for tenant");
      return;
    }

    // Assign all default roles
    for (const role of defaultRoles) {
      try {
        await this.assignRole({
          userId,
          roleId: role.id,
          tenantId,
          reason: "Auto-assigned default role",
        });
      } catch (error) {
        // Skip if already assigned
        if (!(error instanceof ConflictException)) {
          throw error;
        }
      }
    }

    console.log(
      `✅ Assigned ${defaultRoles.length} default role(s) to user ${userId}`,
    );
  }

  /**
   * Remove expired role assignments
   */
  async cleanupExpiredRoles(tenantId: string): Promise<number> {
    const expiredAssignments = await this.userRoleRepository
      .createQueryBuilder("userRole")
      .where("userRole.tenantId = :tenantId", { tenantId })
      .andWhere("userRole.expiresAt < :now", { now: new Date() })
      .andWhere("userRole.expiresAt IS NOT NULL")
      .getMany();

    if (expiredAssignments.length > 0) {
      await this.userRoleRepository.remove(expiredAssignments);
      console.log(
        `🗑️  Removed ${expiredAssignments.length} expired role assignments`,
      );
    }

    return expiredAssignments.length;
  }

  /**
   * Get users by role
   */
  async getUsersByRole(roleId: string, tenantId: string): Promise<User[]> {
    const userRoles = await this.userRoleRepository.find({
      where: { roleId, tenantId },
      relations: ["user"],
    });

    return userRoles.filter((ur) => !ur.isExpired).map((ur) => ur.user);
  }

  /**
   * Get users by role name
   */
  async getUsersByRoleName(
    roleName: string,
    tenantId: string,
  ): Promise<User[]> {
    const role = await this.roleService.getRoleByName(roleName, tenantId);

    if (!role) {
      throw new NotFoundException(`Role not found: ${roleName}`);
    }

    return this.getUsersByRole(role.id, tenantId);
  }

  /**
   * Get role statistics
   */
  async getRoleStats(
    roleId: string,
    tenantId: string,
  ): Promise<{
    totalUsers: number;
    activeUsers: number;
    expiredAssignments: number;
  }> {
    const userRoles = await this.userRoleRepository.find({
      where: { roleId, tenantId },
    });

    const active = userRoles.filter((ur) => !ur.isExpired);
    const expired = userRoles.filter((ur) => ur.isExpired);

    return {
      totalUsers: userRoles.length,
      activeUsers: active.length,
      expiredAssignments: expired.length,
    };
  }

  // ============================================================
  // SEEDING & INITIALIZATION
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Initialize RBAC for tenant (create default permissions and roles)
   */
  async initializeTenant(tenantId: string, createdBy?: string): Promise<void> {
    console.log(`🔧 Initializing RBAC for tenant ${tenantId}...`);

    // 1. Create default permissions
    const permissions =
      await this.permissionService.seedDefaultPermissions(tenantId);
    console.log(`✅ Created ${permissions.length} default permissions`);

    // 2. Create default roles
    const roles = await this.roleService.seedDefaultRoles(tenantId, createdBy);
    console.log(`✅ Created ${roles.length} default roles`);

    console.log(`🎉 RBAC initialization complete for tenant ${tenantId}`);
  }

  /**
   * Assign admin role to user (convenience method)
   */
  async makeUserAdmin(
    userId: string,
    tenantId: string,
    assignedBy?: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.assignRolesByNames({
      userId,
      roleNames: ["admin"],
      tenantId,
      assignedBy,
      reason: "Admin access granted",
    });

    console.log(`✅ User ${userId} is now an admin`);
  }

  /**
   * Remove admin role from user
   */
  async removeAdminAccess(userId: string, tenantId: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.removeRoleByName(userId, "admin", tenantId);
    console.log(`🗑️  Removed admin access from user ${userId}`);
  }
}

// apps/auth-service/src/modules/rbac/services/role.service.ts

import {
  Injectable,
  NotFoundException,
  ConflictException,
  BadRequestException,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { InjectRepository } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { Repository, In } from "typeorm";
import { Role, RolePermission, Permission } from "@repo/database";

export interface CreateRoleDto {
  tenantId: string;
  name: string;
  displayName?: string;
  description?: string;
  scope?: string;
  isSystem?: boolean;
  isDefault?: boolean;
  priority?: number;
  metadata?: Record<string, any>;
  createdBy?: string;
  permissionNames?: string[]; // Permission names to assign
}

export interface UpdateRoleDto {
  displayName?: string;
  description?: string;
  isActive?: boolean;
  isDefault?: boolean;
  priority?: number;
  metadata?: Record<string, any>;
}

@Injectable()
export class RoleService {
  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(Role)
    private roleRepository: Repository<Role>,
    @InjectRepository(RolePermission)
    private rolePermissionRepository: Repository<RolePermission>,
    @InjectRepository(Permission)
    private permissionRepository: Repository<Permission>,
  ) {}

  /**
   * Create a new role
   */
  async createRole(data: CreateRoleDto): Promise<Role> {
    // Check if role already exists
    const existing = await this.roleRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        tenantId: data.tenantId,
        name: data.name,
      },
    });

    if (existing) {
      throw new ConflictException(`Role ${data.name} already exists`);
    }

    const role = await this.roleRepository.save({
      tenantId: data.tenantId,
      name: data.name,
      displayName: data.displayName,
      description: data.description,
      scope: data.scope || "tenant",
      isSystem: data.isSystem || false,
      isActive: true,
      isDefault: data.isDefault || false,
      priority: data.priority || 0,
      metadata: data.metadata,
      createdBy: data.createdBy,
    });

    // Assign permissions if provided
    if (data.permissionNames && data.permissionNames.length > 0) {
      await this.assignPermissionsToRole(
        role.id,
        data.tenantId,
        data.permissionNames,
        data.createdBy,
      );
    }

    console.log(`✅ Created role: ${role.name}`);

    return role;
  }

  /**
   * Get role by ID
   */
  async getRole(
    id: string,
    tenantId: string,
    loadPermissions = false,
  ): Promise<Role> {
    const query = this.roleRepository
      .createQueryBuilder("role")
      .where("role.id = :id", { id })
      .andWhere("role.tenantId = :tenantId", { tenantId });

    if (loadPermissions) {
      query
        .leftJoinAndSelect("role.rolePermissions", "rolePermission")
        .leftJoinAndSelect("rolePermission.permission", "permission");
    }

    const role = await query.getOne();

    if (!role) {
      throw new NotFoundException("Role not found");
    }

    return role;
  }

  /**
   * Get role by name
   */
  async getRoleByName(
    name: string,
    tenantId: string,
    loadPermissions = false,
  ): Promise<Role | null> {
    const query = this.roleRepository
      .createQueryBuilder("role")
      .where("role.name = :name", { name })
      .andWhere("role.tenantId = :tenantId", { tenantId });

    if (loadPermissions) {
      query
        .leftJoinAndSelect("role.rolePermissions", "rolePermission")
        .leftJoinAndSelect("rolePermission.permission", "permission");
    }

    return query.getOne();
  }

  /**
   * List all roles for tenant
   */
  async listRoles(
    tenantId: string,
    options?: {
      isActive?: boolean;
      isSystem?: boolean;
      isDefault?: boolean;
      loadPermissions?: boolean;
    },
  ): Promise<Role[]> {
    const query = this.roleRepository
      .createQueryBuilder("role")
      .where("role.tenantId = :tenantId", { tenantId });

    if (options?.isActive !== undefined) {
      query.andWhere("role.isActive = :isActive", {
        isActive: options.isActive,
      });
    }

    if (options?.isSystem !== undefined) {
      query.andWhere("role.isSystem = :isSystem", {
        isSystem: options.isSystem,
      });
    }

    if (options?.isDefault !== undefined) {
      query.andWhere("role.isDefault = :isDefault", {
        isDefault: options.isDefault,
      });
    }

    if (options?.loadPermissions) {
      query
        .leftJoinAndSelect("role.rolePermissions", "rolePermission")
        .leftJoinAndSelect("rolePermission.permission", "permission");
    }

    query.orderBy("role.priority", "DESC").addOrderBy("role.name", "ASC");

    return query.getMany();
  }

  /**
   * Update role
   */
  async updateRole(
    id: string,
    tenantId: string,
    data: UpdateRoleDto,
  ): Promise<Role> {
    const role = await this.getRole(id, tenantId);

    if (role.isSystem) {
      throw new ConflictException("Cannot update system role");
    }

    Object.assign(role, data);

    await this.roleRepository.save(role);

    console.log(`✅ Updated role: ${role.name}`);

    return role;
  }

  /**
   * Delete role
   */
  async deleteRole(id: string, tenantId: string): Promise<void> {
    const role = await this.getRole(id, tenantId);

    if (role.isSystem) {
      throw new ConflictException("Cannot delete system role");
    }

    if (role.isDefault) {
      throw new ConflictException("Cannot delete default role");
    }

    await this.roleRepository.remove(role);

    console.log(`🗑️  Deleted role: ${role.name}`);
  }

  /**
   * Assign permissions to role
   */
  async assignPermissionsToRole(
    roleId: string,
    tenantId: string,
    permissionNames: string[],
    assignedBy?: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const role = await this.getRole(roleId, tenantId);

    if (role.isSystem) {
      throw new ConflictException("Cannot modify system role permissions");
    }

    // Get permissions by names
    const permissions = await this.permissionRepository.find({
      where: {
        tenantId,
        name: In(permissionNames),
      },
    });

    if (permissions.length !== permissionNames.length) {
      const foundNames = permissions.map((p) => p.name);
      const missing = permissionNames.filter(
        (name) => !foundNames.includes(name),
      );
      throw new BadRequestException(
        `Permissions not found: ${missing.join(", ")}`,
      );
    }

    // Get existing role permissions
    const existing = await this.rolePermissionRepository.find({
      where: { roleId, tenantId },
    });

    const existingPermissionIds = new Set(
      existing.map((rp) => rp.permissionId),
    );

    // Add new permissions
    const toAdd = permissions.filter((p) => !existingPermissionIds.has(p.id));

    if (toAdd.length > 0) {
      const rolePermissions = toAdd.map((permission) => ({
        tenantId,
        roleId,
        permissionId: permission.id,
        assignedBy,
        assignedAt: new Date(),
      }));

      await this.rolePermissionRepository.save(rolePermissions);

      console.log(
        `✅ Assigned ${toAdd.length} permissions to role ${role.name}`,
      );
    }
  }

  /**
   * Remove permissions from role
   */
  async removePermissionsFromRole(
    roleId: string,
    tenantId: string,
    permissionNames: string[],
  ): Promise<void> {
    const role = await this.getRole(roleId, tenantId);

    if (role.isSystem) {
      throw new ConflictException("Cannot modify system role permissions");
    }

    // Get permissions by names
    const permissions = await this.permissionRepository.find({
      where: {
        tenantId,
        name: In(permissionNames),
      },
    });

    const permissionIds = permissions.map((p) => p.id);

    // Delete role permissions
    await this.rolePermissionRepository.delete({
      roleId,
      tenantId,
      permissionId: In(permissionIds),
    });

    console.log(
      `🗑️  Removed ${permissions.length} permissions from role ${role.name}`,
    );
  }

  /**
   * Set exact permissions for role (replace all)
   */
  async setRolePermissions(
    roleId: string,
    tenantId: string,
    permissionNames: string[],
    assignedBy?: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const role = await this.getRole(roleId, tenantId);

    if (role.isSystem) {
      throw new ConflictException("Cannot modify system role permissions");
    }

    // Remove all existing permissions
    await this.rolePermissionRepository.delete({
      roleId,
      tenantId,
    });

    // Add new permissions
    if (permissionNames.length > 0) {
      await this.assignPermissionsToRole(
        roleId,
        tenantId,
        permissionNames,
        assignedBy,
      );
    }

    console.log(
      `✅ Set ${permissionNames.length} permissions for role ${role.name}`,
    );
  }

  /**
   * Get role permissions
   */
  async getRolePermissions(
    roleId: string,
    tenantId: string,
  ): Promise<Permission[]> {
    const rolePermissions = await this.rolePermissionRepository.find({
      where: { roleId, tenantId },
      relations: ["permission"],
    });

    return rolePermissions.map((rp) => rp.permission);
  }

  /**
   * Seed default roles for tenant
   */
  async seedDefaultRoles(
    tenantId: string,
    createdBy?: string,
  ): Promise<Role[]> {
    const roles = [
      {
        name: "super_admin",
        displayName: "Super Admin",
        description: "Platform administrator with full access",
        priority: 1000,
        isSystem: true,
        permissionNames: ["*:*"],
      },
      {
        name: "admin",
        displayName: "Admin",
        description: "Tenant administrator with full tenant access",
        priority: 900,
        isSystem: true,
        permissionNames: [
          "users:*",
          "roles:*",
          "permissions:read",
          "clients:*",
          "api:*",
        ],
      },
      {
        name: "developer",
        displayName: "Developer",
        description: "Developer with OAuth client management access",
        priority: 500,
        isSystem: false,
        permissionNames: [
          "users:read",
          "clients:*",
          "api:read",
          "api:write",
          "profile:*",
        ],
      },
      {
        name: "user",
        displayName: "User",
        description: "Standard user with basic access",
        priority: 100,
        isSystem: false,
        isDefault: true,
        permissionNames: ["profile:read", "profile:write"],
      },
    ];

    const created: Role[] = [];

    for (const roleData of roles) {
      try {
        const role = await this.createRole({
          tenantId,
          ...roleData,
          createdBy,
        });
        created.push(role);
      } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof ConflictException) {
          console.log(`⏭️  Skipping existing role: ${roleData.name}`);
        } else {
          throw error;
        }
      }
    }

    return created;
  }
}

  Body,
  Param,
  UseGuards,
  Req,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { type Request } from "express";
import { TenantService } from "./tenant.service";
import { JwtAuthGuard } from "@/common/guards";

// DTO for creating tenant
export class CreateTenantDto {
  slug!: string;
  name!: string;
  environment?: string;
}

export class SetCustomDomainDto {
  domain!: string;
}

@Controller("tenants")
@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard) // Only authenticated users
export class TenantController {
  constructor(private tenantService: TenantService) {}

  /**
   * Get current tenant info
   */
  @Get("current")
  async getCurrent(@Req() req: Request) {
    return req.tenant;
  }

  /**
   * Create new tenant (platform admin only - add RBAC later)
   */
  @Post()
  async create(@Body() dto: CreateTenantDto) {
    return this.tenantService.create(dto);
  }

  /**
   * Set custom domain for tenant
   */
  @Patch(":id/custom-domain")
  async setCustomDomain(
    @Param("id") id: string,
    @Body() dto: SetCustomDomainDto,
  ) {
    return this.tenantService.setCustomDomain(id, dto.domain);
  }

  /**
   * Verify custom domain (would be called after DNS verification)
   */
  @Post(":id/verify-domain")
  async verifyDomain(@Param("id") id: string) {
    return this.tenantService.verifyCustomDomain(id);
  }
}

// apps/auth-service/src/modules/tenant/tenant.service.ts
import {
  Injectable,
  NotFoundException,
  Inject,
  BadRequestException,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { InjectRepository } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { Repository } from "typeorm";
import { Tenant } from "@repo/database";
import { CACHE_MANAGER } from "@nestjs/cache-manager";
import { type Cache } from "cache-manager";

@Injectable()
export class TenantService {
  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(Tenant)
    private tenantRepository: Repository<Tenant>,

    @Inject(CACHE_MANAGER)
    private cacheManager: Cache,
  ) {}

  /**
   * Find tenant by slug (subdomain)
   * Cached for 5 minutes
   */
  async findBySlug(slug: string): Promise<Tenant | null> {
    const cacheKey = `tenant:slug:${slug}`;

    // Check cache
    const cached = await this.cacheManager.get<Tenant>(cacheKey);
    if (cached) {
      return cached;
    }

    // Query database
    const tenant = await this.tenantRepository.findOne({
      where: { slug, active: true },
    });

    // Cache for 5 minutes
    if (tenant) {
      await this.cacheManager.set(cacheKey, tenant, 300000);
    }

    return tenant;
  }

  /**
   * Find tenant by custom domain
   * Cached for 5 minutes
   */
  async findByCustomDomain(domain: string): Promise<Tenant | null> {
    const cacheKey = `tenant:domain:${domain}`;

    // Check cache
    const cached = await this.cacheManager.get<Tenant>(cacheKey);
    if (cached) {
      return cached;
    }

    // Query database
    const tenant = await this.tenantRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        customDomain: domain,
        customDomainVerified: true,
        active: true,
      },
    });

    // Cache for 5 minutes
    if (tenant) {
      await this.cacheManager.set(cacheKey, tenant, 300000);
    }

    return tenant;
  }

  /**
   * Find tenant by ID
   * Cached for 10 minutes
   */
  async findById(id: string): Promise<Tenant | null> {
    const cacheKey = `tenant:id:${id}`;

    const cached = await this.cacheManager.get<Tenant>(cacheKey);
    if (cached) {
      return cached;
    }
    try {
      const tenant = await this.tenantRepository.findOne({
        where: { id, active: true },
      });

      if (tenant) {
        await this.cacheManager.set(cacheKey, tenant, 600000);
      }

      return tenant;
    } catch (e) {
      throw new BadRequestException("Tenant Not found");
    }
  }

  /**
   * Invalidate all caches for a tenant
   */
  async invalidateCache(tenantId: string): Promise<void> {
    const tenant = await this.tenantRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: tenantId },
    });

    if (!tenant) return;

    await this.cacheManager.del(`tenant:id:${tenantId}`);
    await this.cacheManager.del(`tenant:slug:${tenant.slug}`);

    if (tenant.customDomain) {
      await this.cacheManager.del(`tenant:domain:${tenant.customDomain}`);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Create tenant (for platform admin)
   */
  async create(data: {
    slug: string;
    name: string;
    environment?: string;
  }): Promise<Tenant> {
    const tenant = this.tenantRepository.create({
      slug: data.slug.toLowerCase(),
      name: data.name,
      environment: data.environment || "production",
      active: true,
    });

    return this.tenantRepository.save(tenant);
  }

  /**
   * Set custom domain
   */
  async setCustomDomain(tenantId: string, domain: string): Promise<Tenant> {
    const tenant = await this.tenantRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: tenantId },
    });

    if (!tenant) {
      throw new NotFoundException("Tenant not found");
    }

    tenant.customDomain = domain;
    tenant.customDomainVerified = false; // Require verification

    await this.tenantRepository.save(tenant);
    await this.invalidateCache(tenantId);

    return tenant;
  }

  /**
   * Verify custom domain
   */
  async verifyCustomDomain(tenantId: string): Promise<Tenant> {
    const tenant = await this.tenantRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: tenantId },
    });

    if (!tenant) {
      throw new NotFoundException("Tenant not found");
    }

    tenant.customDomainVerified = true;

    await this.tenantRepository.save(tenant);
    await this.invalidateCache(tenantId);

    return tenant;
  }
}
