// apps/auth-service/src/modules/user/user.controller.ts
// UPDATED WITH RBAC PROTECTION

import {
  Controller,
  Get,
  Put,
  Post,
  Delete,
  Body,
  Param,
  Query,
  Request,
  HttpCode,
  HttpStatus,
  UseGuards,
  Header,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import {
  ApiTags,
  ApiOperation,
  ApiResponse,
  ApiBearerAuth,
} from "@nestjs/swagger";
import { PermissionsGuard } from "../rbac/guards/permissions.guard";
import { UserService } from "./user.service";
import {
  UpdateUserDto,
  ChangePasswordDto,
  UserStatsDto,
  ListUsersQueryDto,
  BulkUpdateUsersDto,
  ListUsersResponseDto,
} from "./user.dto";
import { JwtAuthGuard } from "@/common/guards";
import { RequirePermissions } from "@/modules/rbac/decorators/rbac.decorator";

@ApiTags("Users")
@Controller("users")
@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard) // All endpoints require authentication
@ApiBearerAuth()
export class UserController {
  constructor(private readonly userService: UserService) {}

  // ============================================================
  // CURRENT USER ENDPOINTS (Own Profile)
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * Get current user profile
   * Anyone authenticated can read their own profile
   */
  @Get("me")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Get current user profile" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "User profile retrieved" })
  async getProfile(@Request() req: any) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    return this.userService.findById(tenantId, req.user.sub);
  }

  /**
   * Update current user profile
   * Anyone authenticated can update their own profile
   */
  @Put("me")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Update current user profile" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Profile updated successfully" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 400, description: "Bad request - validation failed" })
  async updateProfile(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Body() updateUserDto: UpdateUserDto,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    await this.userService.update(tenantId, req.user.sub, updateUserDto);
    return { message: "Profile updated successfully" };
  }

  /**
   * Change own password
   * Anyone authenticated can change their own password
   */
  @Post("me/change-password")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Change user password" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Password changed successfully" })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 400,
    description: "Invalid current password or weak new password",
  })
  async changePassword(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Body() changePasswordDto: ChangePasswordDto,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    await this.userService.changePassword(
      tenantId,
      req.user.sub,
      changePasswordDto.currentPassword,
      changePasswordDto.newPassword,
    );
    return { message: "Password changed successfully" };
  }

  /**
   * Get active sessions for current user
   * Anyone authenticated can view their own sessions
   */
  @Get("me/sessions")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Get all active sessions" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Sessions retrieved" })
  async getSessions(@Request() req: any) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    return this.userService.getActiveSessions(tenantId, req.user.sub);
  }

  /**
   * Revoke a specific session
   * Anyone authenticated can revoke their own sessions
   */
  @Delete("me/sessions/:sessionId")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Revoke a specific session" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Session revoked" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: "Session not found" })
  async revokeSession(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Param("sessionId") sessionId: string,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    await this.userService.revokeSession(tenantId, req.user.sub, sessionId);
    return { message: "Session revoked successfully" };
  }

  /**
   * Revoke all sessions except current
   * Anyone authenticated can revoke all their sessions
   */
  @Delete("me/sessions")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Revoke all sessions except current" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Sessions revoked" })
  async revokeAllSessions(@Request() req: any) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    await this.userService.revokeAllSessions(tenantId, req.user.sub);
    return { message: "All other sessions revoked successfully" };
  }

  /**
   * Get security events for current user
   * Anyone authenticated can view their own security events
   */
  @Get("me/security-events")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Get security event history" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Security events retrieved" })
  async getSecurityEvents(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Query("limit") limit: string = "50",
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    return this.userService.getSecurityEvents(
      tenantId,
      req.user.sub,
      parseInt(limit, 10),
    );
  }

  /**
   * Delete own account (soft delete)
   * Anyone authenticated can delete their own account
   */
  @Delete("me")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Delete user account" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Account deleted successfully" })
  async deleteAccount(@Request() req: any, @Body("password") password: string) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    await this.userService.deleteAccount(tenantId, req.user.sub, password);
    return { message: "Account deleted successfully" };
  }

  // ============================================================
  // ADMIN ENDPOINTS (Manage Other Users)
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * List all users (Admin only)
   * Requires: users:read permission
   */
  @Get()
  @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("users:read")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "List all users (Admin)" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Users retrieved" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: "Forbidden - missing permissions" })
  async listUsers(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Query() query: ListUsersQueryDto,
  ): Promise<ListUsersResponseDto> {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    // Convert date strings to Date objects
    const options: any = { ...query };
    if (query.createdAfter) {
      options.createdAfter = new Date(query.createdAfter);
    }
    if (query.createdBefore) {
      options.createdBefore = new Date(query.createdBefore);
    }

    return this.userService.listUsers(tenantId, options);
  }

  /**
   * Get user statistics (Admin)
   */
  @Get("stats")
  @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("users:read")
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Get user statistics (Admin)",
    description: "Get aggregate statistics about users",
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: "Statistics retrieved",
    type: UserStatsDto,
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: "Forbidden - missing permissions" })
  async getUserStats(@Request() req: any): Promise<UserStatsDto> {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    return this.userService.getUserStats(tenantId);
  }

  /**
   * Export users to CSV (Admin)
   */
  @Get("export")
  @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("users:read")
  @Header("Content-Type", "text/csv")
  @Header("Content-Disposition", 'attachment; filename="users.csv"')
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Export users to CSV (Admin)",
    description: "Download all users as CSV file",
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "CSV file" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: "Forbidden - missing permissions" })
  async exportUsers(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Query() query: ListUsersQueryDto,
  ): Promise<string> {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;

    const options: any = { ...query };
    if (query.createdAfter) {
      options.createdAfter = new Date(query.createdAfter);
    }
    if (query.createdBefore) {
      options.createdBefore = new Date(query.createdBefore);
    }

    return this.userService.exportUsers(tenantId, options);
  }

  /**
   * Bulk update users active status (Admin)
   */
  @Post("bulk/update-status")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("users:write")
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: "Bulk update user status (Admin)",
    description: "Activate or deactivate multiple users at once",
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "Users updated" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: "Forbidden - missing permissions" })
  async bulkUpdateStatus(@Request() req: any, @Body() dto: BulkUpdateUsersDto) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    const adminUserId = req.user.sub;

    const result = await this.userService.bulkUpdateActiveStatus(
      tenantId,
      dto.userIds,
      dto.isActive,
      adminUserId,
    );

    return {
      message: `Successfully updated ${result.updated} users`,
      updated: result.updated,
    };
  }
  /**
   * Get user by ID (Admin only)
   * Requires: users:read permission
   */
  @Get(":id")
  @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("users:read")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Get user by ID (Admin)" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "User retrieved" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: "User not found" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: "Forbidden - missing permissions" })
  async getUserById(@Request() req: any, @Param("id") id: string) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    return this.userService.findById(tenantId, id);
  }

  /**
   * Update user by ID (Admin only)
   * Requires: users:write permission
   */
  @Put(":id")
  @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("users:write")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Update user by ID (Admin)" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: "User updated successfully" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: "User not found" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: "Forbidden - missing permissions" })
  async updateUser(
    @Request() req: any,
    @Param("id") id: string,
    @Body() updateUserDto: UpdateUserDto,
  ) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    await this.userService.update(tenantId, id, updateUserDto);
    return { message: "User updated successfully" };
  }

  /**
   * Delete user by ID (Admin only)
   * Requires: users:delete permission
   */
  @Delete(":id")
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT)
  @UseGuards(PermissionsGuard)
  @RequirePermissions("users:delete")
  @ApiOperation({ summary: "Delete user by ID (Admin)" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 204, description: "User deleted successfully" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: "User not found" })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: "Forbidden - missing permissions" })
  async deleteUser(@Request() req: any, @Param("id") id: string) {
    const tenantId = req.tenantId!;
    // TODO: Implement admin delete in UserService (different from self-delete)
    await this.userService.deleteAccount(tenantId, id, ""); // Requires service update
  }

  /**
   * Force password reset for user (Admin only)
   * Requires: users:write permission

  @Post(":id/force-password-reset")

    return { message: "User will be required to reset password on next login" };

    message:
      "Password must contain uppercase, lowercase, number and special character",

}

import { Module } from "@nestjs/common";
import { TypeOrmModule } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import {
  User,
  Session,
  SecurityEvent,
  UserRole,
  Role,
  RolePermission,
  Permission,
} from "@repo/database";
import { UserController } from "./user.controller";
import { UserService } from "./user.service";
import { RbacService } from "@/modules/rbac/services/rbac.service";

import { RoleService } from "@/modules/rbac/services/role.service";
import { PermissionService } from "@/modules/rbac/services/permission.service";

/**
 * User Module
 *
 * Handles user management functionality:
 * - User profile management
 * - Password changes
 * - Session management
 * - Security event tracking
 * - Account deletion
 */
@Module({
  imports: [
    TypeOrmModule.forFeature([
      User,
      Session,
      SecurityEvent,
      UserRole,
      Role,
      RolePermission,
      UserRole,
      Permission,
    ]),

  controllers: [UserController],

export class UserModule {}

import {
  Injectable,
  NotFoundException,
  BadRequestException,
  UnauthorizedException,
} from "@nestjs/common";
import { InjectRepository } from "@nestjs/typeorm";
import { Repository, MoreThan, IsNull, In } from "typeorm";
import { User, Session, SecurityEvent } from "@repo/database";
import { PasswordService } from "@repo/security";

/**
 * User Service
 *
 * Handles all user management business logic:
 * - User CRUD operations
 * - Password management
 * - Session management
 * - Security event tracking
 */
@Injectable()
export class UserService {
  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(User)
    private readonly userRepository: Repository<User>,
    @InjectRepository(Session)
    private readonly sessionRepository: Repository<Session>,
    @InjectRepository(SecurityEvent)
    private readonly securityEventRepository: Repository<SecurityEvent>,
  ) {}

  /**
   * Find user by ID
   */
  async findById(tenantId: string, id: string): Promise<User> {
    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: { id, tenantId },
      relations: ["tenant"],
    });

    if (!user) {
      throw new NotFoundException("User not found");
    }

    return user;
  }

  /**
   * Find user by email
   */
  async findByEmail(tenantId: string, email: string): Promise<User | null> {
    const where: any = { email };
    if (tenantId) {
      where.tenantId = tenantId;
    }

    return this.userRepository.findOne({
      where,
      relations: ["tenant", "mfaSettings"],
    });
  }

  /**
   * Update user profile
   */
  async update(
    tenantId: string,
    userId: string,
    data: {
      firstName?: string;
      lastName?: string;
      displayName?: string;
      phone?: string;
      avatarUrl?: string;
      timezone?: string;
      language?: string;
    },
  ) {
    const user = await this.findById(tenantId, userId);

    Object.assign(user, data);
    await this.userRepository.save(user);

    // Log profile update
    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      userId,
      tenantId: user.tenantId,
      eventType: "USER_UPDATED",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
      userAgent: "system",
      metadata: { fields: Object.keys(data) },
    });

    return user;
  }

  /**
   * Change password
   */
  async changePassword(
    tenantId: string,
    userId: string,
    currentPassword: string,
    newPassword: string,
  ) {
    // Get user with password hash
    const user = await this.userRepository
      .createQueryBuilder("user")
      .addSelect("user.passwordHash")
      .addSelect("user.passwordHistory")
      .where("user.id = :id", { id: userId })
      .where("user.tenant_id = :tenantId", { tenantId })
      .getOne();

    if (!user) {
      throw new NotFoundException("User not found");
    }

    // Verify current password
    const isValid = await PasswordService.verify(
      user.passwordHash,
      currentPassword,
    );

    if (!isValid) {
      await this.logSecurityEvent({
        userId,
        tenantId: user.tenantId,
        eventType: "PASSWORD_CHANGE_FAILED",
        severity: "warning",
        ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
        userAgent: "system",
        metadata: { reason: "invalid_current_password" },
      });

      throw new UnauthorizedException("Current password is incorrect");
    }

    // Validate new password strength
    const validation = PasswordService.validateStrength(newPassword);
    if (!validation.isValid) {
      throw new BadRequestException({
        message: "New password does not meet security requirements",
        errors: validation.errors,
        score: validation.score,
      });
    }

    // Check password history
    const passwordHistory = user.passwordHistory || [];
    for (const oldHash of passwordHistory) {
      const isReused = await PasswordService.verify(oldHash, newPassword);
      if (isReused) {
        throw new BadRequestException(
          "Password has been used recently. Please choose a different password.",
        );
      }
    }

    // Hash new password
    const newHash = await PasswordService.hash(newPassword);

    // Update password and history
    const updatedHistory = [user.passwordHash, ...passwordHistory].slice(0, 5);

    await this.userRepository.update(userId, {
      passwordHash: newHash,
      passwordHistory: updatedHistory,
      passwordChangedAt: new Date(),
      forcePasswordChange: false,
    });

    // Log password change
    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      userId,
      tenantId: user.tenantId,
      eventType: "PASSWORD_CHANGED",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
      userAgent: "system",
    });

    // Revoke all sessions except current (force re-login on other devices)
    await this.sessionRepository.update(
      { userId, revokedAt: IsNull() },
      {
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: "password_changed",
      },
    );
  }

  /**
   * Get active sessions for user
   */
  async getActiveSessions(tenantId: string, userId: string) {
    const sessions = await this.sessionRepository.find({
      where: {
        userId,
        revokedAt: IsNull(),
        tenantId,
        expiresAt: MoreThan(new Date()),
      },
      order: {
        lastActivity: "DESC",
      },
    });

    return sessions.map((session) => ({
      id: session.id,
      deviceInfo: session.deviceInfo,
      ipAddress: session.ipAddress,
      location: session.location,
      lastActivity: session.lastActivity,
      createdAt: session.createdAt,
    }));
  }

  /**
   * Revoke a specific session
   */
  async revokeSession(tenantId: string, userId: string, sessionId: string) {
    const session = await this.sessionRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: sessionId, userId, tenantId },
    });

    if (!session) {
      throw new NotFoundException("Session not found");
    }

    await this.sessionRepository.update(sessionId, {
      revokedAt: new Date(),
      revokedReason: "manual_revocation",
    });

    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      userId,
      eventType: "SESSION_REVOKED",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
      userAgent: "system",
      metadata: { sessionId },
    });
  }

  /**
   * Revoke all sessions for user
   */
  async revokeAllSessions(tenantId: string, userId: string) {
    await this.sessionRepository.update(
      { userId, revokedAt: IsNull(), tenantId },
      {
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: "user_request",
      },
    );

    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      userId,
      eventType: "ALL_SESSIONS_REVOKED",
      severity: "info",
      ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
      userAgent: "system",
    });
  }

  /**
   * Get security events for user
   */
  async getSecurityEvents(
    tenantId: string,
    userId: string,
    limit: number = 50,
  ) {
    const events = await this.securityEventRepository.find({
      where: { userId, tenantId },
      order: { createdAt: "DESC" },
      take: limit,
    });

    return events.map((event) => ({
      id: event.id,
      eventType: event.eventType,
      severity: event.severity,
      ipAddress: event.ipAddress,
      location: event.location,
      metadata: event.metadata,
      createdAt: event.createdAt,
    }));
  }

  /**
   * Delete account (soft delete)
   */
  async deleteAccount(tenantId: string, userId: string, password: string) {
    // Get user with password hash
    const user = await this.userRepository
      .createQueryBuilder("user")
      .addSelect("user.passwordHash")
      .where("user.id = :id", { id: userId })
      .where("user.tenant_id = :id", { id: tenantId })
      .getOne();

    if (!user) {
      throw new NotFoundException("User not found");
    }

    // Verify password
    const isValid = await PasswordService.verify(user.passwordHash, password);

    if (!isValid) {
      await this.logSecurityEvent({
        userId,
        tenantId: user.tenantId,
        eventType: "ACCOUNT_DELETION_FAILED",
        severity: "warning",
        ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
        userAgent: "system",
        metadata: { reason: "invalid_password" },
      });

      throw new UnauthorizedException("Password is incorrect");
    }

    // Soft delete user
    await this.userRepository.softDelete(userId);

    // Revoke all sessions
    await this.sessionRepository.update(
      { userId, revokedAt: IsNull() },
      {
        revokedAt: new Date(),
        revokedReason: "account_deleted",
      },
    );

    // Log account deletion
    await this.logSecurityEvent({
      userId,
      tenantId: user.tenantId,
      eventType: "ACCOUNT_DELETED",
      severity: "critical",
      ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
      userAgent: "system",
    });
  }

  /**
   * Check if account is locked
   */
  async isAccountLocked(tenantId: string, userId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const user = await this.findById(tenantId, userId);

    if (!user.lockedUntil) {
      return false;
    }

    return user.lockedUntil > new Date();
  }

  /**
   * Increment failed login attempts
   */
  async incrementFailedAttempts(tenantId: string, userId: string) {
    const user = await this.findById(tenantId, userId);

    const newAttempts = user.failedLoginAttempts + 1;

    const update: any = {
      failedLoginAttempts: newAttempts,
    };

    // Lock account after 5 failed attempts
    if (newAttempts >= 5) {
      update.lockedUntil = new Date(Date.now() + 15 * 60 * 1000); // 15 minutes

      await this.logSecurityEvent({
        userId,
        tenantId: user.tenantId,
        eventType: "ACCOUNT_LOCKED",
        severity: "critical",
        ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
        userAgent: "system",
        metadata: { attempts: newAttempts },
      });
    }

    await this.userRepository.update(userId, update);
  }

  /**
   * Reset failed login attempts
   */
  async resetFailedAttempts(tenantId: string, userId: string) {
    await this.userRepository.update(userId, {
      failedLoginAttempts: 0,
      lockedUntil: undefined,
    });
  }

  /**
   * Update last login timestamp
   */
  async updateLastLogin(tenantId: string, userId: string, ip: string) {
    await this.userRepository.update(userId, {
      lastLoginAt: new Date(),
      lastLoginIp: ip,
      failedLoginAttempts: 0,
      lockedUntil: undefined,
    });
  }

  // ============================================================
  // ADD THIS METHOD TO UserService CLASS
  // ============================================================

  /**
   * List users with pagination, filtering, and search
   *
   * @param tenantId - Tenant ID to filter users
   * @param options - Pagination and filtering options
   * @returns Paginated list of users
   *
   * @example
   * // Simple pagination
   * const result = await userService.listUsers(tenantId, { page: 1, limit: 20 });
   *
   * @example
   * // With search
   * const result = await userService.listUsers(tenantId, {
   *   page: 1,
   *   limit: 20,
   *   search: 'john',
   * });
   *
   * @example
   * // With filters
   * const result = await userService.listUsers(tenantId, {
   *   page: 1,
   *   limit: 20,
   *   emailVerified: true,
   *   isActive: true,
   *   sortBy: 'createdAt',
   *   sortOrder: 'DESC',
   * });
   */
  async listUsers(
    tenantId: string,
    options: ListUsersOptions = {},
  ): Promise<PaginatedUsersResponse> {
    // Default values
    const page = Math.max(1, options.page || 1);
    const limit = Math.min(100, Math.max(1, options.limit || 20)); // Max 100 per page
    const sortBy = options.sortBy || "createdAt";
    const sortOrder = options.sortOrder || "DESC";
    const skip = (page - 1) * limit;

    // Validate sortBy field
    const allowedSortFields = [
      "createdAt",
      "updatedAt",
      "email",
      "firstName",
      "lastName",
      "lastLoginAt",
    ];

    if (!allowedSortFields.includes(sortBy)) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        `Invalid sort field. Allowed: ${allowedSortFields.join(", ")}`,
      );
    }

    // Build query
    const queryBuilder = this.userRepository
      .createQueryBuilder("user")
      .leftJoinAndSelect("user.tenant", "tenant")
      .where("user.tenantId = :tenantId", { tenantId });

    // Apply search filter
    if (options.search) {
      const searchTerm = `%${options.search}%`;
      queryBuilder.andWhere(
        "(user.email ILIKE :search OR user.firstName ILIKE :search OR user.lastName ILIKE :search OR user.displayName ILIKE :search)",
        { search: searchTerm },
      );
    }

    // Apply isActive filter
    if (options.isActive !== undefined) {
      queryBuilder.andWhere("user.isActive = :isActive", {
        isActive: options.isActive,
      });
    }

    // Apply emailVerified filter
    if (options.emailVerified !== undefined) {
      queryBuilder.andWhere("user.emailVerified = :emailVerified", {
        emailVerified: options.emailVerified,
      });
    }

    // Apply date range filters
    if (options.createdAfter) {
      queryBuilder.andWhere("user.createdAt >= :createdAfter", {
        createdAfter: options.createdAfter,
      });
    }

    if (options.createdBefore) {
      queryBuilder.andWhere("user.createdAt <= :createdBefore", {
        createdBefore: options.createdBefore,
      });
    }

    if (options.role) {
      queryBuilder
        .leftJoin("user.userRoles", "userRole")
        .leftJoin("userRole.role", "role")
        .andWhere("role.name = :role", { role: options.role });
    }

    // Apply sorting
    queryBuilder.orderBy(`user.${sortBy}`, sortOrder);

    // Get total count
    const total = await queryBuilder.getCount();

    // Get paginated results
    const users = await queryBuilder.skip(skip).take(limit).getMany();

    // Calculate pagination metadata
    const totalPages = Math.ceil(total / limit);
    const hasNextPage = page < totalPages;
    const hasPreviousPage = page > 1;

    // Format response
    const formattedUsers = users.map((user) => ({
      id: user.id,
      email: user.email,
      firstName: user.firstName,
      lastName: user.lastName,
      displayName: user.displayName,
      emailVerified: user.emailVerified,
      isActive: user.isActive,
      lastLoginAt: user.lastLoginAt,
      createdAt: user.createdAt,
      updatedAt: user.updatedAt,
      tenant: user.tenant
        ? {
            id: user.tenant.id,
            name: user.tenant.name,
            slug: user.tenant.slug,
          }
        : undefined,
    }));

    return {
      users: formattedUsers,
      pagination: {
        page,
        limit,
        total,
        totalPages,
        hasNextPage,
        hasPreviousPage,
      },
    };
  }

  /**
   * Get user statistics for admin dashboard
   */
  async getUserStats(tenantId: string): Promise<{
    total: number;
    active: number;
    inactive: number;
    verified: number;
    unverified: number;
    recentLogins: number; // Last 7 days
  }> {
    const total = await this.userRepository.count({
      where: { tenantId },
    });

    const active = await this.userRepository.count({
      where: { tenantId, isActive: true },
    });

    const verified = await this.userRepository.count({
      where: { tenantId, emailVerified: true },
    });

    const sevenDaysAgo = new Date();
    sevenDaysAgo.setDate(sevenDaysAgo.getDate() - 7);

    const recentLogins = await this.userRepository.count({
      where: {
        tenantId,
        lastLoginAt: MoreThan(sevenDaysAgo),
      },
    });

    return {
      total,
      active,
      inactive: total - active,
      verified,
      unverified: total - verified,
      recentLogins,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Bulk activate/deactivate users
   */
  async bulkUpdateActiveStatus(
    tenantId: string,
    userIds: string[],
    isActive: boolean,
    adminUserId: string,
  ): Promise<{ updated: number }> {
    if (userIds.length === 0) {
      return { updated: 0 };
    }

    // Verify all users belong to tenant
    const users = await this.userRepository.find({
      where: {
        id: In(userIds),
        tenantId,
      },
    });

    if (users.length !== userIds.length) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        "Some users not found or belong to different tenant",
      );
    }

    // Update all users
    const result = await this.userRepository.update(
      {
        id: In(userIds),
        tenantId,
      },
      { isActive },
    );

    // Log security event for each user
    for (const user of users) {
      await this.logSecurityEvent({
        userId: user.id,
        tenantId,
        eventType: isActive ? "USER_ACTIVATED" : "USER_DEACTIVATED",
        severity: "info",
        ipAddress: "0.0.0.0",
        userAgent: "system",
        metadata: { performedBy: adminUserId },
      });
    }

    console.log(
      `✅ Bulk ${isActive ? "activated" : "deactivated"} ${users.length} users`,
    );

    return { updated: result.affected || 0 };
  }

  /**
   * Export users to CSV format
   */
  async exportUsers(
    tenantId: string,
    options: ListUsersOptions = {},
  ): Promise<string> {
    // Get all users (no pagination)
    const result = await this.listUsers(tenantId, {
      ...options,
      limit: 10000, // Max export limit
    });

    // Generate CSV
    const headers = [
      "ID",
      "Email",
      "First Name",
      "Last Name",
      "Display Name",
      "Email Verified",
      "Active",
      "Last Login",
      "Created At",
    ];

    const rows = result.users.map((user) => [
      user.id,
      user.email,
      user.firstName || "",
      user.lastName || "",
      user.displayName || "",
      user.emailVerified ? "Yes" : "No",
      user.isActive ? "Yes" : "No",
      user.lastLoginAt ? user.lastLoginAt.toISOString() : "Never",
      user.createdAt.toISOString(),
    ]);

    // CSV format
    const csv = [
      headers.join(","),
      ...rows.map((row) =>
        row.map((cell) => `"${String(cell).replace(/"/g, '""')}"`).join(","),
      ),
    ].join("\n");

    return csv;
  }

  /**
   * Log security event
   */
  private async logSecurityEvent(data: {
    userId?: string;
    tenantId?: string;
    eventType: string;
    severity: "info" | "warning" | "critical";
    ipAddress: string;
    userAgent: string;
    metadata?: Record<string, any>;
  }) {
    await this.securityEventRepository.save({
      userId: data.userId,
      tenantId: data.tenantId,
      eventType: data.eventType,
      severity: data.severity,
      ipAddress: data.ipAddress,
      userAgent: data.userAgent,
      metadata: data.metadata || {},
    });
  }
}

/**
 * Pagination options for listing users
 */
export interface ListUsersOptions {
  page?: number;
  limit?: number;
  sortBy?: string;
  sortOrder?: "ASC" | "DESC";
  search?: string;
  isActive?: boolean;
  emailVerified?: boolean;
  role?: string;
  createdAfter?: Date;
  createdBefore?: Date;
}

/**
 * Paginated response for listing users
 */
export interface PaginatedUsersResponse {
  users: Array<{
    id: string;
    email: string;
    firstName?: string;
    lastName?: string;
    displayName?: string;
    emailVerified: boolean;
    isActive: boolean;
    lastLoginAt?: Date;
    createdAt: Date;
    updatedAt: Date;
    tenant?: {
      id: string;
      name: string;
      slug: string;
    };
  }>;
  pagination: {
    page: number;
    limit: number;
    total: number;
    totalPages: number;
    hasNextPage: boolean;
    hasPreviousPage: boolean;
  };
}

        .text-muted { color: #6c757d; font-size: 14px; }
        .alert { background-color: #d1ecf1; border-left: 4px solid #0c5460; padding: 16px; margin: 20px 0; border-radius: 4px; }
        .success-icon { font-size: 48px; text-align: center; margin: 20px 0; }
        .powered-by { color: #adb5bd; font-size: 12px; margin-top: 10px; }
        h1 { color: #333333; font-size: 24px; margin-bottom: 20px; }
        @media only screen and (max-width: 600px) {
            .email-wrapper { padding: 20px 10px; }
            .email-body { padding: 30px 20px; }
            .email-footer { padding: 20px; }
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div class="email-wrapper">
        <div class="email-container">
            <div class="email-header">
                <img src="{{tenantLogo}}" alt="{{tenantName}}" />
            </div>

                    <h1>Password Successfully Changed</h1>

                    <p>Hello {{userName}},</p>

                    <p>This is a confirmation that the password for your account <strong>{{userEmail}}</strong> at <strong>{{tenantName}}</strong> has been successfully changed.</p>

                    <p>If you made this change, no further action is required.</p>

                        <strong>🔒 Security Notice:</strong> If you did NOT change your password, please contact our support team immediately. Your account may have been compromised.
                    </div>

                    <p>For your security, all active sessions have been logged out. You'll need to sign in again with your new password.</p>

import { Test, TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';
import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import request from 'supertest';
import { App } from 'supertest/types';
import { AppModule } from './../src/app.module';

describe('AppController (e2e)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication<App>;

  beforeEach(async () => {
    const moduleFixture: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({
      imports: [AppModule],
    }).compile();

    app = moduleFixture.createNestApplication();
    await app.init();
  });

  it('/ (GET)', () => {
    return request(app.getHttpServer())
      .get('/')
      .expect(200)
      .expect('Hello World!');
  });
});

FILE: test/scripts/mfa.test.sh

#!/bin/bash
# test-mfa-complete.sh
# Complete MFA integration test script

# Colors
GREEN='\033[0;32m'
RED='\033[0;31m'
BLUE='\033[0;34m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
NC='\033[0m' # No Color

# Configuration
API_URL="http://localhost:4000"
TENANT_ID="d9717e9f-64d1-4f7a-8baa-31d2a91d45a7"
EMAIL="test2@example.com"
PASSWORD="SecureP@ssw0rd123!"

echo -e "${BLUE}🔐 Fort Knox MFA Integration Test${NC}"
echo "====================================="
echo ""

# Variables to store between steps
ACCESS_TOKEN=""
TEMP_TOKEN=""
MFA_CODE=""
BACKUP_CODE=""

# ============================================================
# TEST 1: Register User (if needed)
# ============================================================

echo -e "${YELLOW}📋 Test 1: Register User${NC}"
echo "---"

response=$(curl -s -w "\n%{http_code}" -X POST "$API_URL/auth/register" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "X-Tenant-ID: $TENANT_ID" \
  -d "{
    \"email\": \"$EMAIL\",
    \"password\": \"$PASSWORD\",
    \"firstName\": \"Test\",
    \"lastName\": \"User\"
  }")

http_code=$(echo "$response" | tail -n 1)
body=$(echo "$response" | sed '$d')

if [ "$http_code" -eq 201 ] || [ "$http_code" -eq 409 ]; then
  echo -e "${GREEN}✅ User exists or created successfully${NC}"
else
  echo -e "${RED}❌ Failed to create user (HTTP $http_code)${NC}"
  echo "$body"
fi

echo ""
sleep 1

# ============================================================
# TEST 2: Login (No MFA yet)
# ============================================================

echo -e "${YELLOW}📋 Test 2: Login without MFA${NC}"
echo "---"

response=$(curl -s -w "\n%{http_code}" -X POST "$API_URL/auth/login" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "X-Tenant-ID: $TENANT_ID" \
  -d "{
    \"email\": \"$EMAIL\",
    \"password\": \"$PASSWORD\"
  }")

http_code=$(echo "$response" | tail -n 1)
body=$(echo "$response" | sed '$d')
echo $response


if [ "$http_code" -eq 200 ]; then
  ACCESS_TOKEN=$(echo "$body" | jq -r '.data.accessToken')
  echo -e "${GREEN}✅ Login successful (no MFA)${NC}"
  echo "Access Token: ${ACCESS_TOKEN:0:20}..."
else
  echo -e "${RED}❌ Login failed (HTTP $http_code)${NC}"
  echo "$body"
  exit 1
fi

echo ""
sleep 1

# ============================================================
# TEST 3: Setup TOTP
# ============================================================

echo -e "${YELLOW}📋 Test 3: Setup TOTP (MFA)${NC}"
echo "---"

response=$(curl -s -w "\n%{http_code}" -X POST "$API_URL/mfa/setup/totp" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $ACCESS_TOKEN" \
  -H "X-Tenant-ID: $TENANT_ID")

http_code=$(echo "$response" | tail -n 1)
body=$(echo "$response" | sed '$d')

if [ "$http_code" -eq 201 ]; then
  SECRET=$(echo "$body" | jq -r '.data.secret')
  QR_CODE=$(echo "$body" | jq -r '.data.qrCode')
  BACKUP_CODES=$(echo "$body" | jq -r '.backupCodes[]' | head -1)

  echo -e "${GREEN}✅ TOTP setup successful${NC}"
  echo "Secret: $SECRET"
  echo "Backup Code (first): $BACKUP_CODES"
  echo ""
  echo -e "${BLUE}📱 SCAN THIS QR CODE WITH GOOGLE AUTHENTICATOR:${NC}"
  echo "Open the QR code in your browser:"
  echo "$QR_CODE"
  echo ""

  BACKUP_CODE=$BACKUP_CODES

  echo -e "${YELLOW}Enter the 6-digit code from your authenticator app:${NC}"
  read -p "MFA Code: " MFA_CODE
else
  echo -e "${RED}❌ TOTP setup failed (HTTP $http_code)${NC}"
  echo "$body"
  exit 1
fi

echo ""
sleep 1

# ============================================================
# TEST 4: Verify and Enable TOTP
# ============================================================

echo -e "${YELLOW}📋 Test 4: Verify and Enable TOTP${NC}"
echo "---"

response=$(curl -s -w "\n%{http_code}" -X POST "$API_URL/mfa/verify/totp" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $ACCESS_TOKEN" \
  -H "X-Tenant-ID: $TENANT_ID" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d "{
    \"token\": \"$MFA_CODE\"
  }")

http_code=$(echo "$response" | tail -n 1)
body=$(echo "$response" | sed '$d')

if [ "$http_code" -eq 200 ]; then
  echo -e "${GREEN}✅ TOTP verified and enabled!${NC}"
  echo "Backup codes saved from previous step"
else
  echo -e "${RED}❌ TOTP verification failed (HTTP $http_code)${NC}"
  echo "$body"
  exit 1
fi

echo ""
sleep 1

# ============================================================
# TEST 5: Logout
# ============================================================

echo -e "${YELLOW}📋 Test 5: Logout${NC}"
echo "---"

response=$(curl -s -w "\n%{http_code}" -X POST "$API_URL/auth/logout" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $ACCESS_TOKEN" \
  -H "X-Tenant-ID: $TENANT_ID")

http_code=$(echo "$response" | tail -n 1)

if [ "$http_code" -eq 200 ]; then
  echo -e "${GREEN}✅ Logged out successfully${NC}"
else
  echo -e "${RED}❌ Logout failed (HTTP $http_code)${NC}"
fi

echo ""
sleep 2

# ============================================================
# TEST 6: Login with MFA Required
# ============================================================

echo -e "${YELLOW}📋 Test 6: Login (MFA Required)${NC}"
echo "---"

response=$(curl -s -w "\n%{http_code}" -X POST "$API_URL/auth/login" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "X-Tenant-ID: $TENANT_ID" \
  -d "{
    \"email\": \"$EMAIL\",
    \"password\": \"$PASSWORD\"
  }")

http_code=$(echo "$response" | tail -n 1)
body=$(echo "$response" | sed '$d')

if [ "$http_code" -eq 200 ]; then
  MFA_REQUIRED=$(echo "$body" | jq -r '.data.mfaRequired')

  if [ "$MFA_REQUIRED" = "true" ]; then
    TEMP_TOKEN=$(echo "$body" | jq -r '.data.tempToken')
    echo -e "${GREEN}✅ MFA required (as expected)${NC}"
    echo "Temp Token: ${TEMP_TOKEN:0:20}..."
  else
    echo -e "${RED}❌ MFA not required (unexpected)${NC}"
    exit 1
  fi
else
  echo -e "${RED}❌ Login failed (HTTP $http_code)${NC}"
  echo "$body"
  exit 1
fi

echo ""
echo -e "${YELLOW}Enter the 6-digit code from your authenticator app:${NC}"
read -p "MFA Code: " MFA_CODE
echo ""

# ============================================================
# TEST 7: Verify MFA Code
# ============================================================

echo -e "${YELLOW}📋 Test 7: Verify MFA Code${NC}"
echo "---"

response=$(curl -s -w "\n%{http_code}" -X POST "$API_URL/auth/mfa/verify" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "X-Tenant-ID: $TENANT_ID" \
  -d "{
    \"tempToken\": \"$TEMP_TOKEN\",
    \"code\": \"$MFA_CODE\"
  }")

http_code=$(echo "$response" | tail -n 1)
body=$(echo "$response" | sed '$d')

if [ "$http_code" -eq 200 ]; then
  NEW_ACCESS_TOKEN=$(echo "$body" | jq -r '.data.accessToken')
  NEW_REFRESH_TOKEN=$(echo "$body" | jq -r '.data.refreshToken')

  echo -e "${GREEN}✅ MFA verified successfully!${NC}"
  echo "New Access Token: ${NEW_ACCESS_TOKEN:0:20}..."
  echo "New Refresh Token: ${NEW_REFRESH_TOKEN:0:20}..."

  ACCESS_TOKEN=$NEW_ACCESS_TOKEN
else
  echo -e "${RED}❌ MFA verification failed (HTTP $http_code)${NC}"
  echo "$body"
  exit 1
fi

echo ""
sleep 1

# ============================================================
# TEST 8: Check MFA Status
# ============================================================

echo -e "${YELLOW}📋 Test 8: Check MFA Status${NC}"
echo "---"

response=$(curl -s -w "\n%{http_code}" -X GET "$API_URL/mfa/status" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $ACCESS_TOKEN" \
  -H "X-Tenant-ID: $TENANT_ID")

http_code=$(echo "$response" | tail -n 1)
body=$(echo "$response" | sed '$d')

if [ "$http_code" -eq 200 ]; then
  echo -e "${GREEN}✅ MFA status retrieved${NC}"
  echo "$body" | jq '.'
else
  echo -e "${RED}❌ Failed to get MFA status (HTTP $http_code)${NC}"
fi

echo ""
sleep 1

# ============================================================
# TEST 9: Test Backup Code (Optional)
# ============================================================

echo -e "${YELLOW}📋 Test 9: Test Backup Code (Optional)${NC}"
echo "---"
read -p "Do you want to test backup code? (y/n): " TEST_BACKUP

if [ "$TEST_BACKUP" = "y" ]; then
  # Logout first
  curl -s -X POST "$API_URL/auth/logout" \
    -H "Authorization: Bearer $ACCESS_TOKEN" \
    -H "X-Tenant-ID: $TENANT_ID" > /dev/null

  # Login to get temp token
  response=$(curl -s -X POST "$API_URL/auth/login" \
    -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
    -H "X-Tenant-ID: $TENANT_ID" \
    -d "{
      \"email\": \"$EMAIL\",
      \"password\": \"$PASSWORD\"
    }")

  TEMP_TOKEN=$(echo "$response" | jq -r '.data.tempToken')

  # Verify with backup code
  response=$(curl -s -w "\n%{http_code}" -X POST "$API_URL/auth/mfa/verify-backup" \
    -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
    -H "X-Tenant-ID: $TENANT_ID" \
    -d "{
      \"tempToken\": \"$TEMP_TOKEN\",
      \"code\": \"$BACKUP_CODE\"
    }")

  http_code=$(echo "$response" | tail -n 1)
  body=$(echo "$response" | sed '$d')

  if [ "$http_code" -eq 200 ]; then
    echo -e "${GREEN}✅ Backup code verified successfully!${NC}"
    echo "$body" | jq '.'
  else
    echo -e "${RED}❌ Backup code verification failed (HTTP $http_code)${NC}"
    echo "$body"
  fi
else
  echo "Skipped backup code test"
fi

echo ""
echo "====================================="
echo -e "${GREEN}🎉 All MFA Tests Completed!${NC}"
echo "====================================="
echo ""
echo "Summary:"
echo "✅ User registration/login"
echo "✅ TOTP setup with QR code"
echo "✅ TOTP verification and enablement"
echo "✅ Login with MFA required"
echo "✅ MFA code verification"
echo "✅ MFA status check"
if [ "$TEST_BACKUP" = "y" ]; then
  echo "✅ Backup code verification"
fi
echo ""
echo -e "${BLUE}Your MFA is now fully functional!${NC}"

FILE: tsconfig.build.json
